tōṭarkaḷiṉ tokai nāḷukku nāḷ kuṟaintu koṇṭu varukiṟatu. itaṟkup palakāraṇaṅkaḷ uṇṭu. nōyiṉāl tuṉpuṟṟu niṟaiyappēr maṭikiṉṟaṉar. ivviṉap peṇkaḷil malaṭikaḷ atikam. peṇ kuḻantai piṟantatum koṉṟuviṭum paḻakkam (Female Infanticide) ivarkaḷiṭam uṇṭu . orupeṇ pala āṇkaḷait tirumaṇam ceytu, koḷḷum paḻakka (Polyandry)mum, pala kātalarkaḷai ēṟṟukkoḷḷum paḻakkamum ivarkaḷiṭam uṇṭu . ivvaḷavu taṭaikaḷai vaittukkoṇṭu, avarkaḷuṭaiya iṉam evvāṟu peruka muṭiyum? nīlakiri malaiyil mottam 1000 pērkaḷē vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷ eṇṇikkaiyil kuṟaintavarkaḷāka iruntālum paḻaṅkuṭi ārāycciyāḷariṉ kavaṉattai ivarkaḷ īrkkum aḷavukku, intiya nāṭṭil uḷḷa entap paḻaṅkuṭiyiṉarum īrppatillai. nīlakiri malaimītu ivarkaḷ koṇṭirunta taṉiyurimai piṟarāl koñcaṅ koñcamākap paṟikkappaṭṭuviṭṭatu. poruḷātārattilum, uḷḷamakiḻcciyilum muṉṉaiviṭac caṟṟuk kuṟaintavar kaḷākavē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. intiya ūḻiyarkaḻaka (Servents of India Society)mum, ceṉṉai aracāṅkamum ivarkaḷ muṉṉēṟṟattiṟkut tuṇaipurikiṉṟaṉa. ivarkaḷuṭaiya kuḻantaikaḷukkuk kalvi vacati aḷikkappaṭukiṟatu. maruttuva utaviyum, poruḷutaviyum ceyyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ippoḻutu cōmpalai nīkki orucilar tōṭṭak kūlikaḷākap paṇi yāṟṟukiṉṟaṉar.

tōṟṟam :

ivarkaḷuṭaiya tōṟṟattaiyum, uṭalamaippukaḷaiyum paṟṟi ārāycciyāḷarkaḷ palatiṟappaṭṭa ceytikaḷaik kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷuṭaiya mūkku rōmāṉiya iṉattāruṭaiyataip pōṉṟatu; mukac cāyal kirēkka iṉattaic cārntatu. eṉavē ārāycciyāḷarkaḷ iv viṉattārai rōmāṉiyarkaḷōṭum, kirēkkarkaḷōṭum toṭarpupaṭuttik kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. nīṇṭa mēlāṭai pōrttukkoṇṭiruppatāl ivarkaḷ īpru (Hebrews) iṉattaic cārntavarkaḷāka irukkalām eṉṟu cilar eṇṇukiṉṟaṉar. camaveḷi makkaḷāl turattappaṭṭu, im malaikaḷil tañcam pukunta citiyar iṉattārē ivarkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟuvōrum uṇṭu. tōṭarkaḷiṉ camayac caṭaṅkukaḷil pāṭappaṭum pāṭalkaḷai ārāynta kirēkka nāṭṭu ārāycciyāḷarāṉa ec. ec, piriṉs pīṭṭar (H. H. Prince Peter) eṉpār, cumēriyarkaḷ vaḻipaṭṭa kaṭavuḷariṉ peyarkaḷum, ak kaṭavuḷarkaḷaip paṟṟiya ceytikaḷum, ivarkaḷuṭaiya kaṭavuḷarkaḷaiyum, ceytikaḷaiyum ottirukkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu kūṟukiṟār. eṉavē tōṭarkaḷ cumēriya iṉattaic cārntavarkaḷ eṉṟu avar eṇṇukiṟār.

tōṭarkula āṇmakaṉiṉ carācari uyaram 5 aṭi 7 aṅkulam. peṇkaḷiṉ uyaram 5 aṭi 1 aṅkulam. camaveḷiyiluḷḷa makkaḷaiviṭa aḻakiya niṟattaip peṟṟirukkiṟārkaḷ. vaṭitteṭutta cilai pōṉṟa uṭaṟkaṭṭuṭaiyavarkaḷ. āṇkaḷ talaiyil aṭarttiyāṉa mayiraip peṟṟirukkiṉṟaṉar. peṇkaḷ taṅkaḷuṭaiya kūntalai veṇṇeyiṭṭu nīvi, uruṇṭaiyāṉa kucciyil curuḷkaḷākac cuṟṟip pakkaṅkaḷil toṅka viṭṭirukkiṟārkaḷ. āṇkaḷum peṇkaḷum mottamāṉa pōrvaiyoṉṟu maṭṭum pōrttukkoṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉar. ap pōrvai veṇmai niṟamāṉatu. ōraṅkaḷil civappum nīlamum kalanta vaṇṇak karaikaḷaiyuṭaiyatu. ip pōrvaiyaip 'puṭkuli' eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. ip pōrvaiyaik kaḻuttiliruntu pātaṅkaḷ varaiyil pōrttuk koṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉar. āṇkaḷ kōvaṇam aṇikiṉṟaṉar.



āṭavarkaḷ uṭaluramum, perumitamāṉa tōṟṟamum, nuṇṇaṟivum, uṟcākamum, calippaṟṟa taṉmaiyum, kūriya pārvaiyum, piṟarai aṭakkiyāḷum nampikkaiyum iyaṟkaiyākavē peṟṟirukkiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷ taṅkaḷ mayirai veṭṭuvatumillai; ciraippatumillai. eppōtum caṭāmuṭiyōṭu kāṭciyaḷippārkaḷ. peṇkaḷ aḻakiya tōṟṟamuṭaiyavarkaḷ. āṉāl aṟivil kuṟaintavarkaḷ. oḻukkattaip paṟṟik kavalaippaṭātavarkaḷ, paccai kuttikkoḷḷum paḻakkam ivarkaḷiṭam uṇṭu.

vīṭukaḷ :

tōṭarkaḷ vāḻum ciṟṟūrukku 'maṇṭu' eṉṟu peyar. ovvoru maṇṭuvilum nāṉku allatu aintu vīṭukaḷē uḷḷaṉa. iv vīṭukaḷ arai vaṭṭa vaṭi vamāṉavai; pīppāyai nīḷa vākkil kuṟukkāka aṟuttuk kavittu vaittāṟpōl tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa. marap palakaikaḷālum, caṭṭaṅkaḷālum iv vīṭṭai amaittu, mēlē puṟkaḷaiyum, taḻaikaḷaiyum pōṭṭuk kūrai vēykiṟārkaḷ. vīṭṭiṟku muṉṉāl maṭṭum orē oru vaḻiyuṇṭu. atu kuṟukalāṉatu ; iraṇṭaṭi catura aḷavuḷḷatu. vīṭṭiṟkuḷ celvōr kuḻan taikaḷaip pōl nāṉku kālil tavaḻntukoṇṭē cella vēṇṭum. vīṭṭiṟkuḷ paṭuttuṟaṅkuvataṟkeṉṟu mēṭaikaḷ amaikkappaṭṭirukkum. kāṟṟum katiravaṉ oḷiyum uḷḷē maruntukkukkūṭak kiṭaiyā. vīṭṭaic cuṟṟi nāṟpuṟac cuvar amaintirukkum. erumaikaḷ uḷḷē nuḻaivataṟkeṉṟu taṉi vaḻiyoṉṟu accuvaril irukkum.

moḻi :

tōṭarkaḷ pēcum moḻi, aṅkuḷḷa maṟṟa paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷiṉ moḻikaḷiṉiṉṟum māṟupaṭṭirukkiṟatu. avarkaḷuṭaiya moḻi 'tutam' eṉṟa peyarāl aḻaikkappaṭukiṟatu. tirāviṭa moḻiyārāycci vallunarāṉa kālṭuvel turaimakaṉār, tiruntāta tirāviṭa moḻikaḷil itaiyum cērttirukkiṟār. maṟṟa tirāviṭa moḻikaḷaiviṭat tutam tamiḻōṭu neruṅkiya toṭarpu koṇṭiruppatāka moḻiyārāycci vallunarkaḷ kūṟukiṟārkaḷ. im muṭiviliruntu ōruṇmai maṭṭum pulaṉākiṟatu. atāvatu tōṭarkaḷ tirāviṭa iṉattaic cārntavarkaḷ eṉpatē. tōṭarkaḷil tārttar (Tarthar), tīvali (Teivali) eṉṟa iru piriviṉar uṇṭu . ivarkaḷ vevvēṟu kālaṅkaḷil iṅkuk kuṭiyēṟiyavarkaḷ.

aṉṟāṭa vēlai :

tōṭarkaḷ payirt toḻil ceyvatillai. kālaiyil eḻuntatum, kaṭṭai viralkaḷai mūkkiṟketiril vaittu, maṟṟa viralkaḷai akala virittup pariti vaṇakkam ceyvārkaḷ. piṟaku erumait toḻuvattiṟkuc ceṉṟu pāl kaṟappārkaḷ. mutal nāḷ piraiyiṭṭu uṟainta tayiraik kaṭaivārkaḷ. uṇavu uṇṭapiṉ erumaikaḷai ōṭṭikkoṇṭu celvārkaḷ. nāḷ muḻuvatum erumai mēyttukkoṇṭu cōmpalākap poḻutu pōkkuvar. mālai nērattil viḷakku ēṟṟi atai vaṇaṅkuvar. erumaikaḷait toḻuvattil aṭaitta piṉ uṇṭu uṟaṅkuvar. pāl kaṟattal, erumaikaḷaik kaṇkāṇittal mutaliya paṇikaḷai āṇkaḷ mēṟkoḷvar. vīṭṭu vēlaikaḷaip peṇkaḷ ceyvar.

camaya vāḻvu :

katirōṉ vaṇakkattiliruntu, ivarkaḷ iyaṟkaiyaik kaṭavuḷākak koḷḷum vaḻakkamuṭaiyavarkaḷ eṉpatu pulaṉākiṟatu. atōṭu avarkaḷukkē urittāṉa vēṟupala kaṭavuḷkaḷaiyum vaṇaṅkukiṉṟaṉar. tōṭarkaḷiṉ vaḻipāṭṭiṟkuriya mukkiyamāṉa kaṭavuḷ āṉ (On) ākum. āṉiṉ tantai piti (Pithi). āṉukku oru cōtariyum uṇṭu. avaḷ peyar tīkirci (Teikirzi). piti mikavum paḻamaiyāṉa kaṭavuḷ. pititāṉ tōṭarkaḷaiyum, avarkaḷuṭaiya celvamāṉa erumaikaḷaiyum paṭaittār eṉṟu avarkaḷ karutukiṟārkaḷ. āṉāl piti ippōtu ivvulakil illaiyām, iṟantōr vāḻum ulakiṉ talaivarāka irukkiṟārām. tōṭar iṉattiṉ mutal tōṟṟattaippaṟṟi avarkaḷiṭaiyē oru katai vaḻaṅkukiṟatu.

pitiyum avar maṉaiviyum, oru nāḷ kuntā malaiyiṉ uccikkuc ceṉṟārkaḷām. oru nīṇṭa irumpuc caṭṭattai avvucciyiṉ mēl kuṟukkāka vaittu, accaṭṭattiṉ oru nuṉiyil pitiyum, maṟṟoru nuṉiyil avar maṉaiviyum niṉṟu koṇṭārkaḷām. piti annuṉiyiliruntavaṇṇam pūmiyiliruntu 1800 erumaikaḷai mēlē koṇarntārām. pitiyiṉ maṉaivi, maṟṟoru nuṉiyiliruntukoṇṭu 1600 erumaikaḷaik koṇarntārām. piti koṇṭuvanta erumaikaḷē, ippoḻutu tōṭarkaḷiṭaiyē uḷḷa puṉita erumaikaḷiṉ (Sacredbuffaloes) muṉṉōrkaḷ. pūmiyiliruntu pitiyiṉāl koṇṭuvarappaṭṭa kaṭaicip puṉita erumaiyiṉ vālaip piṭittukkoṇṭu tōṭarkaḷiṉ muṉṉōṉ vantāṉ. avaṉ taṉ mārpu elumpukaḷil oṉṟai eṭuttu, mutal tōṭarkulap peṇṇai uruvākkiṉāṉ. orunāḷ pitiyiṉ makaṉāṉa āṉ taṇṇīril mūḻki iṟantuviṭṭāṉām. makaṉiṉ pirivait tāḷamuṭiyāta piti, tāmum iṟantōr vāḻum ulakiṟkuc ceṉṟu, avvulakiṉ talaimaip poṟuppai ēṟṟukkoṇṭu, aṅkēyē irukkiṟārām. pitikkup piṟaku avar makaḷāṉa tīkirci tōṭaraik kākkum paṇiyai mēṟkoṇṭu, avarkaḷiṉ matippiṟkuriya kaṭavuḷāka vīṟṟirukkiṟāḷ. uṇmaiyilēyē ikkataiyum, ik kataiyil varum kaṭavuḷar peyarkaḷum kirēkka, cumēriya pāṇiyilēyē amaintuḷḷaṉa. ik kataiyiliruntum, ivarkaḷuṭaiya paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷiliruntum, ivarkaḷ taṅkaḷukkeṉa oru taṉic camayattaiyum, kaṭavuḷarkaḷaiyum koṇṭirukkiṟārkaḷ eṉpatu teḷivākiṟatu.

cila camayaṅkaḷil tōṭarkaḷ intu camayak kaṭavuḷarkaḷaiyum vaḻipaṭukiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷ vaṇaṅkum intu camayak kaṭavuḷkaḷil kuṟippiṭattakkatu 'nañcaṉ' eṉpatākum. piḷḷaippēṟṟukkāka maṭṭum, ikkaṭavuḷai vaṇaṅkukiṉṟaṉar. puṉita mantaiyiṉ kuru, mantiravāti, maruttuvaṉ ākiya ovvoruvarukkum taṉittaṉik kaṭavuḷuṇṭu. kurumārkaḷiṉ caṭaṅkukaḷil kūṟappaṭum mantiram malaiyāḷa moḻiyilēyē amaintuḷḷatu.

kōyilum puṉita mantaiyum :

tōṭarkaḷiṉ kōyilkaḷ avarkaḷuṭaiya kuṭicaikaḷaip pōlavē kāṭciyaḷikkiṉṟaṉa. avarkaḷuṭaiya kuṭicaik kūṭṭattiliruntu, ciṟitu tūrattil ik kōyilkaḷ amaintirukkiṉṟaṉa. peṇkaḷ kōyilukkuḷ aṉumatikkappaṭuvatillai. taṅkaḷaip pōlavē kaṭavuḷum erumaikaḷai mēyttukkoṇṭu malaiyucciyil vāḻvatākat tōṭarkaḷ eṇṇukiṟārkaḷ. āṉāl maṉitarkaḷ kaṇṇil teṉpaṭuvatillaiyām. taṅkaḷ mantaiyiliruntu, kōyilukkuc cila erumaikaḷai viṭṭuviṭukiṟārkaḷ. avvāṟu viṭum erumaikaḷaip 'puṉita erumaikaḷ' eṉṟu avarkaḷ kūṟukiṟārkaḷ, avaikaḷ kaṭavuḷukkuc conta māṉavai eṉṟu karutukiṟārkaḷ. ippuṉita erumaikaḷaik kaṇkāṇikka oru kuru amarttappaṭukiṟāṉ. avaṉai ellārum pālōl (Palol) eṉṟu aḻaik kiṉṟaṉar. pālōl tāṉ puṉita erumaikaḷukkum tōṭar kaḷukkum iṭaiyiliruntu paṇipuripavaṉāka. {Intermediary) viḷaṅkukiṟāṉ. avaṉukkut tuṇaiyāka oru paṇiyāḷaṉ uṇṭu. avaṉaik 'kalṭamok' (Kaltmokh) eṉṟu aḻaikkiṉṟaṉar.

tōṭariṉ kōyil iru pakutikaḷākap pirikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. muṟpakuti pālōliṉ iruppiṭamākap payaṉpaṭukiṟatu. uṭpakuti kaṭavuḷiṉ iruppiṭam. kōyililuḷḷa erumaippaṇṇaikku 'ṭi' (Ti) eṉṟu peyar. ittakaiya paṇṇai cīkūr (Sigur) cikarattiṟkarukil uḷḷatu. utakamaṇṭalattiluḷḷa airōppiyarkaḷ itait tōṭar kōyil (Toda Cathedral) eṉṟē aḻaikkiṉṟaṉar. puṉita erumaikaḷiliruntu kiṭaikkum varuvāy kōyil vaḻipāṭṭiṟkup payaṉpaṭuttappaṭukiṟatu. kōyiliṉ muṟpakutiyil pālōl uṟaṅkuvatu vaḻakkam. intuk kōyilkaḷil karuvaṟai (mūlattāṉam) evvaḷavu puṉitamākak karutappaṭukiṟatō, atu pōla itaṉ uṭpakuti tōṭarkaḷāl karutappaṭukiṟatu. ataṉuḷ pālōlait tavira vēṟu yārum nuḻaiyak kūṭātu. puṉitap paṇṇaiyil paṇipuripavarkaḷ kūṭa uḷḷē aṉumatikkappaṭuvatillai. puṉita erumaikaḷiṉ pālaik kaṟattal mutaliya paṇikaḷaikkūṭap pālōl tāṉ. nēraṭiyākac ceyvatu vaḻakkam, kōyilukkuḷ irukkum pōtu pālōl orē oru laṅkōṭutāṉ aṇivatu vaḻakkam. pālōlaṇiyum pōrvai paḻuppu allatu karuppu niṟam uṭaiyatāka irukkum. kōyil vaḻipāṭṭiṟkum, puṉita erumaikaḷiṉ upayōkattiṟkum oru nīrōṭai payaṉpaṭuttappaṭum. ataṉ nīrai vēṟu yārum payaṉ paṭuttakkūṭātu. pālōl etirpārāvitamāka, vēṟu ētēṉum kuṭicaiyil uṟaṅka nēriṭṭāl, taraiyaiyum, uṟaṅkum mēṭaiyaiyum tavira, ak kuṭicaiyiluḷḷa vēṟu epporuḷaiyum toṭuvatu kiṭaiyātu. periya kōyilil uḷḷa pālōl mikavum puṉitamāṉavaṉākak karutappaṭukiṟāṉ. cantaikkuc celluvatum, peṇṇōṭu uṭaltoṭarpu koḷvatum, piṟarōṭu paḻakuvatum avaṉāl vilakkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. tiṅkaḷ, viyāḻaṉ ākiya iraṇṭu nāṭkaḷil maṭṭum piṟar avaṉai neruṅka aṉumatikkappaṭukiṟārkaḷ. āṉāl ikkaṭṭuppāṭukaḷellām ippoḻutu taḷarntu varukiṉṟaṉa. oru kuru puṉita mantaikaḷiṉ poṟuppai ēṟṟukkoḷvataṟku muṉpākac cila caṭaṅkukaḷ naṭattappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. kōyilukkeṉṟu otukkappaṭṭa puṉita aruviyil kuḷittu, ataṉ nīrai uṭkoḷvatu accaṭaṅkukaḷil kuṟippiṭattakka oṉṟākum.

uṇavu :

tōṭarkaḷ potuvāka marakkaṟi uṇavē uṇkiṉṟaṉar. pālum tayirum avarkaḷuṭaiya iṉṟiyamaiyāta uṇavup poruḷkaḷ. caṭaṅkukaḷil erumaik kaṉṟukaḷaip paliyiṭumpōtu maṭṭum avaṟṟiṉ ūṉai uṇkiṉṟaṉar. vēṭṭaiyil māṉ kiṭaittāl, ataiyum viruppattōṭu uṇkiṉṟaṉar. matu aruntuvatil, ivarkaḷ peru viruppam uṭaiyavarkaḷ.

paliyiṭutal :

erumaik kaṉṟukaḷaip paliyiṭutal ivarkaḷiṭam peru vaḻakkākap paraviyiruntatu. kaṭavuḷait tiruptip paṭuttuvataṟkāka āṇṭutōṟum ovvoru maṇṭuvilum ōr erumaik kaṉṟum, ovvoru 'ṭi'yilum iraṇṭu kaṉṟukaḷum paliyiṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. palavita nuṇukkamāṉa caṭaṅkukaḷ appōtu naṭaipeṟum. caṭaṅkukaḷellām muṭintapiṟaku paliyiṭappaṭṭa kaṉṟiṉ ūṉai ellārum uṇpar. pāvaṅkaḷaip pōkkikkoḷvataṟkum, tuyaraṅkaḷait tīrttukkoḷvataṟkumkūṭa, ivarkaḷ paliyiṭum vaḻakkattai mēṟkoṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉar. tēṉum, paḻaṅkaḷum malaic carivil perukavēṇṭum eṉpataṟkāka āṇṭutōṟum akṭōpar tiṅkaḷil pālōlum avaṉ tuṇaivaṉum neruppu mūṭṭi vēḷvi ceyvatu vaḻakkam. araciyalāriṉ talaiyīṭṭāl, paliyiṭum paḻakkam kuṟaintu varukiṉṟatu.

pilli cūṉiyam :

pilli cūṉiyam ellāt tōṭarkaḷukkum ōraḷavu teriyum. āṉāl oru cilarukku naṉṟākat teriyum. mēṉmēlum terintu koḷḷavēṇṭum eṉṟa ācai ellārukkum uṇṭu. ikkalai paramparaiyākap payilappaṭṭu varukiṟatu. piṟa iṉattār itaṉāl tāṉ tōṭarkaḷaik kaṇṭu añcukiṟārkaḷ. ivvaccattiṉālēyē āṇṭutōṟum naṉkoṭaiyāka ivarkaḷukkut tāṉiyam vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉar. maṉita mayiril aintu kallaikkaṭṭi, mantira uccāṭaṉam ceytu, avaṟṟait tuṇiyil kaṭṭi, etiriyiṉ vīṭṭuk kūraiyil ceruki viṭuvatu vaḻakkam. allatu avaṉ vāḻum maṇṭuviṟkarukiluḷḷa ṣōlāvil putaittu viṭuvatu vaḻakkam.

kuḻantaik kolai :

tōṭarkaḷiṉ tokai nāḷukku nāḷ arukik koṇṭu varuvataṟkup peṇ kuḻantaikkolai mukkiyakkāraṇam ākum eṉṟu muṉpē kuṟippiṭṭēṉ. peṇ kuḻantaikaḷiṉ mītu avarkaḷ koṇṭa veṟuppiṟkuriya uṇmaiyāṉa kāraṇam viḷaṅkavillai. peṇkuḻantai piṟantatum, erumait toḻuvattiṉ vāyilil ataik kiṭattiviṭuvārkaḷ. malarntu maṇam vīcavēṇṭiya avviḷamokku, erumaikaḷiṉ kālil mitipaṭṭu maṭikiṟatu. ki. pi. 1820- il utakamaṇṭalattil vāḻnta callivaṉ eṉṟa airōppiyar ippaḻakkattai kaiviṭumāṟu tōṭarkaḷukku aṟivuṟuttiṉār. ki. pi. 1856-ām āṇṭil kōvai māvaṭṭattiṉ taṇṭalarāka iruntavar oru putiya caṭṭam iyaṟṟiṉār. ataṉpaṭi tōṭar vīṭṭil piṟanta peṇ kuḻantaiyaip peṟṟōr koṇṭuvantu āṇṭutōṟum nēril kāṭṭiṉāl, ataṉ vaḷarccikkākak kuṟippiṭṭa oru tokaiyaik koṭuppatākak kūṟiṉār. ippoḻutukūṭa ic caṭṭam amulil uḷḷatu. itaṉāl peṇ kuḻantaik kolaic ceyal ōraḷavu kuṟaintateṉalām. āṉāl muḻutum nīṅkavillai. tōṭarkaḷiṉ icceyalāl peṇkaḷ tokai āṇkaḷiṉ tokaiyaiviṭa ep poḻutum kuṟaintē kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.

utakamaṇṭalattiṟ karukil vāḻum tōṭarkula makaḷir kātaṟ ceyalkaḷil kaitērntavarkaḷ. nakaril vāḻum maṟṟa iṉa āṇkaḷōṭu koṇṭa toṭarpāl, ōraḷavu avarkaḷ makkaḷ tokai atikarittatu eṉalām. āṉāl appaṭip piṟakkum kuḻantaikaḷ tōṭarkaḷiṉ uṭaṟpaṇpukaḷaiyē peṟṟirukkiṉṟaṉa. vēṟṟu iṉap paṇpukaḷaip peṟṟup piṟakkum kuḻantaikaḷ mikavum kuṟaivu.

pala kaṇavar muṟaiyum potu maṉaivi muṟaiyum :

tōṭarkaḷiṉ kātal vāḻkkaiyum, illaṟamum cuvaiyāṉavai, kaṟpaippaṟṟi avarkaḷ atikamākak kavalaippaṭuvatillai. kalācāra varalāṟṟiṉpaṭi, kaṟpu muṟaiyāṉatu, kālattiṟkuk kālam nāṭṭukku nāṭu māṟupaṭṭukkoṇṭu vantirukkiṟatu. oru nāṭṭuk kaṟpumuṟai, maṟṟoru nāṭṭiṉarukku putumaiyākat tōṉṟum. paṇpaṭāta makkaḷiṉattilum kaṟpaik kāṇa muṭiyātu; ellōrum kalvi kaṟṟu aṟivu ārāycciyil mikuntirukkum iṉattāriṭattilum kaṟpaik kāṇa muṭiyātu. muṉṉavar aṟiyāmaiyiṉālum, piṉṉavar alaṭciyattālum kaṟpaik kaikaḻuvi viṭukiṉṟaṉar. kīḻai nāṭṭiṉariṭaiyē kaṟpu ōraḷavu kaṭṭuppāṭṭiṟkuṭpaṭṭirukkiṟatu. nākarikattil (vaḷḷuvar kūṟum nākarikamalla) mēmpaṭṭavarkaḷāka viḷaṅkum mēṉāṭṭiṉariṭaiyē, kaṟpu atika matippup peṟuvatillai. pireñcu nāṭṭil kaṟpu mikavum iḻinta nilaiyil uḷḷatu. amerikka nāṭṭil 10 nimiṭattiṟkoru untuvaṇṭit tiruṭṭum, 40 nimiṭattiṟkoru kaṟpaḻippum naṭai, peṟuvatākak kaṇakkiṭṭirukkiṟārkaḷ. iṅkilāntu nāṭṭil vipacāram atikamillai, ruciya nāṭṭil, vipacārattaic caṭṭapūrvamākat taṭuttuk kaṭuntaṇṭaṉaiyaḷippatāl, kaṟpu aṅkuk kākkappaṭukiṟatu. paṭiyāta makkaḷ pāvattiṟkañci, iḻi ceyal purivatillai. vaṟumai tākkumpōtu maṭṭum, vēṟu vaḻiyiṉṟik kuṟṟam ceykiṉṟaṉar. āṉāl mikavum paṭitta makkaḷukkup pāvattil nampikkai kiṭaiyātu. kaṟpai iḻappataik kuṟṟamākavum avarkaḷ karutuvatillai, etirkālattil taṉi maṉaiviyurimai eṉṟa nilaimāṟi, atilum potuvuṭaimaik koḷkai ēṟpaṭalām eṉpatu ulakap pēraṟiñarāṉa irassaliṉ karuttākum.

tōṭarkaḷ kaṟpaip paṟṟik kavalaippaṭāmaliruppataṟku iraṇṭu kāraṇaṅkaḷuṇṭu. mutalāvatu kāraṇam aṟiyāmai. ovvoru tōṭarkula āṇukkum, ovvoru peṇ kiṭaikkāmaliruppatu iraṇṭāvatu kāraṇam. peṇkaḷiṉ paṟṟāk kuṟaiyiṉāl, ovvoru peṇṇum pala āṭavaraik kaṇavarāka ēṟṟukkoḷḷum vaḻakkattai -(Polyandry}k koṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷōṭu amaiyāmal, vēṟucila āṭavaraik kātalarkaḷākavum oru peṇ ēṟṟuk koḷvatuṇṭu. kaṇavarkaḷum ip paḻakkattai aṉumatikkiṉṟaṉar. ākaiyiṉāl avarkaḷ aṅkīkarikkappaṭṭa kātalarkaḷ (recognised lovers) eṉṟu aḻaikkappaṭukiṟārkaḷ.

pala āṭavarkaḷ cērntu pala maṉaiviyaraip potuvākak koḷḷum paḻakkamum ivarkaḷiṭam uṇṭu. oru kuṭumpattil pala cakōtararkaḷiruntāl, avarkaḷuṭaiya maṉaiviyar, ellārukkum urimaiyuṭaiyavarkaḷ. ippaḻakkam pala maṉaivi muṟaiyōṭu (Polygamy) ōraḷavu toṭarpuṭaiyatākak kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. āṉāl ippoḻutu oru kuṭumpattiluḷḷa ovvoru cakōtaraṉum taṉakkeṉa oruttiyai maṉaiviyāka urimaiyākkik koḷkiṟāṉ. āṉāl kaṭṭuppāṭiṉṟit taṉ uṭaṉpiṟantāriṉ maṉaiviyariṭam toṭarpukoḷḷa avaṉukku urimaiyuṇṭu. ic ceyaliṉāl varampiṟkuṭpaṭṭa maṉaivi muṟai (Monogamy) ōraḷavu talaikāṭṭukiṟatu.

oru peṇṇukkuriya kaṇavarkaḷilō allatu kātalarkaḷilō, yārēṉum oruvaṉ, appeṇ irukkum kuṭicaikkuḷ nuḻaiyumpōtu, oru pūvai vāyilil cerukiviṭṭuc celvatu vaḻakkam. appūvaik kāṇum maṟṟa kaṇavaṉmārō, allatu kātalarkaḷō appōtu kuṭicaikkuḷ nuḻaiyak kūṭātu. mēṟ kūṟiya paḻakkavaḻakkaṅkaḷellām, camaveḷiyiluḷḷa makkaḷ toṭarpāl ippōtu kuṟaintuvarukiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl muṟṟilum maṟaiyavillai,

tirumaṇam :

tārttār, tīvali eṉṟa irupiriviṉar tōṭarkaḷiṭaiyē uṇṭu eṉak kaṇṭōm. avarkaḷiṭaiyē maṇat toṭarpu kiṭaiyātu. āṉāl ōriṉap peṇ maṟṟōriṉattāṉaik kātalaṉāka ēṟṟukkoḷḷalām. oru peṇ kuḻantai piṟantatum, mūṉṟu allatu nāṉkāvatu vayatil, atait tāymāmaṉ makaṉukkō, attai makaṉukkō , tirumaṇam ceytu koṭuppatu vaḻakkam. māppiḷḷai vīṭṭār peṇ vīṭṭārukku aṭikkaṭi paricukaḷ vaḻaṅkuvataṉ mūlam tirumaṇattai uṟuti ceytu koḷkiṉṟaṉar, peṇ 15 allatu 16 vayatu varaiyil peṟṟōr vīṭṭilēyē iruppatu vaḻakkam. oru peṇ paruvam aṭaiyum nilaiyil irukkumpōtu, vēṟṟukkulattāṉ oruvaṉai aḻaittu appeṇṇōṭu ōr iravu muḻuvatum taṉi aṟaiyil irukkumāṟu ceyvar. itu 'paruvap peṇṇiṉ kaṟpaḻippuc caṭaṅku' {ceremonial defloration of nubile girls) eṉappaṭum. iccaṭaṅku mikavum iṉṟiyamaiyātatākak karutappaṭṭatu. iccaṭaṅku naṭaipeṟāmal orupeṇ paruvameytuvatu, avamāṉamākak karutappaṭṭatu. āṉāl ippaḻakkam ippoḻutu kaiviṭappaṭṭatu.

maṇa vilakku :

tōṭarkaḷ kaṭaippiṭit toḻukum maṉaivi muṟaikaḷāl maṇamuṟivum, maṟumaṇamum aṭikkaṭi ēṟpaṭum eṉpatil aiyamillai. maṇavilakkup peṟa virumpuvōr cila erumaikaḷai oṟuppukkaṭṭaṇamākac (Fine) celuttiviṭṭu eḷitil peṟalām. ākaiyiṉāl vēṇṭumeṉṟē cila āṭavarkaḷ, āṭai māṟṟuvatu pōl maṉaiviyarai māṟṟik koṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉar, oru peṇ taṉ vāḻkkaiyil aintu muṟaikūṭa maṇa vilakkup peṟuvatuṇṭu.

piḷḷaippēṟu :

tōṭar kulappeṇ karuvuṟṟa aintāvatu tiṅkaḷil, pala caṭaṅkukaḷ nikaḻttappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avarkaḷ vāḻum ‘maṇṭu'viliruntu ciṟitu tolaivil oru kuṭicai amaittu, atil appeṇṇaik kuṭiyēṟṟukiṉṟaṉar. oru kayiṟṟiṉ nuṉiyil tīyaip paṟṟavaittu, attīyiṉāl taṉ iṭuppiṉ irupuṟaṅkaḷaiyum appeṇṇaiyē cuṭṭuk koḷḷac colkiṉṟaṉar. oru tiṅkaḷ akkuṭicaiyil iruntapiṟaku, pala caṭaṅkukaḷōṭu appeṇ vīṭṭiṟkaḻaikkappaṭukiṟāḷ.

ēḻāvatu tiṅkaḷil maṟupaṭiyum oru caṭaṅku naṭaipeṟukiṟatu. iccaṭaṅku mikavum mukkiyamāṉatu. itaṟku 'villampuc caṭaṅku' eṉṟu peyar. karuvuṟṟa peṇṇum, vayiṟṟil uḷḷa kuḻantaikaḷukkuriya tantai eṉṟu avaḷāl kuṟippiṭappaṭṭa kaṇavaṉum, maṇṭuviṟkarukiluḷḷa ṣōlāviṟkuc celluvārkaḷ. kaṇavaṉ marattiliruntu kucciyai oṭittu mukkōṇamāka vaippāṉ. ataṉ naṭuvil oru viḷakku ēṟṟappaṭum. piṟaku kaṇavaṉum maṉaiviyum vēṟoru kucciyai oṭittu vil ceyvārkaḷ. oru pulliṉāl ampu ceyvārkaḷ. piṟaku tam uṟaviṉarai vaṇaṅkuvārkaḷ, villaik kaiyilēntiya vaṇṇam appeṇ eriyum viḷakkai orumaṇi nēramō, aṇaiyum varaiyilō pārttukkoṇṭirukka vēṇṭum.

piṟaku ammaṇṭuviluḷḷa ellārum, avviṭattilēyē uṇavu camaittu uṇṭuviṭṭu, avviravai avarkaḷōṭu kaḻippar. oru peṇ mutal taṭavaiyākak karuvuṟum pōtō, putiya oru kaṇavaṉai ak kuḻantaiyiṉ tantaiyākat tērnteṭukkum pōtō iccaṭaṅku naṭaipeṟum. tirumaṇamillāmalō, kuḻantaiyillāmalō orupeṇ iṟantāl, cuṭukāṭṭil tirumaṇac caṭaṅkum, villampuc caṭaṅkum naṭai peṟum. kuḻantai piṟantavuṭaṉ appeṇ maṟupaṭiyum maṇṭuviṟku veḷiyiluḷḷa akkuṭicaikku aṉuppappaṭukiṟāḷ. akkuṭicaiyil mūṉṟu vāraṅkaḷō oru tiṅkaḷō avaḷ irukka vēṇṭum. kuḻantaiyiṉ mukattai mūṉṟu tiṅkaḷkaḷ muṭiyum varai yārum pārkkak kūṭātu. ākaiyiṉāl oru tuṇi koṇṭu ataṉ mukattai mūṭiyē vaippārkaḷ. kuḻantaikku muṭi yeṭukkumpōtu peyariṭuvārkaḷ.

cottu muṟai :

oru peṇṇukkup piṟakkum kuḻantai ēḻāvatu mātac caṭaṅkiṉpōtu kuṟippiṭappaṭṭa kaṇavaṉukkē urimaiyāṉatu. avaṉuṭaiya cottellām ak kuḻantaiyaiyē cārum. vēṟoruvar kuḻantaiyait tatteṭukkum vaḻakkam ivarkaḷiṭam illai. peṇkaḷukkuc cotturimai kiṭaiyātu. iccotturimai naṭavaṭikkaikaḷaik kaṇkāṇippataṟku amarttappaṭṭavarai mōṇikar (Monigar) eṉṟu aḻaikkiṉṟaṉar. āṉāl tōṭariṭaiyē irukkum pañcāyattuc capai, mōṇikaraiviṭac celvākku mikuntatāka uḷḷatu. appañcāyattiṉ tīrppaiyē ellārum ēṟṟuk koḷkiṉṟaṉar.

iṟutic caṭaṅku :

tōṭarkaḷiṉ iṟutic caṭaṅku mūṉṟu pirivukaḷākap pirikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. paccai iṟutic caṭaṅku (Green Funeral), kāynta iṟutic caṭaṅku (Dry Funeral) kaṭaici iṟuticcaṭaṅku (Last Funeral) eṉpavaiyē appirivukaḷ. mutaṉ mutalāka iṟantupōṉavaṉai āṭai aṇikaḷāl alaṅkarittu, oru kuṭicaiyil koṇṭupōy vaippārkaḷ. peṇkaḷ iṇai iṇaiyāka oruvar neṟṟiyōṭu maṟṟoruvar muṭṭik koṇṭu kūccaliṭṭu aḻuvārkaḷ. appoḻutu puṉita erumaikaḷil iraṇṭaip paliyiṭa vēṇṭum. ataṟkākap puṉita erumaikaḷai ṣōlāvil ōṭṭi accuṟuttuvārkaḷ. avai maruṇṭu ciṉaṅkoṇṭu ōṭum. appoḻutu palapēr cērntu avaṟṟait turattip piṭippārkaḷ. ammuyaṟciyil pala pērukkuk kaṭumaiyāṉa kāyam ēṟpaṭuvatuṇṭu. kaṭaiciyāka iraṇṭu erumaikaḷaip piṭittup piṇattiṟkarukil koṇṭuvaruvārkaḷ. avaṟṟiṉ kompukaḷil neyyaip pūcuvārkaḷ. piṟaku kōṭariyiṉ piṉpuṟattāl talaiyil tākki, erumaikaḷaik kolluvārkaḷ. iṟantavar āṇāka iruntāl, paliyiṭappaṭṭa erumaikaḷaip piṇattiṉ talaiyarukil, valatukaip puṟamākak kiṭattuvar. peṇṇāka iruntāl, piṇattiṉ kālkaḷai erumaikaḷiṉ talaimītu tūkki vaippar. aṅkirukkum āṭavarkaḷellārum iṟantukiṭakkum erumaikaḷiṉ kompukaḷait toṭṭu, avaikaṭku vaṇakkam celuttuvar. piṟaku piṇam pāṭaiyilēṟṟappaṭṭu iṭukāṭṭukku eṭuttuc cellappaṭum. piṇattōṭu uṇavu, āṭai, aṇi, paṇam, pukaiyilai mutaliyaṉavum aṭutta ulakil payaṉpaṭuttuvataṟkāka vaittaṉuppappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ataṉ piṟaku piṇattaic citaiyil vaittut tīyiṭuvārkaḷ. vilaiyuyarnta poruḷkaḷaiyum, piṇattiṉ talaiyiliruntu ciṟitu mayiraiyum eṭuttukkoḷvārkaḷ, piṇam erikkappaṭṭa piṟaku, citaṟiya ataṉ maṇṭaiyōṭṭiṉ oru pakutiyaiyum eṭuttukkoḷvārkaḷ. ittuṭaṉ paccai iṟutic caṭaṅku muṭivuṟukiṟatu.

mēṟkūṟiya mūṭṭaiyai oru kuṭicaiyil koṇṭupōy vaippārkaḷ. mutalil evvāṟu peṇkaḷ aḻutārkaḷō, atē pōl iṉṟum aḻuvārkaḷ. erumaik kaṉṟukaḷ paliyiṭappaṭum. palakaṟaikaḷāl alaṅkarikkappaṭṭa oru taṭiyai uyarttip piṭittukkoṇṭu, ak kuṭicaiyaic cuṟṟi oruvaṉ āṭi varuvāṉ. kaṭaiciyāka ellārum uṇavu uṇṭu, tattam vīṭukaḷukkup pirintu celvar. itaṟkuk kāynta iṟutic caṭaṅku eṉṟu peyar.



maṟunāḷ kālaiyil kaṭaici iṟutic caṭaṅku toṭaṅkum, kuṭicaiyiliruntu am mūṭṭaiyai eṭuttukkoṇṭu ceṉṟu vaṭṭamāka amaikkappaṭṭa kaṟkaḷukku naṭuvil oru kuḻi tōṇṭi, atil vaippārkaḷ. avviṭattiṟku ' 'ācāram' (Azaram) eṉṟu peyar. am mūṭṭaiyaiyum, uṇavup poruḷōṭu kūṭiya marat taṭṭaiyum vaittu oṉṟāka erippārkaḷ. piṟaku ac cāmpalaik kuḻikkuḷḷēyē vaittu mūṭiviṭuvārkaḷ. piṟaku mūṉṟu muṟai maṇiyaṭikkappaṭum. putiya maṭpāṇṭamoṉṟu uṭaikkappaṭum. ittuṭaṉ cāccaṭaṅkukaḷ muṭivuṟukiṉṟaṉa. cilar ic caṭaṅkukaḷaic cila tiṅkaḷkaḷ kaḻittukkūṭac ceyvatuṇṭu.

tōṭarkaḷ yār?

intiya nāṭṭilēyē mikavum kavarcciyūṭṭum paḻaṅkuṭiyiṉarākiya it tōṭarkaḷiṉ paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷaikkoṇṭu, ivarkaḷ yārāyirukkalām? eṅkiruntu vantirukkalām ? eṉṟa viṉākkaḷukku viṭai kāṇa muyalukiṉṟaṉar. tiruvāḷar rivers (Mr. Rivers) eṉpār, kēraḷattu malaiyāḷa makkaḷiṉ paṇpāṭṭōṭu, ivarkaḷ paṇpāṭu neruṅkiya toṭarpu koṇṭiruppatai viḷakkap pala cāṉṟukaḷ kāṭṭukiṉṟār. oru peṇ pala kaṇavarai ēṟṟukkoḷḷum muṟai kaṭanta aimpatu āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ varai malaiyāḷattil nilavi vantatu. am maṇa muṟai, 'campanta maṇa muṟai' eṉṟu malaiyāḷa makkaḷāl kūṟappaṭṭu vantatu. tirumaṇattiṉpōtu naṭakkum. kūṟaic caṭaṅku, iru iṉattāriṭaiyilum mukkiya māṉatākak karutappaṭukiṟatu. tirumaṇamākāmal oru peṇ iṟantuviṭṭāl, putuppuṭavai vaittu erittalum, maṇattiṟkuriya caṭaṅkukaḷaip piṇattiṟkuc ceytalum. iru iṉattāriṭaiyilēyum nilavivarum paḻakkaṅkaḷākum. palakaṟaikaḷāl oru kaḻiyai alaṅkarittu, piṇam vaikkappaṭṭa kuṭicaiyaic cuṟṟi āṭum paḻakkam malaiyāḷa makkaḷukkē uriyatu. tōṭarkaḷum ap paḻakkattai mēṟkoṇṭu oḻukukiṉṟaṉar. iṟantupōṉavaṉuṭaiya uyir mēṟku nōkkic (malaiyāḷam irukkum ticai nōkki) celluvatākat tōṭarkaḷ karutukiṉṟaṉar. tōṭarkulak kurumārkaḷ, camayac caṭaṅkukaḷiṉpōtu, malaiyāḷa mantiraṅkaḷaiyē kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. tōṭarkaḷiṉ uruva amaippukaḷ malaiyāḷattiluḷḷa nampūtirikaḷaiyum, nāyarkaḷaiyumē perumpālum ottirukkiṉṟaṉa. tōṭarkaḷ nīṇṭa kālattiṟku muṉ (cumār 800 āṇṭukaṭkumuṉ) malaiyāḷattiliruntu iṅku vantirukka vēṇṭum. ākaiyiṉāl tāṉ tōṭar moḻi tamiḻ moḻiyōṭu neruṅkiya toṭarpu koṇṭuḷḷatu. ēṉeṉṟāl, paṇṭaiya malaiyāḷam tamiḻēyaṉṟō?

piṟa ceytikaḷ :

koṉṉamarā eṉṟa iṭattil tōṭarkaḷukkuriya kōyil oṉṟuḷatu. uyaramāṉa vaṭivattai uṭaiyatu. ' piṅkar pōsṭ'ṭiṟkut tirumpum vaḻiyil itaik kāṇalām. kāṭṭerumaikaḷaik kaṇṭāl, ellōrum añci naṭuṅkuvārkaḷ. āṉāl avaikaḷiṉ arukil accamillāmal ceṉṟu, pāl kaṟakkum. kalaiyait tōṭarkaḷ naṉku aṟivārkaḷ.

kōtar :

kōtar nīlakiri malaimītu 1500 pēr vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. kōtakiriyiluḷḷa 6 ciṟṟūrkaḷum, kūṭalūrukkarukiluḷḷa oru ciṟṟūrum, ivarkaḷuṭaiya vāḻviṭaṅkaḷām. ivarkaḷuṭaiya vīṭukaḷ aḻakaṟṟavai ; uruvattāl periyavai. ovvoru vīṭṭiṉ muṉ pakutiyilum tāḻvāram amaintirukkum. kaṟṟūṇkaḷ vīṭṭil nāṭṭappaṭṭirukkum. āṉāl cittira vēlaippāṭu. avaṟṟil atikamirukkātu. vīṭukaḷ perumpālum kūraiyiṉāl vēyappaṭṭavai.

kōtarkaḷ tūymaiyaṟṟa uṭaliṉar. avarkaḷ aṇiyum āṭaikaḷ mikavum aḻukkaṭaintu kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻaraippōl vēṭṭiyē araiyil uṭukkiṉṟaṉar. kōtarkaḷai eḷitil aṟintukoḷḷalām. paṭakarkaḷaiviṭak kaṟutta niṟamuṭaivarkaḷ ; naṭuvil vakiṭu eṭuttup piṉṉāl kuṭumi muṭintirukkiṉṟaṉar. nākarikam vaḷarntirukkum ik kālattilum kūṭa, iṟanta vilaṅkukaḷiṉ ūṉai mukkiya uṇavākak koḷkiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷuṭaiya kaṭavuḷākiya kāmaṭṭarāyaṉ (Kamattarayan) taṉ uṭaliliruntu vaḻinta mūṉṟu tuḷi viyarvaiyiliruntu tōṭarkaḷaiyum, kuṟumparkaḷaiyum, kōtarkaḷaiyum paṭaittāṉām. atōṭu, tōṭarkaḷ pāl, tayir, veṇṇey ākiyavaṟṟaiyum, kuṟumparkaḷ iṟaicciyaiyum, kōtarkaḷ aḻukiya iṟaicciyaiyum uṇṇa vēṇṭumeṉṟu āṇaiyiṭṭāṉām. avvāṇaiyiṉ paṭiyē tāṅkaḷ aḻukiya iṟaicciyai uṇpatākak kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. iv viḻinta uṇavu, avarkaḷ uṭal nilaikku ēṟṟatāka amaintuviṭṭatu. kōtarkaḷ parutta, uṟutiyāṉa uṭaṟkaṭṭuṭaiyavarkaḷ.

kōtarkaḷ maicūr nāṭṭuk kuṉṟukaḷiliruntu iṅkuk kuṭiyēṟiyatākak kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷ pēcum moḻi tiruntāk kaṉṉaṭam. taccarākavum, kammiyarākavum, cakkiliyarākavum, kollarākavum piṟa iṉattārukkuk kōtarkaḷ paṇipurikiṉṟaṉar. ivviṉap peṇkaḷ maṭpāṇṭam ceytu piṟa iṉattārukku vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉar. ic cēvaikku īṭāka maṟṟa varkaḷiṭamiruntu tāṉiyam, erumaikaḷ, iṟanta mirukaṅkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟaip peṟukiṉṟaṉar. aṟuvaṭaik kālattil oru kuṟippiṭṭa aḷavu tāṉiyam paṭakarkaḷāl āṇṭutōṟum ivarkaṭku vaḻaṅkappaṭum. paṭakarkaḷaip pōṉṟu ivarkaḷum āṇṭutōṟum tōṭarkaḷukku naṉkoṭait tāṉiyam vaḻaṅkuvatu vaḻakkam. ataṟkuk 'kuṭu' (Gudu) eṉṟu peyar. paṭakarkaḷiṭaiyē mūṉṟu pirivukaḷuṇṭu. ip piriviṉar taṅkaḷukkuḷ maṇat toṭarpu koḷvatillai. kōtarkaḷ icaiñarkaḷ. ivarkaḷ nīlakiri malaiyiluḷḷa ellā iṉattārkkum, tirumaṇak kālaṅkaḷilum, viḻāk kālaṅkaḷilum, icaip paṇi purikiṉṟaṉar.

tirumaṇam :

tirumaṇam peṟṟōrkaḷiṉ icaivuṭaṉēyē naṭaipeṟukiṉṟatu. maṇamakaṉ māmaṉāriṉ vīṭṭiṟkuc ceṉṟu, vaṇakkam celuttip paricup poruḷkaḷai aḷittuviṭṭut tirumpuvataṉ mūlam tirumaṇam niccayikkappaṭukiṟatu. tirumaṇattiṟkuriya caṭaṅkukaḷ atikamāka oṉṟumillai. paricappaṇattaik koṭutta piṟaku, maṇamakaṉ maṇamakaḷait taṉ vīṭṭiṟku aḻaittuk koṇṭu varuvāṉ. ataṉ piṟaku viruntu oṉṟu naṭaipeṟum. ittuṭaṉ tirumaṇam muṭivaṭaikiṟatu. mutal maṉaivikkup piḷḷaippēṟu illai yeṉṟāltāṉ, āṇkaḷ iraṇṭāvatu maṇam ceytu koḷḷukiṉṟaṉar.

maṇavilakku :

maṇavilakku kōtarkaḷiṭaiyē atikamāka naṭaipeṟuvatillai. poṟuppaṟṟa taṉmaiyum, kuṭiveṟiyum, cōmpalum, oḻukkak kuṟaivum uṭaiya peṇṇē maṇavilakkiṟkuriyavaḷākak karutappaṭukiṟāḷ. maṇavilakku ūriluḷḷa periyavarkaḷ muṉṉilaiyil naṭaipeṟum. mikavum cikkalāṉa vaḻakkāka iruntāl, kōtarkaḷ vāḻum ēḻu ūrkaḷilumuḷḷa periyōrkaḷaṭaṅkiya pañcāyattāl ārāyappaṭṭu maṇavilakku aḷikkappaṭum.

piḷḷaippēṟu :

piḷḷaip pēṟṟukkākap payaṉpaṭuttappaṭum kuṭicai, iru aṟaikaḷākat taṭukkappaṭṭirukkum. ōr aṟai maruttuvattiṟkuriya maṉaiyākavum, maṟṟoṉṟu piḷḷaippēṟṟuk kālattil taṅkuvataṟkuriya iṭamākavum payaṉpaṭuttappaṭum. kuḻantai piṟantatum, tāy aṭutta uvā nāḷ varai ak kuṭicaiyilēyē taṅkavēṇṭum. uvā nāḷaṉṟu ap peṇṇai vīṭṭukkaḻaippar. aṉṟu ellārukkum oru viruntu naṭaipeṟum. ūrttalaivaṉ kuḻantaikkup peyariṭuvāṉ. mutaṟ kuḻantai āṇāka irun tāl, ataṟkuk 'kāmaṭṭaṉ' eṉṟu taṅkaḷ kaṭavuḷ peyarai iṭuvar. peṇkaḷukku ‘māṭi' eṉṟa peyarē atikamāka vaḻaṅkukiṟatu. māṭi eṉpatu avarkaḷ vaḻipaṭum oru ciṟu teyvattiṉ peyar.

camaya vāḻvu :

kōtarkaḷ vaḻipaṭum kaṭavuḷaril talaimai peṟṟavaṉ kāmaṭṭarāyaṉ. kōtarkaḷiṉ camayakuru 'tēvāti' eṉṟu aḻaikkappaṭukiṟāṉ. camaya kuruviṉ patavi paramparai urimaiyuṭaiyatu. kōyil paṇi puriyum pūcārikaḷaik kōtarkaḷ tērnteṭukkiṉṟaṉar. tōṭarkulap pūcārikaḷukkiruppatu pōṉṟu, taṉiyuṭaiyō, vēṟu kaṭṭuppāṭukaḷō ivarkaḷukkuk kiṭaiyā. maṟṟa makkaḷaip pōlat tirumaṇam ceytu koṇṭu, kuṭumpa vāḻkkaiyil īṭupaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉar. kāmaṭṭarāyaṉ maṉaiviyiṉ peyar 'kācikai.' kōtarkaḷ vāḻum ovvōr ūrilum ivarkaḷukkuk kōyil uṇṭu. kāmaṭṭarāyaṉukku āṇṭutōṟum peru viḻā oṉṟu naṭaipeṟum. taṅkaḷ vāḻvil naṉmai peruka vēṇṭum eṉṟu iṟaivaṉai vēṇṭi avviḻāvaik koṇṭāṭuvar. jaṉavarit tiṅkaḷil uvā nāḷ kaḻintu, mutal tiṅkaṭkiḻamaiyaṉṟu iv viḻā tuvaṅkum. piṟaku patiṉaintu nāṭkaḷ toṭarntu naṭaipeṟum. ip patiṉaintu nāṭkaḷaiyum, ovvōr āṇṭilum ōyvu nāṭkaḷāka ivarkaḷ karutukiṉṟaṉar. it tiruviḻāviṉ pōtu, āṇkaḷum peṇkaḷum oṉṟākak kūṭi āṭum āṭṭam mikavum āpācamāṉatu.

arukil vāḻum paṭakarkaḷil mukkiyamāṉavarkaḷaik kōtarkaḷ ivviḻāviṟku aḻaippatu vaḻakkam. ivarkaḷuṭaiya aḻaippai ēṟṟukkoṇṭu varāviṭṭāl, acceyal perum avamāṉattiṟkuriyatākak kōtarkaḷāl karutappaṭum. avvāṟu vantu viḻāvil kalantu koḷḷāta paṭakarkaḷiṉ nilaṅkaḷil yārum paṇi purivatillai. vēṟu entac caṭaṅkukaḷilum kalantu koḷvatillai. kāmaṭṭarāyaṉ viḻā naṭaipeṟum appatiṉaintu nāṭkaḷum, kōyiliṉ muṉṉāl mūṭṭiya tī aṇaiyāmal erintukoṇṭē irukkum. kōyiliṉ paḻaiya kūraiyai nīkkiviṭṭu, mūṅkiliṉāl putiya kūrai vēyvārkaḷ. iṟaivaṉukkuc ciṟappāṉa āṭaikaḷum, talaippākaiyum aṇivittu alaṅkāram ceyvar. tiruviḻāk kālaṅkaḷil ellārum cērntu vēṭṭaikkuc celvārkaḷ. tēvāti ōr irumput tuṇṭai neruppil pōṭṭup paḻukkak kāyccuvāṉ. pūcāri avvirumput tuṇṭaic cuttiyāl aṭippāṉ. ivvāṟākap palavitac caṭaṅkukaḷ naṭaipeṟukiṉṟaṉa.

iṟuticcaṭaṅku :

iṟakkum nilaiyil iruppavariṉ vāyil paṭakarkaḷaip pōṉṟu vīrarāyap paṇattaip pōṭṭu nīr ūṟṟum paḻakkam ivarkaḷiṭamum uṇṭu, piṇattiṉ kaikaḷai mārpiṉ mēl kiṭattip peru viralkaḷai nūliṉāl kaṭṭiviṭuvārkaḷ. iṟantavariṉ uṟaviṉarkaḷ, piṇattiṟku iṟuti vaṇakkam celuttuvar. marattiṉāl pāṭaiceytu, atait tuṇiyāl alaṅkarippar. alaṅkaritta appāṭaiyai vīṭṭiṉ muṉṉāl vaippar. uṟaviṉar ellārum aḻuvar. maṟṟaiyōr icaikkēṟpa āṭuvar. ōr erumaiyaip paliyiṭṭu, ataṉ ūṉai ellārum paṅkiṭṭuk koḷvārkaḷ. piṇattai alaṅkarittu ataṉ neṟṟiyil kācaip patippārkaḷ, piṟaku irumpiṉāl ceyta ciṟu karuvikaḷaiyum, arici, pukaiyilai, aṉṟāṭa uṇavup poruḷ mutaliyavaṟṟaiyum piṇattōṭu pāṭaiyil vaippārkaḷ. aḻukaiyum, āṭalum, matuvaruntalum palamaṇi nēram naṭaipeṟum. kaṭaiciyākap pāṭaiyai iṭukāṭu nōkki eṭuttuc celluvar, iṟantavaṉ maṉaivi, taṉ nakaikaḷai iṭukāṭṭil kaḷaintu, iṟuti vaṇakkam celuttuvāḷ, piṇattaic citaiyil eṭuttuvaitta piṟaku, pāṭaiyai aḻittu viṭuvar. piṇam erinta piṟaku, nīrūṟṟic citaiyai naṉaittu, elumpukaḷaiyellām poṟukki oru kuḻiyil putaittu mūṭi, ataṉmēl naṭukal nāṭṭuvar.

tōṭarkaḷ evvāṟu kāynta iṟutic caṭaṅku ceykiṟarkaḷō, atē pōl ticampar tiṅkaḷil kōtarkaḷum ceykiṟārkaḷ. iṟantu pōṉavariṉ vīṭukaḷiṉ muṉṉāl, caṭaṅkukaḷ naṭaipeṟuvataṟku eṭṭu nāḷ muṉpiruntē tiṉamum āṭalpāṭal naṭai peṟum. kuṟippiṭṭa nāḷil uṟaviṉarkaḷ ellārum cērntu, erumaiyiṉ maṇṭaiyōṭṭait tuṇiyil oru mūṭṭaiyākak kaṭṭip pāṭaiyiṉ mēl vaittu vaṇaṅkuvārkaḷ. aṉṟu piṇattōṭu vaittavaikaḷaip pōṉṟē, iṉṟum pukaiyilai, arici, uṇavup poruḷ mutaliyavaṟṟaiyum palakaṟaikaḷāl alaṅkarikkappaṭṭa oru taṭiyaiyum am mūṭṭaiyōṭu iṭukāṭṭil vaittu erippārkaḷ. apporuḷkaḷellām erinta piṟaku, citaiyai muṉ pōlavē nīr ūṟṟi naṉaippārkaḷ. ellārum avviravai iṭu kāṭṭilēyē kaḻippar. ittuṭaṉ kōtarkaḷiṉ iṟuticcaṭaṅku muṭivuṟukiṟatu.

piṟa ceytikaḷ :

tōṭarkaḷiṭaiyē iruppatu pōṉṟu oru peṇ pala kaṇavarai ēṟṟukkoḷḷum muṟai kōtarkaḷiṭaiyē illaiyeṉṟālum, ataṉiṉṟum ciṟitu māṟupaṭṭa orumuṟai ivarkaḷiṭaiyē nilavi varukiṉṟatu. oru kuṭumpattil pala cakōtararkaḷ iruntāl, oruvaṉ maṟṟavaṉuṭaiya maṉaiviyōṭu entavita vēṟupāṭumiṉṟit toṭarpu vaittuk koḷvatu vaḻakkam. itai avarkaḷ kuṟṟamākak karutuvatillai. itaṉ kāraṇamākap poṟāmaiyō, pūcalō ēṟpaṭuvatillai. kōtarkaḷiṭaiyē uṭaṉ piṟanta oṟṟumai mikavum atikam. avarkaḷuṭaiya camūka, poruḷiyal muṟaikaḷellām, cōtara oṟṟumaiyiṉ aṭippaṭaiyilēyē amaintuḷḷaṉa. 'uṭaṉ piṟantār illātavaṉ kaiyiḷaittavaṉ' eṉṟa oru paḻamoḻi avarkaḷiṭaiyē nilavukiṟatu. matu aruntuvatilum, apiṉi tiṉpatilum ivarkaḷukkup peruviruppam uṇṭu.

kuṟumpar :

ivarkaḷaik kuṟumar eṉṟum, kuṟupar eṉṟum vēṟu peyarkaḷāl aḻaikkiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷ nīlakirip pīṭapūmiyiluḷḷa ciṟṟūrkaḷilum, vaya nāṭṭilum vāḻkiṟārkaḷ. immakkaḷ kuṟumpar, ūrkkuṟumpar, jēṉ kuṟumpar eṉa mūṉṟu piriviṉarākap pirintu vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. nīlakirip pīṭapūmiyil kuṟumparkaḷum, nelliyāḷattai (Nellialam)c cuṟṟi ūrkkuṟumparkaḷum vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. jēṉ kuṟumparkaḷ, 'ṣōlā nāyakkar'kaḷ eṉṟum aḻaikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷ vaya nāṭṭilum, kuṟippāka matumalaip pakutiyilum vāḻkiṉṟaṉar.

ēḻu, eṭṭu, oṉpatām nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷil, kāñciyait talainakarākak koṇṭu, pallavar eṉṟa iṉattār tamiḻakattaic ciṟappāka āṇṭu vantaṉar. avarkaḷukkum kuṟumparkaḷ eṉṟa vēṟu peyaruṇṭu. māmallapuram. ciṟṟaṉṉavācal, kuṭumiyāṉ malai, nāmakkal, tirucci ākiya iṭaṅkaḷil uḷḷa kuṭaikōyilkaḷum, kāñci nakariluḷḷa peruṅkōyilkaḷum avarkaḷuṭaiya, kalaiyaiyum nākarikattaiyum paṟaicāṟṟiya vaṇṇam iṉṟum kāṭciyaḷikkiṉṟaṉa. 10ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ tuvakkattil peruñcōḻa (Imperial Cholas)riṉ āṭci tamiḻakattil ōṅkiyapōtu, pallavarkaḷiṉ pēraracu citaintu, cōḻap pēraracōṭu oṉṟiviṭṭatu. cōḻariṭam tōlviyuṟṟa pallavarkaḷ, nīlakiri malaiyil ōṭi oḷintaṉareṉṟum, avar vaḻi vantōrē iṉṟuḷḷa kuṟumpar eṉṟum cilar karutukiṉṟaṉar. immuṭivu ēṟṟukkoḷḷak kūṭiyatāka illai. ēṉeṉṟāl pallavarkaḷ nākarikamum, kalaiyuṇarvum mikutiyākak koṇṭavarkaḷ; vaṭa moḻiyaiyō, ataṉ cārpu moḻiyaiyō tāymoḻiyākak koṇṭavarkaḷ; vaṭamoḻik kalaikaḷai vaḷarttavarkaḷ. āṉāl nīlakirik kuṟumparkaḷ nākarikamum kalaiyuṇarvum aṟṟavarkaḷ. tirāviṭa moḻiyait tāymoḻiyākak koṇṭavarkaḷ. pallavarkaḷ poṉṉiṟa mēṉiyum, aḻakiya tōṟṟamum vāykkap peṟṟavarkaḷ. āṉāl kuṟumparkaḷō, kaṟutta uṭalum, aḻakaṟṟa tōṟṟamum koṇṭavarkaḷ. pallavarkaḷ cōḻa nāṭṭai viṭṭu veḷiyēṟiya kālam pattām nūṟṟāṇṭu. tōṭarkaḷiṉ varukaikkup piṉ pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷ kaḻittē kuṟumparkaḷ nīlakiri malaiyil kuṭiyēṟiṉar. eṉavē, nīlakirik kuṟumparkaḷ pallavarkaḷāka irukka muṭiyātu.

ūrkkuṟumparait tavira maṟṟa iru iṉattārum kūccam koḷḷum iyalpiṉar. āṉāl kōḻaikaḷalla. veḷḷaiyaraik kaṇṭāl muṉpellām ōṭi oḷintu koḷvārkaḷ. kuṟumpar vāḻum ciṟṟūr 'mōṭṭā' eṉṟum, 'kampai' eṉṟum aḻaikkappaṭukiṟatu. ippoḻutu 'kulakkampai, mañcak kampai' eṉṟu vaḻaṅkum iṭaṅkaḷil kuṟumpar vāḻvatillai. āṉāl mutalil avviṭaṅkaḷilum vāḻntirukka vēṇṭum eṉpatai appeyarkaḷāl aṟiyalām. ovvoru kampaiyilum, cumār paṉṉiraṇṭu kuṭicaikaḷ irukkum. ikkuṭicaikaḷ maṇṇiṉāl kaṭṭappaṭṭup puṟkaḷāl kūrai vēyappaṭṭavai.

tōṟṟam :

kuṟumparkaḷ karuniṟamum, kuḷḷamāṉa uruvamum, oṟṟai nāṭi uṭalum peṟṟu, tōṟṟattil iruḷarkaḷaippōl viḷaṅkukiṟārkaḷ. ivarkaḷ talaiyiluḷḷa mayir taṭippākavum curuṇṭum irukkum; talaiyaic cuṟṟip putar pōlap paravi muḷaittirukkum, peṇkaḷ orē oru tuṇiyai āṭaiyāka aṇikiṉṟaṉar. mārpai mūṭumpaṭiyāka akkuḷaic cuṟṟi attuṇiyai muṭintu koḷkiṉṟaṉar. muḻantāḷ varaiyil attuṇi kīḻē maṟaittukkoṇṭirukkiṟatu.

moḻi ;

kuṟumparkaḷ pēcum moḻi kaṉṉaṭattōṭu neruṅkiya toṭarpuṭaiyateṉṟum, citainta kaṉṉaṭamē ammoḻi eṉṟum kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. āṉāl tirāviṭa moḻiyārāycci vallunarāṉa kālṭuvel ammoḻiyaic citainta tamiḻ eṉṟu kūṟukiṟār.



toḻil :

kuṟumparkaḷ tōṭarkaḷaippōl mēyccalkārarkaḷumalla; paṭarkaḷaip pōl nilakkiḻārkaḷumalla, ivarkaḷ kōtarkaḷaip pōl ēḻaikaḷ. piṟa iṉattārkkup paṇipurintē vāḻpavarkaḷ, tōṭarkaḷukku ivarkaḷ añcuvatumillai; maṟṟavarkaḷaip pōl 'kuṭu' koṭuppatumillai. kōtarkaḷōṭu cērntu maṟṟaiyōrkku icaippaṇi purintu vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. mantira vittaiyil ivarkaḷ mikavum vallavarkaḷ. itaṉālēyē ivarkaḷaik kaṇṭu maṟṟa iṉattār mikavum añcukiṉṟaṉar. 'paṭakar' vāḻum ovvoru ciṟṟūrilum, avarkaḷukkut tuṇaipurivataṟkākak kuṟumparkaḷai amarttik koḷḷuvatuṇṭu. nilattai mutaṉ mutalāka uḻum caṭaṅkaiyum, mutal vitaiyai vitaikkum caṭaṅkaiyum, muṟṟiya payirai mutaṉ mutalāka aṟukkum caṭaṅkaiyum nikaḻttivaikkap paṭakarkaḷāl ivarkaḷ aḻaikkappaṭukiṟārkaḷ. paṭakarkaḷiṉ nilattil paṇṇaiyāṭkaḷākavum kuṟumparkaḷ paṇipurikiṉṟaṉar, kāl naṭaikaḷukkum, payirkaḷukkum ēṟpaṭum nōyait tīrppataṟkup paṭakarkaḷ ivarkaḷaiyē nāṭavēṇṭiyirukkiṟatu. ippōtu kuṟumparkaḷiṭam paṭakarkaḷ koṇṭirukkum accam ciṟitu kuṟaintuviṭṭatu eṉalām. paṇṭaikkālattil paṭakarkaḷ koṇṭirunta accattaip paṟṟi tiru. meṭs (Mr. metz) eṉṟa airōppiyar kuṟippiṭum pōtu, “oru paṭakaṉ taṉiyāka oru kuṟumpaṉaik kāṭṭiṟkuḷ etirpārātavitamākak kāṇa nēriṭṭāl, añci naṭuṅki appoḻutē uyirviṭṭuviṭuvāṉ" eṉṟu kūṟukiṟār.

jēṉ kuṟumparkaḷ malai iṭukkukaḷil uḷḷa malait tēṉīyiṉ kūṭṭai aḻittut tēṉ eṭuppatil vallavarkaḷ, itai ivarkaḷ toḻilākavum koṇṭirukkiṟārkaḷ. jēṉu eṉṟāl kaṉṉaṭattil tēṉ eṉṟu poruḷ. ākaiyiṉāl ikkuṟumparkaḷ taṅkaḷait “tēṉeṭukkum kāṭṭut talaivarkaḷ" (jēṉu koyyō ṣōlā nāyakā) eṉṟu aḻaittuk koḷkiṟārkaḷ.

aka vāḻkkai :

kuṟumpar kulattil oru peṇ paruvamaṭaintatum, appeṇṇiṉ mūtta cakōtaraṉ putitāka oru kuṭicaiyai amaippatu vaḻakkam. atil appeṇṇaik koṇṭupōy vaippārkaḷ. uṟaviṉarkaḷ nāḷ tōṟum kuṭicaikkuc ceṉṟu appeṇṇaip pārppārkaḷ. paruvamaṭainta pattām nāḷ appeṇṇaik kuḷippāṭṭi vīṭṭiṟ kaḻaippārkaḷ. attai makaḷē perumpālum maṇattiṟkuriyavaḷākat tērnteṭukkappaṭukiṟāḷ. tirumaṇam peṟṟōriṉ icaivuṭaṉēyē naṭaipeṟum. maṇamakaḷiṉ tāyār peṇṇukkut tāliyum, maṇamakaṉukkup puttāṭaiyum aḷippāḷ. maṇamakaṉiṉ peṟṟōr peṇṇukkuk kūṟaippuṭavaiyum, pittaḷaiyiṉāl ceyta mōtiraṅkaḷum vaḷaiyalkaḷum aḷippar. maṇap peṇṇiṉ tāy tāliyaik kaiyileṭuttu makaḷiṉ kaḻuttil kaṭṭuvāḷ. piṟaku ellārum makiḻcciyuṭaṉ viruntuṇpar. viruntiṉpōtu maṇamakkaḷukku orē uṇkalattil uṇavu parimāṟappaṭum. uṇaviṉ oru pakutiyai maṇamakaṉ maṇap peṇṇukkum maṇappeṇ maṇamakaṉukkum ūṭṭa vēṇṭum. viruntu muṭintatum maṇamakkaḷait taṉiyaṟaiyil viṭṭuviṭṭu ellārum pirintu celvar.

maṇavilakku kuṟumparkaḷiṭaiyē aṉumatikkappaṭukiṟatu. āṇukkē maṇavilakkuk kōra urimaiyuṇṭu; peṇṇukkuk kiṭaiyātu. maṟumaṇamum, kaimpeṇ maṇamum ivarkaḷiṭaiyē naṭaipeṟukiṉṟaṉa. im maṇaṅkaḷil peṇvīṭṭārē māppiḷḷai tēṭavēṇṭum. maṟumaṇattiṟku enta vitac caṭaṅkum kiṭaiyātu. peṟṟōr icaivu kiṭaittavuṭaṉ, taṉik kuṭittaṉam ceyya vēṇṭiyatu tāṉ.

iṟutic caṭaṅku :

kuṟumparkaḷiṉ iṟutic caṭaṅkukaḷum paṭakarkaḷiṉ caṭaṅkukaḷaiyē ottirukkiṉṟaṉa. ivarkaḷum vīrarāyap paṇattai iṟappavaṉ vāyil pōṭṭu nīr ūṟṟukiṉṟaṉar. tēramaittu atil piṇattaik kiṭatti iṭukāṭṭiṟku eṭuttuccellukiṉṟaṉar. mikavum vayatu mutirntavarkaḷai erittuviṭukiṉṟaṉar. maṟṟavarkaḷai uṭkārnta nilaiyil putaittu naṭukal nāṭṭukiṉṟaṉar. ēḻām nāḷ iṟantavariṉ kuṭumpattaic cērnta ellārum iṭukāṭṭiṟkuc celvar. oru mutiyavar kuḻipaṟittu atil oru pullai naṭuvār. mūṅkiliṉāl ceyta oru kalattil nīrai nirappi, atil iraṇṭu coṭṭu viḷakkeṇṇey viṭuvārkaḷ. ivviraṇṭu coṭṭu viḷakkeṇṇeyum tolaivil vilakiyiruntāl, tīccakuṉam eṉṟu karuti, vēṟu entac caṭaṅkum ceyyamāṭṭārkaḷ. oṉṟu cērntāl naṟcakuṉamākak karuti, annīraik kīḻē koṭṭi viṭuvārkaḷ. piṟaku putuppāṉai oṉṟu vāṅkip 'pillālā' kōyilil vaippārkaḷ, uṟaviṉarkaḷ yārēṉum camātiyaik kaṭantu cella nēriṭṭāl veṟṟilai, pākku, pukaiyilai mutaliyavaṟṟai vaittu vaṇaṅkiviṭṭuc celvar. iraṇṭāṇṭukaḷ muṭiyum varai, maḻai kālattil camātiyiṉ mēl pantal pōṭuvārkaḷ!

piṟa ceytikaḷ :

kuṟumparkaḷ kallāttā (peṇ teyvam), airu pilli, kāṭupillālā eṉṟa mūṉṟu teyvaṅkaḷai vaṇaṅkukiṉṟaṉar. airupilliyum, kāṭupillālāvum malaiyāḷattiliruntu vayanāṭṭil kuṭipukunta teyvaṅkaḷākak karutappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. nīlakiriyiluḷḷa maṟṟa paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷaiviṭa, jēṉ kuṟumparkaḷum mantirattil vallavarkaḷākak karutappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷ niṉaittapōtu kāṭṭāṉaikaḷai aḻaikkum āṟṟal mikkavarkaḷeṉṟum, cila marmamāṉa mūlikaikaḷai vīcip pāṟaikaḷaikkūṭap poṭi ceytu viṭukiṟārkaḷ eṉṟum katai kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. camūkac caṭṭaṅkaḷai amul naṭatta ovvoru ūrilum oru pañcāyattu uḷḷatu. ap pañcāyattiṉ talaivar 'ecamāṉ' eṉṟu aḻaikkappaṭukiṟār. tōṭarkaḷum paṭakarkaḷum oṉṟu cērntu kuṟumparkaḷukku aṭikkaṭi tollai koṭuppatuṇṭu. kuṟumparkaḷ vāḻum ūrkaḷil pukuntu, kūṭṭaṅ kūṭṭamāka avarkaḷaik koṉṟuviṭuvārkaḷ. it takaiya paṭukolaikaḷ, ki. pi. 1824, 1835, 1875, 1882, 1900 ākiya āṇṭukaḷil naṭaipeṟṟirukkiṉṟaṉa. kuṟumparkaḷ ciṟanta villāḷikaḷ. kōṭṭaiyam maṉṉarukkum āṅkilēyarukkum pōr ēṟpaṭṭa pōtu, kōṭṭaiya maṉṉariṉ cārpiliruntu kuṟumparkaḷ vīrappōr purintaṉar.

vayanāṭṭuc ceṭṭikaḷ :

nīlakiri malaiyiluḷḷa vaya nāṭṭu vaṭṭattil vāḻpavarkaḷ ālaiyāl, ivarkaḷ vaya nāṭṭuc ceṭṭikaḷ eṉṟu aḻaikkappaṭukiṟārkaḷ, ivarkaḷ toḻilāl uḻavarkaḷ. ivarkaḷ malaiyāḷa moḻi pēcuvatōṭu, malaiyāḷattiluḷḷa marumakkaḷ tāya muṟaiyaiyum kaṭaippiṭittu oḻukukiṉṟaṉar. vaya nāṭṭuc ceṭṭikaḷiṉ muṉṉōrkaḷ kōvai māvaṭṭattiluḷḷa tārāpurattiliruntu nīlakiri malaimītu kuṭipukunta veḷḷāḷac ceṭṭiyarē āvar. eṉavē ivarkaḷ iṉattāl tamiḻar, vaya nāṭṭuc ceṭṭikaḷiṭaiyē iraṇṭuvitat tirumaṇaṅkaḷ uṇṭu. mutalvakait tirumaṇattiṉpaṭi, peṇṇāṉavaḷ kaṇavaṉiṭam toṭarpu (marital relations} koḷḷuvāḷ ; āṉāl kaṇavaṉ vīṭṭukkuc ceṉṟu vāḻamāṭṭāḷ. maṟṟoru vakait tirumaṇam ‘mālaik kalyāṇam' eṉṟu collappaṭukiṟatu. ataṉpaṭi maṉaivi kaṇavaṉōṭu vāḻa aṉumatikkappaṭukiṟāḷ. marumakkaḷ tāya muṟaiyē it tirumaṇa vēṟupāṭukaḷukkuk kāraṇam eṉṟu collalām. ivarkaḷ vaya nāṭṭup pīṭapūmiyil, iḻintuvarum nīraruvikaḷiṉ nīraippāycci nel viḷaivikkiṉṟaṉar. atōṭu puṉceyt tāṉiyaṅkaḷaiyum viḷaivikkiṉṟaṉar. vaya nāṭṭuc ceṭṭikaḷiṉ camutāyattil aintu kuṭumpaṅkaḷ talaimaipeṟṟa kuṭumpaṅkaḷākak karutappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. akkuṭumpattāriṉ āṇaivaḻi ellārum oḻukukiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷ ciṟanta uḻaippāḷikaḷ ; kaṭṭuppāṭṭiṟ kaṭaṅkiyavarkaḷ ; amaitiyāṉa vāḻkkaiyai uṭaiyavarkaḷ ; vēṭṭaiyil viruppamuṭaiyavarkaḷ; pulikaḷai valai pōṭṭup piṭittu, īṭṭiyāl kuttik kollum vēṭṭai muṟaiyil kaitērntavarkaḷ. vayanāṭṭuc ceṭṭiyaril palar nilakkiḻārkaḷāka iruntālum, perumpālōr ēḻaikaḷ. cultāṉ pēṭṭariyilirukkum māppiḷḷaikaḷiṭam ellārum kaṭaṉpaṭṭu vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. (māppiḷḷaikaḷeṉpōr malaiyāḷattu muslīmkaḷ, arēpiyāviliruntu malaiyāḷakkaṭaṟkaraiyil kuṭiyēṟi aṅkēyē nilaittuviṭṭavarkaḷ. ivarkaḷ cēra nāṭṭut tamiḻp peṇkaḷai maṇantu, namakku māppiḷḷaikaḷāki viṭṭaṉar pōlum. vaya nāṭṭil periya vaṭṭikkaṭaikkārarkaḷāka ivarkaḷ viḷaṅkukiṟārkaḷ). vaya nāṭṭuc ceṭṭikaḷ peruṅ kuṭiyarkaḷ.

piṟa ceytikaḷ :

nīlakiriyil paṉiyar, iruḷar eṉṟu vēṟucila paḻaṅkuṭiyiṉarum vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷ ellārum maṟṟa iṉattārkkup paṇipurintu varukiṉṟaṉar. kuṟumparkaḷ iruḷarkaḷaik kaṇṭu mikavum añcuvārkaḷ. kāraṇam ivarkaḷum periya mantiravātikaḷ eṉpatē. iruḷar kulattaic cērnta peṇ, veḷiyil vēlaikkākac cellumpōtu, peṇ pulikaḷait taṉ kuḻantaikaḷukkuk kāvalāka vaittuviṭṭuc celluvāḷ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.

malaikaḷil vāḻum ip paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷiṉ nalaṉukkāka, mattiya aracāṅkamum, ceṉṉai aracāṅkamum niṟaiyap poruṭ celavu ceykiṉṟaṉa. ivarkaḷiṉ kalvikkāka rū. 10.67 ilaṭcam otukkiyuḷḷaṉar. mutal aintāṇṭut tiṭṭattiṉpaṭi māṇavarkaḷ taṅkip paṭikkak kūṭiya 9 paḷḷikaḷum, 11 cātāraṇap paḷḷikaḷum ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iraṇṭāvatu aintāṇṭut tiṭṭattil, 16 paḷḷikaḷum tuvakkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivaikaḷaṉṟi 4 taṅkum viṭutikaḷum naṭattappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.

malaivāḻ makkaḷil perumpālōr uḻavuta toḻil ceyya nilam illātavarkaḷāka irukkiṉṟaṉar. orucilar tām uḻavut toḻilil īṭupaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉar. uḻavut toḻil ceyvataṟkum, uḻavuk karuvikaḷ vāṅkuvataṟkum avarkaḷiṭam paṇavacati kiṭaiyātu. ākavē uḻavu māṭukaḷ, uḻavuk karuvikaḷ, vitaikaḷ mutaliyaṉavāṅkuvataṟkāka ivarkaḷukku māṉiyaṅkaḷ vaḻaṅkuvataṟkeṉṟu rū 5.8 ilaṭcam otukkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa.

cila malaivāḻ makkaḷ kūṭaimuṭaital, taccuvēlai mutaliya ciṟu kaittoḻilkaḷaic ceytal ākiyavaṟṟil vallavarkaḷāka irukkiṉṟaṉar. it tuṟaiyil avarkaḷukku utaviyaḷippataṟkāka rū 2.75 ilaṭcam otukkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. nīlakirip pīṭapūmiyiluḷḷa kōtakiriyil orupayiṟci uṟpatti nilaiyam tuvakkavum, cila kuṭicait toḻilkaḷait tuvakkavum inta niti payaṉpaṭuttappaṭum.

malaivāḻ makkaḷ nalla kāṟṟuḷḷa iṭaṅkaḷil vāḻntuvanta pōtilum, nalvāḻvu naṭattavillai. malēriyāk kāyccaliṉāl ivarkaḷ kaṭumaiyākat tākkappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. itaṉāl palar āṇṭutōṟum iṟantuviṭukiṉṟaṉar. nīlakiri malaiyiluḷḷa tōṭarkaḷ tōlnōyiṉāl pīṭikkappaṭṭu āṇṭutōṟum makkaḷ tokaiyil kuṟaintuvantaṉar. nīlakiri māvaṭṭap paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷukkāka oru naṭamāṭum maruttuvamaṉai {mobile dispensary) iyaṅkivarukiṉṟatu. malaivāḻ makkaḷ irukkum ellāp pakutikaḷukkum immaruttuvamaṉai ceṉṟu paṇipurivataṉ mūlam tōṭarkaḷiṭaiyē paravivanta tōlnōy aṟavē nīṅkiviṭṭatu. koyiṉā maruntu ellārukkum vaḻaṅkuvataṉ mūlam malēriyāvum kaṭṭuppaṭuttappaṭṭirukkiṟatu.

paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷ taṇṇīr niṟaiyak kiṭaikkak kūṭiya malaippakutikaḷil vāḻnta pōtilum, tūymaiyāṉa kuṭi taṇṇīr kiṭaikkāmal varuntukiṉṟaṉar. eṉavē ellā māvaṭṭaṅkaḷilumuḷḷa malaivāḻ makkaḷukkuk kuṭi taṇṇīr vacatikaḷ ceyvataṟkāka iraṇṭāvatu aintāṇṭut tiṭṭattil rū 80,000 otukkap paṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa.

malaivāḻ makkaḷ ciṟukuṭicaikaḷil kuṭiyirukkiṉṟaṉar. akkuṭicaikaḷ vilaṅkukaḷum taṅkamuṭiyātapaṭi mikavum kēvalamāṉa nilaiyil uḷḷaṉa. nalla kuṭiyiruppukaḷ illāmal avarkaḷ nalattuṭaṉ vāḻvatu eppaṭi? ākavē vīṭukaḷ kaṭṭa rū 5.53 ilaṭcam otukkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. ovvoru vīṭṭiṟkum rū 900 celaviṭa muṭivu ceytuḷḷaṉar.



iraṇṭāvatu aintāṇṭut tiṭṭattil nīlakiri, cēlam māvaṭṭaṅkaḷiluḷḷa malaikaḷiṉmītu cālai vacatikaḷaip perukkuvataṟkāka rū. 37,000 celaviṭappaṭum. āṭaikaḷ mutaliya itara celaviṉaṅkaḷukkāka rū 77,000 otukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ittiṭṭaṅkaḷellām nalla muṟaiyil amulākkappaṭṭāl, paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷiṉ vāḻvu ciṟappaṭaiyum eṉpatil aiyamillai.

kuṭiyēṟṟam :

mutaṉ mutalākak ki. pi. 1602 ām āṇṭil oru pōrttukīciyap pātiriyār, nīlakiri malaiyaik kāṇpataṟkāka vantār. avar peyar ḵperrīri (Ferreiri). nīlakiriyil vāḻum tōṭarkaḷōṭu uraiyāṭiyatōṭu amaiyāmal, avarkaḷ kuṭumpaṅkaḷukkup pala paricilkaḷum vaḻaṅkiṉār. mikavum toṉmaiyāṉa paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷāṉa tōṭarkaḷ vāḻum ceyti, appoḻututāṉ camaveḷiyil vāḻum makkaḷukkut terintatu.

ḵperrīrikkup piṟaku nīlakiri malaiyai ārāyvataṟku, 200āṇṭukaḷ varaiyil yārum muyaṟci eṭuttuk koḷḷavillai. ṭākṭar puccāṉaṉ (Dr. Buchanan) eṉpavar, intiya araciyalāriṉ āṇaiyai mēṟkoṇṭu, maicūr nāṭṭilum ataṟku aṇmaiyilum uḷḷa malaikaḷai, vāṇipa nōkkōṭum, payirt toḻil nōkkōṭum ārāyvataṟkāka vantār. nīlakiri malaiyaiyum cuṟṟip pārttār. oru nāḷil evvāṟu atai ārāyamuṭiyum? nīlakiri malaiyaip paṟṟi avar koṭutta kuṟippukaḷ payaṉaṟṟavai ; mēṟpōkkāṉavai. ṭākṭar puccāṉaṉ ceṉṟu tirumpiyapiṉ, 12 āṇṭukaḷ kaḻittu nīlakiri malaiyai viññāṉa nōkkōṭu ārāyvataṟkāka tiru. kīs (Keys) eṉṟa ōr ārāycciyāḷaraiyum, avarukkut tuṇaiyāka makmakāṉ (Macmahon) eṉṟa oruvaraiyum kōvai māvaṭṭat taṇṭalar aṉuppivaittār. āṉāl avarkaḷ aḷitta kuṟippukaḷ conta ārāycciyiṉāl eḻuntavaikaḷalla. piṟar vāyilākak kēṭṭaṟintavaiyē. eṉavē avarkaḷuṭaiya ārāycciyum payaṉaṟṟatāka oḻintatu.

āṟu āṇṭukaḷ kaḻintapiṉṉar, kōvai māvaṭṭattiṉ taṇṭalarākap paṇiyāṟṟiyavarum, nīlakirimalaiyiṉ varalāṟṟil mukkiya iṭam peṟṟavarumāṉa tiruvāḷar jāṉ callivaṉ eṉpār, ārāycci viruppum, viṭā muyaṟciyum koṇṭa iruvarai nīlakiri malaikku aṉuppiṉār. avarkaḷ ārāyntu veḷiyiṭṭa karuttukkaḷ taṇṭalariṉ karuttai īrttaṉa. avarum nīlakirikkuc ceṉṟār. ceṉṟatōṭu amaiyāmal, oru nalla iṭattait tērnteṭuttu, kōṭaiyil taṅkuvataṟkuriya kuḷirmaṉai yoṉṟai eḻuppuvataṟkāṉa muyaṟcikaḷil īṭupaṭṭār. nīlakirimalaiyiṉ mītu kuṭiyēṟiya mutal airōppiyar ivarē.

tiruvāḷar jāṉ callivaṉ nīlakiri malaikkuc ceṉṟa āṇṭu ki. pi. 1819 ākum. nīlakiri malaiyai uṇmaiyil ārāyntu piṟarukku veḷiyiṭṭavar ivarē eṉil, atu mikaiyākātu. ippōtu utaka maṇṭalam amaintirukkumiṭattai tiru. callivaṉ mutalil kaṇṭatum, avviṭattiṉ iyaṟkaiyaḻakum. taṭpa veppa nilaiyum avar uḷḷattai vekuvākak kavarntaṉa. uṭaṉē, avviṭam oru kuṟiñci nakaram amaippataṟkuriya ciṟanta takutikaḷaiyellām peṟṟirukkiṟatu eṉṟa ceytiyai araciyalārukku aṟivittār. ki. pi. 1823-ām āṇṭu tām taṅkiyiruppataṟkāka ōraḻakiya maṉaiyai eḻuppiṉār. atu ippoḻutu 'kalmaṉai' (Stone House) eṉṟa peyarāl aḻaik ppaṭukiṟatu. utaka maṇṭalattiluḷḷa cantai veḷiyiṉarukil, uyaramāṉa iṭattil cila māṟutalkaḷaip peṟṟu iṉṟum kalmaṉai kaṇṇaikkavarum tōṟṟattōṭu viḷaṅkukiṟatu.

ki. pi. 1824 ām āṇṭiliruntu airōppiyarkaḷ perum aḷavil nīlakiri malai mītu kuṭiyēṟat toṭaṅkiṉar. kōṭaikkālaṅkaḷil, ceṉṉai mānila āḷunarum (governor) iṅku vantu taṅkat toṭaṅkiṉār. āḷunar āṇṭutōṟum vantu taṅkuvataṟkeṉṟu araciyalār ōraḻakiya māḷikai eḻuppiṉar. ammāḷikai mutalil ' 'araciyal maṉai' (Government House) eṉṟa peyarāl aḻaikkappaṭṭatu. ippoḻutu atai 'nārvuṭ maṉai' (Norwood House) eṉṟu ellārum aḻaikkiṉṟaṉar. pakkiṅkām pirapu (Duke of Buckingham) ceṉṉai mānila āḷunarākap paṇiyāṟṟiya pōtu, araciyal maṉaiyiṉ vaḷarccikkup peritum pāṭupaṭṭār. ammāḷikaiyaic cuṟṟi aḻakāṉa tōṭṭattaiyum amaittār. māḷikaiyilum tōṭṭattilum paṉipurivōriṉ aluvalkaḷai aṭikkaṭi kaṇkāṇittār. ivaruṭaiya iṭaiyaṟāta talaiyīṭṭai appaṇiyāḷarkaḷ periya tollaiyākak kūṭak karutiṉar. ōyvu nāṭkaḷil eḷiya uṭaiyaṇintu cuṟṟi varuvār pirapu. orunāḷ kālaiyil, mukac cavaram kūṭac ceytu koḷḷāmal, alaṅkōlamāṉa tōṟṟattōṭu tōṭṭattaic cuṟṟi vantār. ōr airōppiya vēlaikkāraṉ pātti amaittukkoṇṭiruntāṉ. pirapu avaṉarukil ceṉṟu, "ēṉappā, ippāttiyai ippaṭi amaittāl naṉṟāyirukku mallavā? ic ceṭikaḷai ēṉ ippaṭi naṭak kūṭātu?" eṉṟu viṉaviṉār. av vēlaikkāraṉ pirapuvai attakaiya tōṟṟattil itaṟku muṉ pārttatu kiṭaiyātu. vēṟu, yārō vaḻippōkkar eṉṟu eṇṇiṉāṉ. avaṉ avarai nimirntu kūṭap pārkkavumillai ; avaruṭaiya viṉākkaḷaic ceviyil vāṅkik koḷḷavumillai. pirapu mēlum mēlum viṉavavē, avaṉ poṟumai yiḻantāṉ. uṭaṉē talai nimirntu, "ēṉayyā! uṉakku vēṟu vēlaiyillaiyā? um vaḻiyaip pārttukkoṇṭu pōm. pakkiṅkām pirapu eṉṟa eṇṇam pōlirukkiṟatu!" eṉṟu ciṉanturaittāṉ. uṭaṉē pirapu taṉṉai maṟantu cirittār. vēlaikkāraṉ avarai uṭaṉē yāreṉṟu uṇarntu koṇṭāṉ. taṉ ceyalukkāka varunti maṉṉippuk kēṭṭukkoṇṭāṉ.

araciyal maṉaikkuc caṟṟu mēlē, kāṭṭilākā atikāri oruvarāl payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭa kuṭicai yoṉṟuḷḷatu. araciyal maṉaiyil taṅkiyirunta āḷunariṉ kuḻantaikaḷ aṅkuc ceṉṟu viḷaiyāṭuvatu vaḻakkam. ippoḻutu atu iṉpa viruntu (Picnic) uṇṇa mikavum ēṟṟa iṭamāka viḷaṅkukiṟatu.

utakamaṇṭalam

peyark kāraṇam :

utaka maṇṭalam eṉṟu innakarukkup peyar ēṟpaṭṭatu paṟṟi oru cevi vaḻic ceyti ulavu kiṉṟatu. 'maṇṭu' eṉṟa col tōṭar vāḻum ciṟṟūraik kuṟikkum eṉṟu muṉpē kuṟippiṭṭēṉ. tiruvāḷar callivaṉ kaṭṭiya kalmaṉai amaintirukkum iṭam, muṉpu oru maṇṭuvāka iruntatām. ovvoru maṇṭuvilum vaḻakkamāka nāṉkaintu kuṭicaikaḷ irukkum. āṉāl inta maṇṭuvil orē oru kuṭicaitāṉ iruntatām. pārtkai (Parth-Kai) eṉṟa oru vayatu mutirnta tōṭar kulac celvaṉ ak kuṭicaiyil taṉ kuṭumpattārōṭu vāḻntu vantāṉ, celvarkaḷāka irukkum tōṭar vīṭṭiṉ etirē ciṟitu tolaivil oru kal naṭappaṭṭirukkum. ataṉmēl taṅkaḷ kaṭavuḷukku uṇavu paṭaippatu avarkaḷ vaḻakkam.

tiruvāḷar callivaṉ mutaṉ mutalāka nīlakiri malaikku vantapoḻutu, pārtkaiyaik kaṇṭu pēciṉār. pārtkai kal nāṭṭappaṭṭirunta avviṭattaik kāṭṭi, “jellōkō e maṇṭu" eṉṟu kūṟiṉāṉām. “kal nāṭṭappaṭirukkum ic ciṟṟūrai nī vaittuk koḷ” eṉpatu attoṭariṉ poruḷ. 'jel lōkō' eṉṟa toṭar moḻic col, taṉiyāka niṟkum oru kallaiyō, allatu kal nāṭṭappaṭṭa ciṟṟūraiyō kuṟippiṭum. ac colliṉ tamiḻ moḻi peyarppu, oṟṟaikkal maṇṭu eṉpatākum. oṟṟaikkal maṇṭu eṉṟa peyar ottaikkal maṇṭu eṉa māṟi, ippoḻutu 'uṭakamaṇṭ' (Ootacamund) eṉṟu vaḻaṅkuvatākak kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.

vēṟu cilar ippeyaraip paṭakarkaḷōṭu toṭarpu paṭuttukiṉṟaṉar, paṭakar, vaṭakkiluḷḷa maicūr nāṭṭiliruntu vantatāl vaṭakar eṉṟum aḻaikkappaṭṭaṉar eṉpatai muṉpē kuṟippiṭṭēṉ, vaṭakar vantu kuṭiyēṟiya avviṭam • vaṭakamaṇṭu' eṉṟu tōṭarkaḷāl aḻaikkappaṭṭatām, piṟaku appeyar ‘vaṭṭayāka maṇṭu,' ' 'vaṭṭaca maṇṭu' eṉap palavitamāka māṟi, ippoḻutu uṭakamaṇṭ eṉṟu vaḻaṅkuvatākavum kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. vēṟu cilar maṟṟoru kāraṇamum kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. ikkāraṇam ēṟṟuk koḷḷak kūṭiyatākavum uḷḷatu. 'utakamaṇṭalam' eṉpatē ātiyiliruntu ivvūrukku vaḻaṅkum peyar eṉpatu ak koḷkai, utakamaṇṭalam eṉṟāl paṭakar moḻiyil 'eppōtum maḻai peytu koṇṭirukkumiṭam' eṉpatu poruḷ.



vaḻi :

utakamaṇṭalattai iru vaḻikaḷil vantaṭaiyalām. maicūriliruntu malaivaḻip pātaiyil 100 kal untu vaṇṭiyiṉ mūlam ceṉṟu utaka maṇṭalattai aṭaiyalām. kōvaiyiliruntu mēṭṭup pāḷaiyam vaḻiyākavum ceṉṟaṭaiyalām. maicūriliruntu varum vaḻi kaṉṉaṭak kāṭṭil (Kanara forest) pukuntu varukiṟatu. ivvaḻi aḻakiya kāṭcikaḷaik koṇṭatu; uḷḷattiṟku iṉpattaip payappatu. kōvaiyiliruntu utaka maṇṭalam cella untu vaṇṭikaḷum niṟaiya uḷḷaṉa. āṉāl pukaivaṇṭiyil ēṟi, mēṭṭup pāḷaiyam vaḻiyākac ceṉṟāl nīlakiriyiṉ malaivaḷattai nalla muṟaiyil kaṇṭu iṉpuṟalām. pukaivaṇṭic celavu nam poṟumaiyai aḷavukku mīṟic cōtittuviṭum. pukai vaṇṭi mikavum metuvākac cellum. mēlum iṭaiyiluḷḷa pukai vaṇṭi nilaiyaṅkaḷil aṭikkaṭi niṉṟu viṭum. maṟṟa pukai vaṇṭikaḷukkum nīlakiri malaimēl cellum pukai vaṇṭikaḷukkum oru vēṟupāṭu uṇṭu. ivvaṇṭikaḷil iyaṅki (engine) muṉṉāl poruttappaṭuvatillai, piṉṉiruntu vaṇṭiyait taḷḷukiṟatu. ivvaṇṭiyiluḷḷa mutal peṭṭi tiṟanta pakkaṅkaḷaiyuṭaiya tāka irukkum. ākaiyiṉāl ippeṭṭiyil iṭam piṭikka ellāp pirayāṇikaḷum pōṭṭiyiṭuvar. ēṉeṉṟāl appeṭṭiyil uṭkārntiruppōr malaiccarivukaḷil uḷḷa iyaṟkaik kāṭcikaḷai naṉku. pārttu makiḻa muṭiyum.

vaḷarcci :

araciyal maṉai (Government House) kaṭṭappaṭṭatum, cutēca maṉṉarkaḷum celvantarkaḷum utakamaṇṭalattil periya periya māḷikaikaḷai eḻuppat toṭaṅkiṉar. ippōtu ūṭṭiyil ēṟakkuṟaiya 5000 vīṭukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. iv vīṭukaḷellām vaṇṇam pūcappaṭṭup pēraḻakuṭaṉ viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.

innakariṉ makkaḷ tokai perukikkoṇṭē iruppatālum, putiya putiya illaṅkaḷ tōṉṟik koṇṭē iruppatālum nakariṉ vacatikaḷaiyum perukka vēṇṭiya nilai ēṟpaṭṭatu. ki. pi. 1826-ām āṇṭu , mutal añcal maṉai {Post-office) tōṟṟu vikkappaṭṭatu. ki. pi. 1829-ām āṇṭu mutal maruttuvamaṉai kaṭṭappaṭṭatu. ki. pi. 1863-ām āṇṭu nakarāṭci maṉṟam ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. taṇṭalar aluvalakam ki. pi. 1866-ām āṇṭu niṟuvappaṭṭatu. 1878-ām āṇṭu 40,000 nūlkaḷaṭaṅkiya oru nūlakam ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. teṉṉintiyāviluḷḷa mikavum ciṟanta nūlakaṅkaḷil ituvum oṉṟākak karutappaṭukiṟatu. cevvāykkiḻamai tōṟum aṅkuc cantai kūṭukiṟatu. utakamaṇṭalattil vāḻum āṇkaḷum peṇkaḷum aṅkuk kūṭi, oru vārattiṟku vēṇṭiya poruḷkaḷai vāṅkic cellum kāṭci uṇmaiyilēyē uḷḷak kiḷarcciyūṭṭum taṉmaiyatu. 'cārmiṅ kirās' (Charming Cross) eṉpatu utakamaṇṭalattiṉ naṭuviṭam. ammuṉaiyil nāṉku puṟamum paravic coriyum ceyaṟkai nīr ūṟṟu (Flora Fountain) oṉṟu amaikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. kārkālattil avvūṟṟu kaḷinaṭam puriyum. avviṭattaic cuṟṟi aḻakiya kaṭaikaḷ varicaiyāka uḷḷaṉa.

makkaḷ tokai perukiyatum pala kaḻakaṅkaḷ tōṉṟat toṭaṅkiṉa. ki. pi. 1841-ām āṇṭu ūṭṭik kaḻakam (Ooty Club) tōṉṟiyatu. ataṉ uṟuppiṉarkaḷellām veḷḷaiyarē. tōṭṭa mutalāḷikaḷukkākavē ik kaḻakam amaikkappaṭṭatu. ki. pi. 1869-ām āṇṭu jimkāṉā kaḻakam (Gymkhana Club) tōṉṟiyatu. atu ippoḻutu ūṭṭi kuḻippantāṇṭak kaḻakam (Golf Club) eṉṟa peyar koṇṭu viḷaṅkukiṟatu. ūṭṭiyiluḷḷa vēṭṭaiyar kaḻakam (Hunt Club) ippoḻutu ciṟanta muṟaiyil uḷḷatu. ivaikaḷēyaṉṟi vēṟu cila kaḻakaṅkaḷum uḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟil kuṟippiṭat takkatu lāli niṟuvaṉam (Lawly Institute) eṉpatākum, kōṭaik kālattil maṭṭaip pantāṭṭap (Tennis Championship) pōṭṭiyai itu ciṟanta muṟaiyil naṭatti vaikkiṟatu.

kōṭaiyil intiya nāṭṭiṉ pala pakutikaḷiliruntum makkaḷ peruntiraḷāka iṅku vantu kūṭukiṉṟaṉar. eṉavē avarkaḷuṭaiya vacatikkākap pala ciṟanta uṇavu viṭutikaḷum, taṅkal maṉaikaḷum ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. kōṭaiyil taṅkuvataṟkeṉṟu vīṭukaḷ kuṭik kūlikku viṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.

ciṟappu :

nīlakiri malaiyiṉ parappu 900 catura mail ākum. itaṉmītu amaintuḷḷa kuṟiñci nakaramāṉa utakamaṇṭalam iyaṟkaiyaḻakil īṭu iṇaiyaṟṟatu. car ci. pi. irāmacāmi aiyar oru camayam, “ulakilēyē mikavum aḻakiya kuṟiñci nakaram utakamaṇṭalam" eṉṟu pārāṭṭip pēciṉār. 'kuṟiñci nakaraṅkaḷiṉ araci' (Queen of Hill stations) eṉṟa ciṟappup peyarukku utakamaṇṭalam takutiyuṭaiyatu. ekkālattum itu taṉ ciṟappuk kuṉṟāmal viḷaṅkukiṟatu. ēpral toṭaṅki jūṉ tiṅkaḷ muṭiyum varai itaippōl makiḻcciyum malarcciyum tāṇṭavamāṭum iṭam etaiyum intiya nāṭṭil kāṇa muṭiyātu. intiya nāṭṭiṉ ellāp pakutikaḷiliruntum, iṉpattai nāṭuvōr iṅku vantu kuvikiṉṟaṉar. viruntuṇṭum, vēṭṭaiyāṭiyum, āṭal nikaḻttiyum, kutiraip pantayattil paṅku koṇṭum, kuḻippantu maṭṭaip pantāṭṭap pōṭṭikaḷaik kaṇṭu kaḻittum, mīṉ piṭittum, malaikaḷil cuṟṟit tirintum, ēriyil tōṇiyūrntum, pacumpuṟṟaraiyil paṭuttup puraṇṭum, paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷaik kaṇṭu kaḷittum, kuṟiñciyaḻakil uḷḷattaip paṟikoṭuttum poḻutai iṉpamākak kaḻikkiṉṟaṉar. utakamaṇṭalattiṉ taṭpa veppanilai iḷaiñar, mutiyōr, nōyvāyppaṭṭōr ākiya ellārukkum ēṟṟatāka amaintuḷḷatu. innakaril kār kālam eṉṟu taṉiyāka oru kālam kiṭaiyātu. āṇṭu muḻuvatum ciṟu tūṟalkaḷāka maḻai tūṟikkoṇṭē irukkum. āṇṭukku 30 mutal 40 aṅkulam varai maḻai peykiṟatu. innakariṉ veppam 60°-kku mēl uyarvatumillai. 50°-kkuk kīḻk kuṟaivatum illai .

cikaraṅkaḷ :

mutalilēyē cikaraṅkaḷaip paṟṟip potuvākak kuṟippiṭṭēṉ. avaikaḷil mukkiyamāṉavaṟṟaiyum aḻakiyavaṟṟaiyum paṟṟic ciṟitu kuṟippiṭuvōm. utakamaṇṭalattaic cuṟṟit toṭṭapeṭṭā, elk, paṉivīḻcikaram, ḵperṉ, keyraṉ eṉṟa aḻakiya cikaraṅkaḷ vāṉaḷāvi niṟkiṉṟaṉa. toṭṭapeṭṭā : toṭṭapeṭṭā eṉṟāl perumalai eṉpatu poruḷ. itaṉuṭaiya uyaram 8,640 aṭi, nīlakirip pīṭapūmiyil amaintuḷḷa mika uyarnta cikaram itu tāṉ, iccikarattai eḷitil aṭaiyalām. mūṉṟil iraṇṭu paṅku tūrattai untuvaṇṭiyil kaṭantuviṭalām. mītippakutiyai naṭantē ēṟic cella vēṇṭum. utakamaṇṭalattiṉ naṭuviluḷḷa cārmiṅ kirāsiliruntu, tār pōṭappaṭṭa pātai kōtakirikkuc cellukiṟatu. appātaiyil nāṉku kal tolaivu ceṉṟu piṟaku 13-kkal malaimītu ēṟa vēṇṭum. mutal nāṉku kal tūramuḷḷa pātai vaḷaintu vaḷaintu cellukiṟatu. vaṇṭiyai ōṭṭic celluvōr mikavum eccarikkaiyōṭu cella vēṇṭum.

toṭṭapeṭṭā ucciyil vāṉāyvukkūṭamoṉṟu ki. pi. 1846-ām āṇṭu niṟuvappaṭṭatu. āṉāl ki. pi. 1859-il utakamaṇṭalattiṟku oṉpatu kal tolaivil uḷḷa irāṇuvattāriṉ taṅkal iṭamāṉa velliṅṭaṉukku itu māṟṟappaṭṭatu. cila āṇṭukaḷ kaḻittu maṟupaṭiyum toṭṭapeṭṭāviṟkē māṟṟappaṭṭatu. āṉāl itu ippoḻutu payaṉpaṭum nilaiyil illai. toṭṭapeṭṭāviṉ ucciyiliruntu kāṇpōr kaṇkaḷukku utakamaṇṭalattiṉ muḻuk kāṭciyum pēraḻakōṭu teṉpaṭum. atōṭu kōvai māvaṭṭattiluḷḷa ellā malaikaḷum teṉpaṭum.

paṉivīḻ cikaram :

toṭṭapeṭṭāviṟku aṭuttāṟpōl mikavum uyariya cikaram paṉivīḻcikaram (Snow down} eṉpatākum. itaṉ uyaram 8,299 aṭi. toṭṭapeṭṭāvaippōl eḷitāka itaṉmītu ēṟa muṭiyātu. ēṟuvataṟkut tollaiyāka iruntālum, uyarnta maraṅkaḷaṭarnta vaḻikaḷūṭē ēṟic cellumpōtu uḷḷattiṟku iṉpamāka irukkum. speṉcar malai mītu amaintuḷḷa ceyiṇṭ sṭīḵpaṉ carcciṟkup piṉṉāl irukkum merlimaṇṭ pātaiyil iraṇṭu kal tolaivu ceṉṟāl, ivvucciyai aṭaiyalām. ivvaḻiyum maraṅkaḷaṭarntatu.



elk cikaram :

elk cikaram 8000 aṭikaḷukku mēl uyaramuḷḷatu. itaṉmītu pala kōyilkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. ivvucciyiṉ pakkattil ōr aḻakiya kōyil kuṭaiyappaṭṭuḷḷatu. aṅkuḷḷa pūcāri mālaitōṟum oru viḷakkēṟṟi vaikkiṟār. ūṭṭiyiṉ eppakutiyiliruntu pārttālum avviḷakku naṉṟākat teriyum.

keyraṉ cikaram :

ellāvaṟṟaiyumviṭa mikavum aḻakiyatu keyraṉ cikaram (Cairn Hill). maṟṟa ellāc cikaraṅkaḷaiyum viṭa itu maraṅkaḷaṭarntatu. nalla vēḷaiyāka, maṟṟa cikaraṅkaḷil maraṅkaḷ aḻikkappaṭṭāṟpōl itil aḻikkappaṭavillai . itaṉ uyaram 6,583 aṭi. paccaik kampaḷam parappiṉāṟ pōṉṟa pulveḷikaḷ iccikarattil eṅkum paravik kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa. iṅkuḷḷa maraṅkaḷiṉ aṭiyil mēṭaiyiṭṭāṟpōl pāci paṭarntuḷḷatu. ataṉmītu ilakkiya uṇarvuḷḷa yārēṉum taṉimaiyil amarntiruntārkaḷāṉāl, jōkaṉ sṭrās (Johan Strause) eṉṟa mēlai nāṭṭuk kaviñaṉiṉ uḷḷattil, amaratvam peṟṟa icaip pāṭal uruvākumāṟu tūṇṭiya, viyaṉṉāviṉ iḷamarakkāṭukaḷ niṉaivil tōṉṟum, keyraṉ cikaram utakaiyiliruntu caṟṟuttolaivil uḷḷatu. nakarattiliruntu puṟappaṭṭu, ēriyaic cuṟṟic cellum pukaivaṇṭip pātaiyōramākac ceṉṟōmāṉāl, tūṇkaḷ pōl uyarntu niṟkum pāṟaikaḷaik kāṇalām. avviṭattiliruntu oru kal tolaivu mikavum mōcamāṉa pātai amaintuḷḷatu, ataik kaṭantuviṭṭāl, keyraṉ malaiyiṉ iḷamarak kāṭṭiṟkuḷ nuḻaiyalām.



mūkkaṟutti cikaram :

mūkkaṟutti cikaram (Mukerti Peak) tōṭarkaḷāl puṉitamāṉatākak karutappaṭukiṟatu. iccikarattiṟku appāl cuvarkkattiṉ vāyil iruppatāka avarkaḷ karutukiṟārkaḷ. ic cikarattōṭu toṭarpuṭaiya iraṇṭu kataikaḷ tōṭarkaḷiṭaiyē vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. tōṭarkaḷiṭaiyē peṇ kuḻantaikaḷaik kolai ceyyum paḻakkam iruntatākak kuṟippiṭṭōm. kolai ceyya vēṇṭiya kuḻantaikaḷai iṅkuk koṇarntu eṟintu koṉṟu viṭuvatuṇṭām, ākaiyiṉāl entap peṇṇaiyum tōṭarkaḷ iccikarattiṉ pakkamākac cella viṭuvatillai. ik kaṭṭuppāṭṭai mīṟi oru peṇ iṅku vantuviṭṭāḷām. itaiyaṟinta tōṭarkaḷ, ap peṇṇiṉ mūkkai aṟuttut taṇṭittārkaḷām. taṇṭaṉai peṟṟa ap peṇ eppaṭiyō immalaic cikarattai aṭaintu maṟaintuviṭṭaṭāḷām. iṉṟukūṭa ap peṇṇai oru ciṟu teyvamākat tōṭarkaḷ vaṇaṅku kiṉṟaṉar.

ic cikarattōṭu toṭarpuṭaiya maṟṟoru katai irāvaṇaṉaip paṟṟiyatākum. tōṭarkaḷ irāvaṇaṉukku mariyātai kāṭṭāmal, irāmaṉiṭattil atika aṉpu celuttiṉārkaḷām. ataṉāl ciṉaṅkoṇṭa ilaṅkai maṉṉaṉ oru kai maṇṇai eṭuttuk kāṟṟil vīciṉāṉām. ammaṇ koṭiya kirumikaḷāka māṟit tōṭarkaḷuṭaiya kālnaṭaikaḷaiyum, vīṭukaḷaiyum pīṭittut tuṉpuṟuttiyatōṭu, avarkaḷaiyum nōy koḷḷumāṟu ceytu koṭumai purintatām. iṉṟukūṭa akkirumikaḷāl tuṉpuṟuvatākat tōṭarkaḷ nampukiṟārkaḷ. itai uṇarnta irāmaṉ icceyalukkup paḻivāṅka eṇṇi irāvaṇaṉ taṅkaiyāṉa cūrppanakaiyiṉ mūkkai aṟuttu, ellārukkum teriyumpaṭi ic cikarattil patittu vaittāṉām. ik kāraṇaṅkaḷālēyē iccikaram ip peyar peṟṟatākat tōṭarkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.

kuṉṟu veḷikaḷ :

utakamaṇṭalattaic cuṟṟip paranta kuṉṟu veḷikaḷ (downs) amaintuḷḷaṉa. vēṟu entak kuṟiñci nakarattilum iṅkiruppaṉa pōṉṟu paranta veḷikaḷaik kāṇa muṭiyātu. untuvaṇṭikaḷil ēṟi ullācamākac cuṟṟit tiriya virumpuvōr, iṇaiyaṟṟa iṉpam kāṇa ivveḷikaḷ peruntuṇai purikiṉṟaṉa. utakamaṇṭalattaic cuṟṟiyuḷḷa pātaikaḷ amaippilum, aḻakilum īṭaṟṟavai, vēṟeṅkum ittakaiya malaivaḻip pātaikaḷaik kāṇa muṭiyātu. makāpalēcuvaram (Mahabaleshwar), māteraṉ (Matheran) ākiya nakaraṅkaḷil iruppatupōl, utakaiyaic cuṟṟi uyarnta malaikaḷum, kuṟukiya paḷḷattākkukaḷum kiṭaiyā. maicūriliruntu kūṭalūr vaḻiyāka utakai cellum pātai. aḻakiya veḷikaḷiṭaiyē pukuntu cellukiṟatu. ivvaḻic celvōr eytum iṉpam eṭuttiyampaṟpālatō?

ivvaḻi tāḻnta kuṉṟukaḷil ēṟi iṟaṅkic cellukiṟatu. aṅkup paravik kāṇappaṭum pacum pulveḷikaḷ, iṅkilāntu nāṭṭil mikka aḻakōṭu viḷaṅkum, yārkṣair pulveḷikaḷai (Yorkshire Moors) niṉaivūṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivviṭam kuḻip pantāṭṭattiṟku mikavum ēṟṟa iṭamāka viḷaṅkukiṟatu. iṅkuḷḷa kuḻippantāṭṭa maitāṉam (Golf Course) intiya nāṭṭilēyē mikavum aḻakiyatu. ivveḷikaḷil amaintuḷḷa iḷamarakkāṭukaḷil cempuli (leopards) kaḷum, ciṟuttai (Panthers) kaḷum, varaiyāṭukaḷum niṟaiya uḷḷaṉa. ivaikaḷai vēṭṭaiyāṭuvatu, utakai vāḻ makkaḷukkuc ciṟanta poḻutu pōkkāka uḷḷatu. āṇṭutōṟum cepṭampar tiṅkaḷiṉpōtu ivviṭaṅkaḷil eṅkup pārttālum pōṭṭip pantayaṅkaḷ naṭaipeṟum. aṭuttaṭuttu varum kuṉṟukaḷiṭaiyē, vaḷaintu vaḷaintu cellum pātaikaḷil kavalaiyiṉṟi naṭantu tirivatum īṭaṟṟa iṉpamē.

tiru. veṉlāk pirapu (Lord Wenlock) ceṉṉai mānila āḷunarākap paṇiyāṟṟiya pōtu, ivveḷikaḷiṉ muṉṉēṟṟattiṟkup peritum pāṭupaṭṭār. ākaiyiṉālēyē ivveḷikaḷ avaruṭaiya peyarai muṉṉē koṇṭu 'veṉlāk veḷikaḷ' (Wenlock Downs) eṉṟu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. utakamaṇṭalattiluḷḷa ellā mukkiya uṇavu viṭutikaḷiliruntum iv veḷikaḷai eḷitil ceṉṟaṭaiyalām. uṇavu viṭutikaḷiliruntu puṟappaṭṭu 1 allatu 1½kkal, vesṭpari pātaiyil cella vēṇṭum. utakaiyiliruntu kiḷampuvōr pātaiyai aṭaintu ḵpiṅkar pōsṭ (Finger Post) ṭai nōkkic ceṉṟāl ivveḷikaḷaic cīkkiram aṭaiyalām, kai viralkaḷai virittu vaittāṟpōl aintu pātaikaḷ pirintu celvatāl ivviṭam ḵpiṅkar pōsṭ eṉṟu peyar peṟṟatu.

ēri :

utaka maṇṭalattil amaintuḷḷa ēri ciṟiyatu. itu ceyaṟkai ēri eṉpatai ellārum aṟivar. āṉāl itaik kāṇpōr iyaṟkaiyāka amaintuḷḷa eḻil mikka ēri eṉṟē eṇṇuvar. itaṉ cuṟṟaḷavu iraṇṭu kal irukkum. mutaṉ mutalāka ivvēriyiṉ muṉṉēṟṟattil akkaṟai kāṭṭiyavar tiruvāḷar callivaṉ turai. ivvēriyiṉ nīraip payirt toḻilukkup payaṉpaṭutta mutalil ellārum eṇṇiṉar. ciṟitu cīrtiruttappaṭṭatum itu kaṇ kavarum vaṉappōṭu kāṭciyaḷittatāl, aḻakukkākavē itaip payaṉ paṭuttap piṉṉar muṭivu ceytaṉar. ki. pi. 1823-ām āṇṭiliruntu, itu pala māṟutalkaḷukkuṭpaṭṭup pala vaḻikaḷilum muṉṉēṟiyatu. ki. pi. 1896-liruntu 1899 varaiyil rū.20,000 itaṟkuc celaviṭappaṭṭatu. ippōtu ivvēriyaic cuṟṟi maraṅkaḷaṭarnta kāṭu oṉṟuḷḷatu. amaitiyāṉa aḻaku avviṭattil kuṭi koṇṭirukkiṟatu. utakai makkaḷ ivvēriyil tōṇiyūrntum, mīṉ piṭittum iṉpamākap poḻutaik kaḻikkiṉṟaṉar. ēriyiṉ etirk karaiyil perṉ malai toṭaṅkukiṟatu. ēriyaic cuṟṟi naṭantu ceṉṟāl, nam uḷḷattiṟkum uṭalukkum oru vitap puttuṇarcci ēṟpaṭukiṟatu. ēriyiṉ karaiyōramākak kaṭaivītiyai nōkki naṭantu ceṉṟāl hōparṭ pūṅkā (Hobert Park) viṉ ellaiyai aṭaiyalām. avviṭattil kutiraip pantaya maitāṉamum pārvaiyāḷar iruppiṭamum (Pevilian) amaintuḷḷaṉa. kutiraip pantaya vīrarkaḷaiyum, poḻutu pōkkiṟkākak kutirai mītum, kōvēṟu kaḻutai mītum cavāri ceyvōraiyum aṅkuk kāṇalām. akkāṭci mikavum vēṭikkaiyāṉatāka irukkum.

iṉpac celaviṟkuriya iṭaṅkaḷ (Excursions)

avalañci :

utakamaṇṭalattaic cuṟṟi iṉpamākap poḻutu pōkkaṟkuriya pala iṭaṅkaḷum, kaṇṭu makiḻtaṟkuriya pala iṭaṅkaḷum uḷḷaṉa. keyraṉ malaiyil uḷḷa kāṭukaḷiṉ orupakuti paṉippaṭalattil maṟaintu tōṉṟum, utaka maṇṭalattiliruntu 15-āvatu kallil ikkāṭci amaintuḷḷatu. ivviṭattiṟku 'avalañci' eṉṟu peyar. avalañci (Avalanche) eṉṟa āṅkilac collukku, 'iḻintu varum paṉikkaṭṭi' eṉṟu peyar. ki. pi. 1823-ām āṇṭu, paṉiveḷḷam ivviṭattil ōṭivanta kāraṇat tāl itu ippeyar koṇṭu viḷaṅkukiṟatu.

utakamaṇṭalattiliruntu ivviṭattiṟkup cellum vaḻi mikavum mōcamāṉatu. āṉāl ippātaiyiṉ vaḷaivukaḷil ceṟintu tōṉṟum iyaṟkai vaḷam, kāṇpōr uḷḷattai viyappilum makiḻcciyilum āḻttum. ivvaḻiyil pala ciṟṟūrkaḷ amaintuḷḷaṉa. acciṟṟūrkaḷaik kaṭantatum vaḻi iraṇṭākap pirintu celkiṟatu. oruvaḻi emarālṭu paḷḷattākki (Emerald Valley)ṟkuc cellukiṟatu. appaḷḷattākkil irāṇuvattiṉar taṅki, mātirip pōr (Mock battle) payilukiṉṟaṉar. maṟṟōr vaḻiyil aintu kal ceṉṟāl avalañciyai aṭaiyalām. utakamaṇṭalattiliruntu 14 āvatu kallil avalañci āṟu ōṭukiṟatu. iyaṟkaivaḷam ceṟinta accarivil, paḷiṅku pōl cuḻittu ōṭum avvāṟṟiṉ aḻakaic coṟkaḷāl kūṟamuṭiyātu. avviṭattil amaintirukkum pirayāṇikaḷiṉ taṅkal maṉai (Travellers' Bangalow)yiliruntu oru ciṟiya vaḻi mēlē cellukiṟatu. avvaḻiyāka mēlēṟi nōkkiṉāl orē aḷavāṉa uyarattōṭu 8300 aṭi nimirntu niṟkum cikaraṅkaḷaik kāṇalām.

mīṉ paṇṇai :

pirayāṇikaḷ taṅkal maṉaiyiliruntu orukal tolaivil oru periya mīṉ paṇṇai (Trout Hatchery) amaintuḷḷatu. ittakaiya paṇṇai nīlakiriyiṉ mītu eṅkum kiṭaiyātu. 'iṅku vaḷarkkappaṭumpaṭiyāṉa mīṉiṉ peyar 'ṭiravuṭ' eṉappaṭum. itu 'cāl maṉ' (Salmon) eṉṟa mīṉiṉattaic cārntatu. āṉāl, ataiviṭa uruvattil ciṟiyatu. ippaṇṇaiyil aintu periya kuḷaṅkaḷil ōrāṇṭiliruntu mūṉṟāṇṭuvarai vayatuṭaiya mīṉkaḷ vaḷarkkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. navampar mutal ḵpepruvari varaiyilum uḷḷa kālamē immīṉiṉ vaḷarccikku ēṟṟa paruvamākum. 10,000 mīṉkaḷ ippaṇṇaiyil vaḷarkkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. immīṉiṉ muṭṭaikaḷ 29 nāṭkaḷil kaṇ viḻikkiṉṟaṉa,

piṟa iṭaṅkaḷ :

ippaṇṇaik karukiluḷḷa kāṭu uḷ nuḻaintu kāṇpataṟku ēṟṟatu. aḻakiya oru ciṟupātai kāṭṭiṉ uṭpakutiyil cellukiṟatu. appātaiyil 9 kal tolaivu ceṉṟāl kāṭṭilākāt talaivariṉ māḷikai uḷḷatu. avviṭattiṟkup paṇṭakapal (Bantakapal) eṉṟu peyar.

utakamaṇṭalattiliruntu avalañci cellum vaḻiyil amaintuḷḷa mūkkaṟutti, paikkārā cikaraṅkaḷ kuṟippiṭattakkavai. utakai ceṉṟuviṭṭu ivviraṇṭaiyum kāṇāmal yārum varamāṭṭārkaḷ. iccikaraṅkaḷ amaintuḷḷa iṭaṅkaḷil pala āṟukaḷum, aṇaikaḷum, ērikaḷum pēraḻakuṭaṉ kāṭci aḷikkiṉṟaṉa.

utakamaṇṭalattiliruntu patiṉōrāvatu kallil amaintuḷḷa paikkārā āṟṟiṉ pālattaik kaṭantu ceṉṟōmāṉāl, ōr aṇai kaṭṭik koṇṭiruppataik kāṇalām. ivvaṇai paikkārā aṇaikkaṭṭiṉ toṭarcci. itaṉ uyaram 150 aṭi.

ivvaḻiyilēyē mēlum aintu kalceṉṟu iṭatu puṟamākat tirumpiṉāl ōr aḻakiya pātai cellukiṟatu. avvaḻiyiṉ ovvoru tiruppattilum calacalattu ōṭum nīraruviyaiyō, pacuṅ kuṉṟaiyē. allatu ciṟu ciṟu kuṭicaikaḷaiyō kāṇalām. kkap kāṭukaḷil nuḻaintāl tōṭariṉ vāḻviṭaṅkaḷaik kāṇalām.

mūkkaṟuttiyil amaintirukkum taṅkal māḷikai (Dak Bangalow) aḻakiyatākavum, vacatiyāṉa tākavum amaintuḷḷatu. itu aṇaik kaṭṭiliruntu orukal tolaivil amaintuḷḷatu. ivvaṇai ki. pi. 1835il iruntu ki. pi. 1938 varai kaṭṭappaṭṭatu. itu uruvattāl ciṟiyatu. āṉāl, itil iraṇṭu etirolikaḷ tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa. mutal etiroli toṭaṅki arai nimiṭam kaḻittu iraṇṭāvatu etiroli tōṉṟukiṟatu. iṅkuḷḷa ēri pārppataṟkuc ciṟitākat tōṉṟum. āṉāl itu mūkkaṟutti malaiyaic cuṟṟip paraviyuḷḷatu. ivvēriyiṉ nāṉku pakkaṅkaḷilum niṟaiyappēr mīṉpiṭittup poḻutaik kaḻikkiṉṟaṉar. ivvēriyil tōṇiyūrntu ceṉṟē mūkkaṟuttiyiṉ aṭivārattai aṭaiya muṭiyum. aṭi vārattiliruntu accikaram mikavum ceṅkuttāka uyarntu cellukiṟatu. utakamaṇṭalattiliruntu pārppōrukku avvucci teḷivākat teriyum. iv vēriyaic cuṟṟiyirukkum karaṭumuraṭāṉa pātai 12 kal nīḷamuṭaiyatu. ippātaiyil naṭantu ēriyai valam varutal peruveṟṟikkuriya ceyalākum.

kavarṉars ṣōlā {Governor's Shola) eṉṟa iḷamarakkāṭu kuṟippiṭattakka aḻakuvāyntatu. akkāṭṭiṉ iṭaiyē niḻalaṭarnta maraṅkaḷiṉaṭiyil pukuntu cellum 5 kal nīḷamuḷḷa pātai, iṅku vāḻnta mānila āḷunarkaḷukku mikavum piṭittamāṉatu. kaṉṉamarā pātai eṉṟa maṟṟoru vaḻi 10 kal nīḷamuḷḷatu. kuṭai maraṅka (Umbrella trees) ḷiṭaiyē ippātai pukuntu cellum kāṭci pēraḻaku āppatu. ippātaiyiliruntu orukal tolaivu ceṉṟāl iṉpa viruntu (Picnic) uṇpataṟkāṉa aḻakiya iṭam oṉṟuḷḷatu. avviṭattiliruntu mēkamūṭṭamaṟṟa nāḷil nōkkiṉāl, maicūr nakaram naṉṟākat teriyum. kaṉṉamarāp pātaiyiṉ muṭivil ceṅkuttāṉa cīkūr malai (Sigur ghat) toṭaṅkukiṟatu. attoṭar 3000 aṭikkuk kīḻ aṭarnta kāṭukaḷiṭaiyē amaintirukkum kālkaṭṭi nīr vīḻcci (Kalhatty falls) varai cellukiṟatu.

araciyalār payirt tōṭṭam :

utakamaṇṭalattil amaintuḷḷa aracāṅkap payirt tōṭṭam (The Botanical Gardens), in nakara varalāṟṟil kuṟippiṭattakka oṉṟākum. ki. pi. 1850 ām āṇṭu tiruvāḷar makkalvar (Mr. Mclver} eṉpavarāl ippayirt tōṭṭam niṟuvappaṭṭatu. mutalil itu 'kiyū tōṭṭam' (Kew Gardens) eṉṟa peyar koṇṭu viḷaṅkiyatu. ki.pi. 1950-ām āṇṭu it tōṭṭattiṉ nūṟṟāṇṭu viḻā mikavum ciṟanta muṟaiyil koṇṭāṭappaṭṭatu. mutaṉ mutalāka itu kāykaṟi payiriṭuvataṟkāka niṟuvappaṭṭatu. āṉāl itu ippoḻutu tāvarappayir ārāyccikkuriya iṭamāka viḷaṅkukiṟatu. iṅkut tāvara ārāyccik kaḻakam (Harticultural Society) ki. pi. 1847-ām āṇṭu potu makkaḷiṉ naṉkoṭaiyaik koṇṭu tuvakkappaṭṭatu. ki. pi. 1855-ām āṇṭu ittōṭṭam araciyalār kaikku māṟiyatu. piṟaku itu palavita māṟutalkaḷaiyum, viyakkattakka muṉṉēṟṟaṅkaḷaiyum mēṟkoṇṭatu. taṟpōtu itu pala aṭukkukaḷāka amaikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. payaṉuḷḷa ceṭikaḷum, palavita malarc ceṭikaḷum ulakattiṉ pala pakutikaḷiliruntum koṇarappaṭṭu iṅkup payiriṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ippōtu ēṟakkuṟaiya 650 vakaiyāṉa ceṭi koṭikaḷum, maraṅkaḷum iṅku uḷḷaṉa. aḻakukkākavē vaḷarkkappaṭum maraṅkaḷ, muṭceṭikaḷ, āṇṭu muḻutum payaṉ tarattakka maraṅkaḷ, kiḻaṅkuc ceṭikaḷ, vēli amaippataṟkup payaṉpaṭum paṭiyāṉa ceṭikaḷ, mūlikaic ceṭikaḷ, nīrc ceṭikaḷ, maṇapporuḷ ceyyappayaṉpaṭum ceṭikaḷ, aḻaku vaḷaivukaḷil paṭaraviṭum koṭikaḷ, rōjāc ceṭikaḷ eṉa aḷavaṟṟa tāvara vakaikaḷ iṅku vaḷarkkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.

pūk kaṇkāṭci :

āṇṭu tōṟum mēt tiṅkaḷil iṅku naṭai peṟum pūk kaṇkāṭci (Flower Show) mikavum kuṟippiṭattakkatu. ak kaṇkāṭciyil pūvōṭu kāykaṟi, paḻam mutaliyaṉavum vaikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa, nīlakiri malaiyiṉ ellāp pakutikaḷiliruntum, camaveḷiyiliruntum makkaḷ akkaṇkāṭciyil vantu kūṭukiṉṟaṉar. nākarika makkaḷum, paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷum oṉṟucēra ivviḻā peritum tuṇaipurikiṉṟatu. nākarikar paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷaiyum, paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷ nākarikaraiyum aṅkānta vāyōṭu pārttu viyappaṭaivar. pūk kaṇkāṭci naṭaipeṟum an nāḷilēyē paṟavaikaḷiṉ kaṇkāṭci (Poultry Show) oṉṟum naṭaipeṟum. nāyk kaṇkāṭcikkāka oru nāḷ appōtu otukkappaṭukiṟatu. utakamaṇṭalattil pala nāy vaḷarppu nilaiyaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa: avviṭaṅkaḷiliruntum veḷiyūrkaḷiliruntum kāṭcikkāka niṟaiya nāykaḷ aṅku vantu kūṭum. uyarnta iṉa nāykaḷil ciṟappāṉavaṟṟaiyellām aṅkuk kāṇalām. pūṉaikkuṭṭi aḷaviliruntu kaṉṟuk kuṭṭiyiṉ aḷavu varaiyuḷḷa nāykaḷ aṅku aṇi aṇiyāka irukkum.

vaṉa vilaṅkukaḷaik kāṇa virumpuvōr, kūṭalūrukku appāl uḷḷa kāṭukaḷukkuc cellukiṉṟaṉar. ūṭṭiyiliruntu nāṟpatu kallukku appāl tāṉ vaṉavilaṅkukaḷaik kāṇa iyalum. mālaiyil puṟappaṭṭu, cūriyaṉ malaivāyil viḻuntatum yāṉai mēl ēṟik kāṭṭiṟkuḷ nuḻaintāl, kāṭṭu vilaṅkukaḷiṉ kūṭṭattaik kāṇalām. puli, kāṭṭerumai, māṉiṉam ākiyavai etirppaṭum.

paḷḷikaḷ :

utakamaṇṭalattil māṇavarkaḷ taṅkip payiluvataṟkēṟṟa nāṉku paḷḷikaḷ (boarding schools) uḷḷaṉa. pirīks niṉaivuppaḷḷi (Breek's Memorial School), tiruccapai makaḷir uyar nilaippaḷḷi (High School for Sisters of the Church), nācarēttu makaḷir paḷḷi (The Nazareth Convent), lāraṉs paḷḷi (The Lawrence School) eṉpaṉa avai. mēṟkūṟiya nāṉku paḷḷikaḷil mikavum ciṟappāṉatu vāraṉs paḷḷi. itu utakamaṇṭalattiliruntu nāṉku kal tolaivil lavṭēl (Lovedale) eṉṟa iṭattil amaintuḷḷatu. 'lavṭēl' eṉṟāl aṉpup paḷḷattākku' eṉṟu peyar. aṉpāl piṇikkappaṭṭup palkalai payilum piñcuḷḷaṅkaḷiṉ kalaik kūṭam amaintirukkumiṭattiṟku ippeyar mikavum poruttamē. ivviṭattiṉ taṭpa veppa nilai uṭal nalattiṟku mikavum ēṟṟatāka amaintuḷḷatu.

ippaḷḷi ki. pi. 1858-ām āṇṭu cepṭampar tiṅkaḷ 6-ām nāḷ tuvakkappaṭṭatu. appōtu intiya nāṭṭup paṭaiyiṉ taḷapati (Major General) yāka viḷaṅkiya car heṉṟi lāraṉs eṉpāriṉ peyar koṇṭu ippaḷḷi viḷaṅkukiṟatu. intiyap paṭaiyil paṇiyāṟṟiya paṭaivīrarkaḷ, ōyvupeṟṟa paṭaivīrarkaḷ, ākiyōriṉ kuḻantaikaḷ ciṟanta muṟaiyil kalvi payiluvataṟkāka, nīlakiri malaiyil uyariya oru paḷḷiyai niṟuva vēṇṭum eṉṟa karuttai mutaṉ mutalāka veḷiyiṭṭavar avarē. ki. pi. 1949-ām āṇṭu varaiyil, paṭaiyiṉar kuḻantaikaḷ payilum paḷḷiyākavē itu iruntuvantatu. avvāṇṭu intiya araciyalār ippaḷḷiyiṉ poṟuppai ēṟṟukkoṇṭatum, ellāruṭaiya kuḻantaikaḷum payilum potuppaḷḷiyāka itu māṟṟappaṭṭuviṭṭatu.

ki. pi. 1949-kku muṉ āṅkilēyarukkē ēkapōka urimai peṟṟirunta ippaḷḷi, ippōtu intiya nāṭṭuc celvarkaḷukkē urimaiyuṭaiyatāka irukkiṟatu. iṅkilāntu nāṭṭil īṭaṉ {Eton) eṉṟa iṭattil uḷḷa paḷḷi, araciḷaṅkumararkaḷum, pirapukkaḷiṉ kuḻantaikaḷum, māperum ālai mutalāḷikaḷiṉ makkaḷum payilum paḷḷiyāka viḷaṅkukiṟatu. appaḷḷi, varalāṟṟup pukaḻpeṟṟatu ; pala nūṟṟāṇṭu vaḷarcciyuṭaiyatu; pala aṟiñarkaḷaiyum, eḻuttāḷarkaḷaiyum, kaviñarkaḷaiyum uruvākkiyatu; pala ilakkiya āciriyarkaḷāl eṭuttuk kaiyāḷappaṭṭatu. attakaiya oru paḷḷi intiya nāṭṭil irukkiṟa teṉṟāl, atu lāreṉs paḷḷiyākattāṉ irukka muṭiyum. 'intiya nāṭṭiṉ īṭaṉ' eṉṟu itai aḻaittālum takum. āṇṭu oṉṟukku ēṟakkuṟaiya rū. 4000 iṅku payilum ovvoru māṇavaṉukkum celavākiṉṟaṉa. ippaḷḷiyil iṭam piṭippateṉpatu, avvaḷavu eḷitāṉa kāriyamalla. māṇavarkaḷaic cērttuk koḷḷum atikāram ippaḷḷiyiṉ ceyaṟkuḻu (The Board of Administration) viṟkē uriyatu. intiya nāṭṭuk kalvit tuṟaic ceyalāḷarē (The Secretary to the Government of India, Ministry of Education) ic ceyaṟkuḻuviṉ talaivar. ippaḷḷi amaintirukkum iṭattiṉ mottap parappu 700 ēkar. itu amaintirukkum iṭattiṉ uyaram kaṭal maṭṭattiliruntu 7200 aṭi. ippaḷḷiyiṉ naṭuvil aḻakiya ēri oṉṟum amaintuḷḷatu.



itu taṅkip payilum paḷḷi. itil 550 māṇavarkaḷē taṅkip payila iṭamuṇṭu. 5 vayatu mutal 9 vayatu varai uḷḷa iḷamāṇavar pirivu (Preparatory School), āṇkaḷ pirivu, peṇkaḷ pirivu eṉa ippaḷḷi muppirivukaḷaik koṇṭatu. im muppiriviṉarkkum taṅkumiṭaṅkaḷ taṉittaṉiyē amaikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉavēyaṉṟi, āṇkaḷum peṇkaḷum oṉṟākavē payilkiṉṟaṉar. kuṭumpakkalai (Demestic Science) mutaliya peṇkaḷuk kurittāṉa kalaikaḷum, poḻutu pōkkuk kalaikaḷum taṉiyāka avarkaḷukkuk kaṟṟukkoṭukkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ovvoru viṭutiyum oru kuṭumpam pōṉṟa muṟai (House ---System)yilēyē naṭattappaṭukiṟatu. iṅku mikavum ciṟanta āṅkilak kalvi payiṟṟappaṭukiṟatu. āṅkilattiṟ kaṭuttāṟpōl, inti kaṭṭāya pāṭamākak kaṟpikkappaṭukiṟatu. iṅku mēlai nāṭṭu icaikkalaiyum, intiya icaikkalaiyum kaṟṟukkoṭukkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. icaik kalaiyaip pāṭamāka eṭuttukkoṇṭa ellā māṇavarkaḷum piyāṉō, vayaliṉ ākiya icaik karuvikaḷai icaikkak kaṟṟukkoṭukkappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. mēlai nāṭṭu icaiyil nalla tērcciyum, ārvamumuḷḷa māṇavarkaḷ ilaṇṭaṉ ṭiriṉiṭi kallūri icaittērvukku aṉuppappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. maṭṭaip pantu (Tennis), tuṭuppup pantu, (Cricket), kūṭaip pantu (Basket ball), kālpantu (Foot ball), kaip pantu (Volley ball), valaippantu(Net ball), taṭippantu (Hockey) mutaliya viḷaiyāṭṭukkaḷ iṅkuk kaṟṟuk koṭukkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. aṉupavam vāynta ōrāciriyar kutirai ēṟṟamum māṇavarkaṭkuk kaṟṟukkoṭukkiṟār. āṉāl, ataṟkut tiṅkaḷ tōṟum rū. 20 taṉik kaṭṭaṇamākac celuttavēṇṭum. camayak kalviyum iṅkup payiṟṟappaṭukiṟatu.



māṇavarkaḷuṭaiya uṭal nalattai nalla muṟaiyil kaṇkāṇikka oru maruttuva maṉaiyum uḷḷatu. ammaṉaiyil payiṟci peṟṟa oru maruttuvar iruntu paṇipurikiṟār. 55 paṭukkaikaḷ ammaruttuva maṉaiyil uḷḷaṉa. ovvoru kuḻantaiyiṉ vaḷarcciyum karuttōṭu kavaṉikkappaṭukiṟatu. māṇavarkaḷiṉ eṭai, uyaram mutaliyaṉa yāvum tiṅkaḷtōṟum kaṇakkiṭappaṭṭup pativu ceyyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. nāḷ tōṟum cōtaṉaiyiṭappaṭṭa cīriya uṇavē ellārukkum aḷikkappaṭukiṟatu. maruttuvamaṉaiyil oru māṇavaṉ 24 maṇi nērattiṟkumēl taṅkiyirukka nēriṭṭāl, ac ceytiyaip paḷḷi nirvākikaḷ peṟṟōrukku aṟivittu viṭukiṉṟaṉar. maruttuva maṉaiyil cikiccai peṟum māṇavaṉiṉ uṭal nala muṉṉēṟṟam, vārantōṟum peṟṟōrkaḷukku aṟivikkappaṭum. māṇavaṉiṉ uṭal nalattaip paṟṟi viṉavum peṟṟōrukku uṭaṉukkuṭaṉē muṭaṅkal mūlam akkaṟaiyōṭu nilaimai aṟivikkappaṭum. mikavum nōyvāyppaṭṭa māṇavarkaḷ utakamaṇṭalattiluḷḷa puṉita pārttalōmi maruttuvamaṉai {St. Bartholomews Hospital) yil cērkkappaṭuvar. appōtu ēṟpaṭum celaviṉattaip peṟṟōrkaḷ ēṟṟukkoḷḷa vēṇṭum. paḷḷi maruttuvamaṉaiyil ceyyum cikiccaikkup paṇam kiṭaiyātu. eks-rē paṭam piṭippataṟkum, aṟuvai maruttuvattiṟkum, ṭāṉikkukaḷukkum, vilaiyuyarnta maruntukaḷukkumāṉa paṇattaip peṟṟōrkaḷ aḷikka vēṇṭum. nīlakiri māvaṭṭa maruttuva atikāri, (District Medical Officer) ip paḷḷiyiṉ maruttuva ālōcakarāka eppoḻutum iruppār. ip paḷḷikkeṉṟu taṉippaṭṭa pal maruttuvar (Dental Surgeon) oruvar irukkiṟār. māṇavarkaḷuṭaiya paṟkaḷiṉ nalam naṉku kavaṉikkappaṭukiṟatu. itaṟkeṉṟu āṇṭutōṟum rū. 5 kaṭṭaṇamākap peṟappaṭukiṟatu.



pattu vayatiṟkuṭpaṭṭa māṇavarkaḷiṉ piṟanta nāḷ viruntum iṅku naṭattappaṭukiṟatu. yārāvatu oru māṇavaṉiṉ peṟṟōr tam kuḻantaiyiṉ piṟanta nāḷ viḻāvaik koṇṭāṭa virumpiṉāl, piṟanta nāḷ varuvataṟkup patiṉaintu nāṭkaḷukkumuṉ oru viṇṇappattōṭu rū. 10-m aṉuppi vaikka vēṇṭum. vārattiṟku oru muṟaiyāvatu peṟṟōrukku muṭaṅkal eḻuta vēṇṭumeṉpatu paḷḷiyiṉ caṭṭam, muṭaṅkal eḻuta muṭiyāta tuvakkappaḷḷi māṇavarkaḷukkāka vakuppu āciriyarkaḷē iraṇṭu vāraṅkaḷukkoru muṟai eḻutukiṉṟaṉar. muṭaṅkal tavaṟiviṭṭāl, ataip paṟṟiya ceytiyaip peṟṟōrkaḷ paḷḷi . nirvākikaḷukkut teriyappaṭutta vēṇṭum. paḷḷiyil aṇivataṟkeṉṟu taṉippaṭṭa uṭai (Uniform) ellā māṇavarkaḷukkum aḷikkappaṭukiṟatu.

aḻakuk kalaikaḷāṉa ōviyam, ciṟpam, āṭal mutaliyaṉavum iṅkuc ciṟanta muṟaiyil kaṟṟuk koṭukkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ciṟanta paṭaip payiṟciyum, tuppākki cuṭum payiṟciyum iṅku aḷikkappaṭukiṉṟaḷa.

kiruttavak kōyilkaḷ :

utakamaṇṭalattil ciṟanta mūṉṟu kiruttavak kōyilkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. puṉita sṭīḵpaṉ tirukkōyil (St. Stephen's Church), puṉita tāmacu kōyil (St. Thomas Church), kaṇṭal urōmaṉ kattōlikkat tirukkōyil (The Tiny Church of Kandal) eṉpavaiyē avai. puṉita sṭīḵpaṉ tirukkōyil ki. pi. 1830-ām āṇṭil kaṭṭappaṭṭatu. ik kōyiliṉ muṉ pakutiyil poruttappaṭṭirukkum marattiṉāl ceyyappaṭṭa vaḷaivukaḷ kaṇṇaiyum karuttaiyum kavarum kalaivaṉappuṭaiyavai. ivai tippu cultāṉ māḷikaiyiliruntu koṇarappaṭṭavai, utakamaṇṭalattai nīlakiri malaiyiṉmītu tōṟṟuvitta tiruvāḷar callivaṉ avarkaḷuṭaiya camātiyum, avaruṭaiya kuṭumpattār camātikaḷum ik kōyililēyē uḷḷaṉa. ikkōyiliṉ maṇik kūṇṭu ki. pi. 1851-ām āṇṭilum, ālayamaṇi ki. pi. 1894-ām āṇṭilum amaikkappaṭṭaṉa, puṉita tāmacu tirukkōyil ki. pi. 1867-ām āṇṭu tuvakkappaṭṭu ki. pi. 1870-ām āṇṭil muṭikkappaṭṭatu. ivviru kōyilkaḷum nakarattiṉ naṭuvilēyē amaintuḷḷaṉa.

utakamaṇṭalattiliruntu iraṇṭu kal tolaivil uḷḷa 'kaṇṭal' eṉṟa iṭattil amaintuḷḷa kattōlikkar kōyil mikavum pukaḻ peṟṟatu. utakamaṇṭalattiliruntu puṟappaṭṭu, ērikkarai vaḻiyāka vaḷaintu cellum maicūrppātaiyil ceṉṟāl, iṭatu puṟattil iyaṟkaiyaḻaku koḻikkum oru mēṭṭu nilam teṉpaṭum. atuvē kaṇṭal tirukkōyilākum. akkōyilil kuṟippiṭattakka periya kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷ etuvumillai. ammēṭṭiṉ ucciyil ēcu nātarōṭu kūṭiya oru ciluvai nāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. icciluvai pāriciluḷḷa kattōlikkat tiruccapaiyāriṉ āṇaiyāl ki. pi. 1933-il iṅku nāṭaṭappaṭṭatu. itu nāṭṭappaṭṭa cila āṇṭukaḷil ivviṭattiṉ pukaḻ nāṭeṅkum paravat toṭaṅkiviṭṭatu. pallāyirakkaṇakkāṉa makkaḷ āṇṭu tōṟum iṅkup puṉita yāttirai cellukiṉṟaṉar. ciluvai nāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷa mēṭaikkuc cellum vaḻi, mikka aḻakōṭu amaikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. av vaḻiyil iṭaiyiṭaiyē pala taṅkumiṭaṅkaḷ kaṇ kavarum vaṉappōṭu amaikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.

ikkōyil amaintuḷḷa iṭam kattōlikkak kiruttavarkaḷaip putaippataṟkāka araciyalāriṭamiruntu peṟappaṭṭatākum. camātikaḷellām nila maṭṭattiṟkuk kīḻē aṭukkaṭukkāka amaikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa, kōyiliṉ vaḷarccikkutaviya naṉkoṭaiyāḷarkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ caṭṭamiṭṭa takaṭukaḷil poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivviṭukāṭṭiṉ naṭuvil nāṭṭappaṭṭirukkum ciluvai, ēcu nātariṉ cilaiyōṭu aḻakuṭaṉ viḷaṅkukiṟatu. accilai veṇkalattāl āṉatu; ciṟpak kalaivaḷam ceṟintatu.
