icciluvai cakti vāyntatākavum, kēṭṭōrukkuk kēṭṭa varam nalkum peṟṟiyuṭaiyatākavum, teyvat taṉmai mikkatākavum ellārālum karutappaṭukiṟatu. icciluvai paṟṟip pala kataikaḷ vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. icciluvaiyil aṭikkappaṭṭirunta pittaḷai āṇikaḷai māṭu mēykkum ciṟuvarkaḷ oru nāḷ tiruṭik koṇṭu cella muyaṉṟaṉarām. āṉāl avaikaḷaip piṭuṅka muṭiyāmal vīṭu ceṉṟaṉar. vīṭu ceṉṟatum acciṟuvarkaḷ kaṭum nōyvāyppaṭṭu varuntiṉarām. uṇmaiyaṟinta peṟṟōrkaḷ ciluvaiyiṉ muṉ maṇṭiyiṭṭu vaṇaṅkit tam ciṟuvarkaḷiṉ aṟiyāmaiyai maṉṉikkumāṟu vēṇṭiṉar. piṟaku ac ciṟuvarkaḷ piḻaitteḻuntaṉar. ki. pi. 1935-ām āṇṭu camaveḷiyil vāḻnta ēḻeṭṭup pērkaḷaṭaṅkiya oru kuṭumpam icciluvaiyaik kaṇṭu vaṇaṅkak kaṇṭalukku vantiruntatām. mikavum kuḷirāṉa ac camayattil pōrttuk koḷḷap pōrvaikaḷ kūṭa koṇṭuvara villaiyām. ciluvaikketiriluḷḷa pantalil avarkaḷaik kaṇṭa pātiriyār, kuḷiriṉ koṭumaiyaip paṟṟik kūṟi, takka pātukāppōṭu illāviṭṭāl iṟakka nēriṭum eṉṟukūṭac coṉ ṉārām. āṉāl ak kuṭumpattār pātiriyārai nōkki, "aiyarē! ēcu perumāṉ paṉiyil naṉaintu kuḷirāl varuntumpōtum, nāṅkaḷ maṭṭum ēṉ pōrvai tēṭa vēṇṭum? ēcu nātariṉ kālaṭiyilēyē nāṅkaḷ eṭṭu nāṭkaḷ taṅkiyirukka muṭivu ceytirukkiṟōm,“ eṉṟu kūṟiṉārkaḷām. eṭṭu nāṭkaḷ iravu pakal ennēramum appantaliṉ aṭiyilēyē iṟaivaṉai vaṇaṅkiya vaṇṇam akkuṭumpattār kāttukkiṭantaṉarām. evvitat tuṉpamumiṉṟi eṭṭu nāṭkaḷaiyum kaḻittuviṭṭu ūr tirumpiṉarām. ikkuṭumpattār intu camayattaic cārntavarkaḷ eṉpatu kuṟippiṭattakkatu. tamiḻakattu intukkaḷ paḻaṉiyāṇṭavaṉukkum, tiruppati vēṅkaṭappaṉukkum ' vēṇṭutalai' mēṟkoḷvatupōl, kiruttavarkaḷ kaṇṭal ciluvaikku vēṇṭutalai mēṟkoḷḷu kiṉṟaṉar. perum perum celvarkaḷum, uyar nilaiyiluḷḷa cīmāṉkaḷum, paṭittavarkaḷum icciluvaiyiṉ makimaiyait tam vāḻvil aṉupavittu aṟintatākap pattirikaiyil aṭikkaṭi eḻutikkoṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉar, icciluvaiyiṉ ciṟappukkuc cāṉṟākap putupputuk kataikaḷ tōṉṟiyavaṇṇamirukkiṉṟaṉa.

nāḷ tōṟum icciluvaiyaik kaṇṭu vaṇaṅkap pirayāṇikaḷ vantukoṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉar. āṉāl veḷḷikkiḻamaiyaṉṟu nōyvāyppaṭṭōrum, maṉṉippup peṟa virumpum pāvikaḷum, varam peṟa virumpum pakta kōṭikaḷum, kavalaiyāl karuttiḻantu amaitikāṇa virumpuvōrum iṅkut tiraḷākak kūṭukiṉṟaṉar. viḻā nāṭkaḷil kaṇṭal ciluvaiyait taricikka varum pirayāṇikaḷuk keṉṟu, taṉippaṭṭa pukaivaṇṭikaḷum, untuvaṇṭikaḷum viṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. nōyvāyppaṭṭōrai, 'ṭōlikaḷil vaittut tūkkik koṇṭuvarukiṉṟaṉar. koṭuṅkuḷiraiyum poruṭpaṭuttāmal viṭiyaṟkālai mūṉṟu maṇikkē ellārum vantu kūṭukiṉṟaṉar.



kūṉūr :

nīlakiri malaikku varum pirayāṇikaḷil perumpālōr iravil taṅkuvataṟku utakaiyaiviṭak kūṉūraiyē ēṟṟa iṭamākat tērnteṭukkiṉṟaṉar. itu mitamāṉa kuḷiraiyuṭaiya iṭamākum. ivvūr amaintirukkum iṭam kaṭal maṭṭattiliruntu 6000 aṭi uyara muḷḷatu. utakamaṇṭalattiliruntu 12 kal ceṉṟāl ivvūrai aṭaiyalām. ivvaḻiyāṉatu keyṭi (Kaity) paḷḷattākkil pukuntu celkiṟatu. aḻaku koluvīṟṟirukkum ippaḷḷattākkil aṉātai viṭuti yoṉṟum amaintuḷḷatu. ivvaḻic cellum pātaiyil amaintuḷḷa aravaṅkāṭṭil paṭaikkalat toḻiṟcālai (Cordite factory) oṉṟu amaintuḷḷatu. itu ki. pi. 1904-ām āṇṭu araciyalārāl iṅku niṟuvappaṭṭatu. ittoḻiṟcālai āṟu pirivukaḷaik koṇṭatu. itaik kāṇa virumpuvōr, intiyap paṭait tuṟaic ceyalāḷa (The secretary, Ministry of Defence)riṭam muṉkūṭṭiyē aṉumati peṟṟirukkavēṇṭum.

velliṅṭaṉ :

aravaṅkāṭṭiliruntu mūṉṟu kal tolaivu vantāl 'velliṅṭaṉ' eṉṟa paṭaiyiṉar vāḻviṭattaik (Military town) kāṇalām. paṭaiyiṉar taṅkuvataṟkeṉṟu amaikkappaṭṭa illaṅkaḷ varicai varicaiyāka amaintuḷḷaṉa. ki. pi. 1852-ām āṇṭu iṅkup pāṭi vīṭukaḷ amaikkap peṟuvutaṟku muṉ ivviṭam 'jēkṭalā' (Jacktala) eṉṟu aḻaikkappaṭṭatu. iṅku aḻakiya poḻutu pōkku maṉṟam (Club} oṉṟu amaintuḷḷatu. iṅkuḷḷa maṭṭaip pantāṭṭa (Tennis courts) maitāṉaṅkaḷil irāṇuva atikārikaḷum, avar tam maṉaiviyarumē viḷaiyāṭukiṉṟaṉar. velliṅṭaṉukkuḷ ellārum aṉumatikkappaṭuvatillai, uṟaviṉarkaḷō, naṇparkaḷō iruntāl aṅkuc ceṉṟu iṉpamākap poḻutaik kaḻikkamuṭiyum. ovvoru caṉikkiḻamai iravilum, iṅkuḷḷa maṉṟaṅkaḷil mēlai nāṭṭu naṭaṉaṅkaḷ naṭaipeṟukiṉṟaṉa, āṭavarum peṇṭirum iṇaintu iṉṉicaik kēṟpa āṭum akkaḷiyāṭṭam kāṇṭaṟkiṉiyatu.

kāṉiṅ cīmāṭṭiyiṉ irukkai :

ki. pi. 1857-ām āṇṭu vaṭaintiyāvil naṭanta cippāyk kalakattiṉpōtu, intiya nāṭṭu aracap piratinitiyāka vīṟṟirunta kāṉiṅ pirapuviṉ maṉaiviyār teṉṉintiyāviṟku vantārkaḷ. appoḻutu ceṉṉai, peṅkaḷūr, nīlakiri ākiya iṭaṅkaḷil ciṟitu kālam taṅkiṉārkaḷ. cīkūr malaittoṭariṉ vaḻiyāka nīlakiriyai aṭaintārkaḷ. ūṭṭiyiliruntu puṟappaṭṭu 1858-ām āṇṭu ēpral tiṅkaḷ 7-ām nāḷ kūṉūrai aṭaintārkaḷ. cīmāṭṭiyum avarōṭuvanta pirayāṇak kuḻuviṉarum oru māḷikaiyil taṅkiṉārkaḷ. ammāḷikai iṉṟu kiḷaṉviyū hōṭṭal (Glenview Hotel) eṉṟa peyar koṇṭu viḷaṅkukiṟatu. atē māḷikaiyil tāṉ kāṉiṅkiṟku muṉ aracap piratinitiyāka irunta ṭalhaucipirapu 1855-ām āṇṭu mē mutal ākasṭu varai taṅkiyiruntār.

kūṉūriliruntu 6 kal tolaivil kāṉiṅ cīmāṭṭiyiṉ irukkai {Lady Cannig's seat) amain tuḷḷatu. ivviṭam ivvammaiyārukku mikavum piṭittamāṉatu eṉṟu kūṟukiṟārkaḷ. nāḷtōṟum ivviṭattiṟkuc ceṉṟu avar amarntiruppārām. 'iraṇṭu uttamar' (Two Noble Lives) eṉṟa āṅkilap putiṉattil (Novel), ivvammaiyār iṅku amarntu eḻutiya muṭaṅkalkaḷ veḷiyiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivviṭam aḻakiya oru kāṭṭiṉ naṭuvil amaintuḷḷatu. ivviṭattiliruntu kāṇpōrukkuk kūṉūriṉ muḻut tōṟṟamum, pala tēyilait tōṭṭaṅkaḷum, aḻakōṭu kāṭciyaḷikkum. avaṟṟōṭu maicūrai āṇṭa tippucultāṉiṉ paḻaiya kōṭṭai oṉṟum teṉpaṭum. pakaivariṭamiruntu kaippaṟṟiya kaitikaḷai avviṭattiliruntu kīḻē taḷḷik koṉṟuviṭuvārkaḷām. mēṭṭuppāḷaiyamum, avvūriliruntu malaiyiṉmēl vaḷaintu vaḷaintu ēṟivarum pukaivaṇṭip pātaiyum, untuvaṇṭip pātaiyum naṉṟākat teriyum. kūṉūriliruntu iv virukkaikkuc cellum vaḻiyil. lēmps pāṟai (Lamb's Rock) eṉṟa ōr aḻakiya iṭamum uṇṭu.

cim pūṅkā :

kūṉūr nakariṉ naṭuvil amaintuḷḷa cim pūṅkā (Sim's Park) mikavum aḻakāṉatu. utakamaṇṭalattiluḷḷa araciyalār payirt tōṭṭattaiviṭa itu aḷavil ciṟiyateṉṟālum uḷḷattaik kavarum taṉmaiyatu. malaiccarivil itu amaintuḷḷatu. ulakiṉ pala pakutikaḷilum uḷḷa maraṅkaḷ iṅkup payiriṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. aṭukkaṭukkāṉa tiṭṭukaḷil varicai varicaiyākap perumaraṅkaḷum, pala vaṇṇa malarc ceṭikaḷum uḷḷaṉa. ciṟiya ōṭaikaḷ nāṟpuṟamum ōṭivantu pūṅkāviṉ naṭuviluḷḷa kuḷattil viḻukiṉṟaṉa. akkuḷattiṉmītu pala aḻakiya ciṟu pālaṅkaḷ amaikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ippūṅkā tiruvāḷar je. ṭi. cim ci. es. ai. (I. D. Sim C. S. I) eṉpavarāl niṟuvappaṭṭatu. ākaiyiṉālēyē ippūṅkā ivar peyar koṇṭu viḷaṅkukiṟatu. ivar ceṉṉai mēl capaiyiṉ uṟuppiṉarāka (Member of the Legislative Council) 1870 mutal 1875 varai paṇiyāṟṟiṉār. intiyāvil tām vāḻnta iṟuti nāṭkaḷaik kūṉūrilēyē kaḻittār. appōtu ippūṅkāvai amaittu ataṉ vaḷarccikkup peritum pāṭupaṭṭār.

pāsṭar niṟuvaṉam :

kūṉūril kuṟippiṭattakka oṉṟu pāsṭar niṟuvaṉam (Pasteur Institute) ākum. lūyi pāsṭarai aṟiyātavarkaḷ ulakil yārum irukka muṭiyātu. viññāṉa ulakil avaruṭaiya ārāyccikaḷ puraṭcikaramāṉa māṟutalkaḷaic ceytaṉa. 19-ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ piṟpakutiyil, avar āyntu veḷiyiṭṭa kirumiyārāycci (Bacteriology)k koḷkaikaḷ maruttuvat tuṟaikkup perum payaṉ viḷaittaṉa. eṉavē, ulakiṉ pala pakutiyilum kirumiyārāyccik kaḻakaṅkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. intiyāvilum pala kaḻakaṅkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. ākrā kaḻakam 1892-lum, pampāyi luḷḷa āḵpkiṉ kaḻakam (Haffkine Institute) 1896-lum, kacauliyiluḷḷa pāsṭar kaḻakam 1900-lum, ceṉṉai aracar ārāyccik kaḻakam (King Institute) 1903-lum, kacauli naṭuvaṇ ārāyccik kaḻakam (Central Research Institute) 1906-lum, niṟuvappaṭṭaṉa. 1902-ām āṇṭu veṟi nāyk kaṭiyiṉāl tākkuṇṭa āṅkilac ciṟumiyai, kacaulikkuk koṇṭu cellak kāla tāmatam ēṟpaṭṭatāl, ap peṇ iṟantuviṭṭāḷ. ic ceyti teṉṉintiyāvilirunta āṅkila atikārikaḷiṉ kavaṉattaik kavarntatu. ataṉ viḷaivākavē kūṉūriluḷḷa pāsṭar niṟuvaṉam 1907-ām āṇṭu ēpral tiṅkaḷ 25-ām nāḷ (viyāḻakkiḻamai) ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭatu.

amerikkāvil vāḻnta tiruvāḷar heṉṟi ḵpips (Mr. Henry Phipps) eṉṟa kōṭīcuvarar, intiya nāṭṭiṉ maruttuvattuṟai muṉṉēṟṟattiṟkākap perun tokaiyai naṉkoṭaiyāka vaḻaṅkiṉār. appōtu intiya nāṭṭiṉ makkaḷ talaivarāka (Governor General} viḷaṅkiya karcaṉ pirapu an naṉkoṭaiyiṉ poṟuppāḷarāka iruntār. at tokaiyiliruntu oru laṭcam rūpāyai, teṉṉāṭṭil pāsṭar kaḻakam niṟuvuvataṟkāka vaḻaṅkiṉār. appōtu ceṉṉai mānila āḷunarāka viḷaṅkiya āmptil pirapu (Lord Ampthill) ap paṇiyil atika akkaṟai kāṭṭiṉār. ceṉṉaiyil ciṟanta maruttuva nipuṇarāka viḷaṅkiya ṭapiḷyu. ār. pirauṉ, ai.em.es, (W. R. Brown, I.M.S.) eṉpāraiyum, ji. āris (G. Harris) eṉṟa kaṭṭiṭa nipuṇaraiyum kūṉūrukku aṉuppi, oru ciṟanta iṭattait tērn teṭuttu, pāsṭar niṟuvaṉattai amaikkumāṟu paṇittār. ippōtu, in niṟuvaṉam nalla muṟaiyil vaḷarcci peṟṟu, pala iṉṟiyamaiyāta ārāyccikaḷaic ceytukoṇṭirukkiṟatu. veṟi nāyk kaṭiyiṉāl tākkuṇṭa āyirak kaṇakkāṉa makkaḷ iṅkuc ciṟanta cikiccai peṟukiṉṟaṉar.

piṟa kāṭcikaḷ :

kūṉūril kāṇṭaṟkuriya vēṟu pala iṭaṅkaḷum uḷḷaṉa. pulimalai (Tiger's Hill), vākkars malai (Walker's Hill), purūklēṇṭs pātai (Brooklands Road) ākiyavai kuḷirnta niḻal tarum maraṅkaḷaṭarnta kāṭukaḷiṉ naṭuvil amaintuḷḷaṉa. utakamaṇṭalattiliruntu puṟappaṭṭu, keyiṭṭi paḷḷattākku, aravaṅkāṭu, kūṉūr, kiḷaṉ mōrkaṉ tēyilait tōṭṭam, kāṭṭēri aṇai ākiyavaṟṟiṉ vaḻiyākac ceṉṟu maṟupaṭiyum utakamaṇṭalattai aṭaiyum cuṟṟuc celavu (Round trip) uḷḷattiṟku mikavum iṉpam payakkakkūṭiyatākum. kūṉūrukku 6 kal tolaiviliruntu aḻakiya kuntā malaittoṭar toṭaṅkukiṟatu. aṭarnta kāṭukaḷaiyuṭaiya am malaittoṭar, ārāyum iyalpūkkamuṭaiyōrkku nalvāyppai nalkum. kūṉūril pala ciṟanta paḷḷikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. puṉita cūcaiyappar paḷḷi (St. Joseph's School) mikavum toṉmaiyāṉatu.

kōtakiri :

utakamaṇṭalattiliruntu 18-āvatu kallil kōtakiri eṉṟa ūr amaintuḷḷatu. nīlakiri maṭantaiyiṉ kātil oḷirum āṇimuttu eṉṟu itaik kūṟalām. utakaiyiliruntu toṭṭapeṭṭāviṟkuc cellum vaḻiyil nāṉku kal ceṉṟu iṭatu puṟamākac cellum ulōkap pātai (Metal Road) yil ceṉṟāl, kōtakiriyai aṭaiyalām. iv vaḻiyiṉ oru puṟattil aḻakiya paḷḷattākkaiyum, ciṟṟūrkaḷaiyum kāṇalām. tummaṉaṭi, maṭittoṟai, kuṇṭamukkai mutaliya ūrkaḷaik kaṭantu cellum pōtu nāṭṭuppuṟa vāḻviṉ ciṟappu nam uḷḷattil teṉpaṭum. ivvūrkaḷaik kaṭantatum vaḻi neṭukat tōṉṟum nīraruvikaḷum, iḷamarak kāṭukaḷum, pacum pul veḷikaḷum nammai mīḷā iṉpattilāḻttum. kuṟuki vaḷaintu cellum pātaikaḷum, avaṟṟil amaintuḷḷa vaḷaivukaḷil perumūccu viṭṭuk koṇṭu tiṇaṟi ēṟum untu vaṇṭikaḷum, kōtarkaḷiṉ nāṭṭuppuṟa vāḻvum, nam uḷḷattai viṭṭu nīṅkāmal nilaittu, niṉaikkuntōṟum iṉikkum. 15 kal kaṭantatum kaṭṭapuṭṭai eṉṟa ciṟṟūr uḷḷatu. avviṭattiliruntu mīṇṭum tār pōṭappaṭṭa pātai toṭaṅkukiṟatu. aḻakiya kāṭcikaḷiṭaiyē mēlum mūṉṟu kal ceṉṟōmāṉāl, kōtakiriyai aṭaiyalām. kōtakiriyiṉ kaṭaivīti oru carivil amaintuḷḷatu. av vītiyilēyē ūrāṇmaik kaḻaka aluvalakamum, vēṟu cila aḻakiya kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷum amaintuḷḷaṉa.



5. paḻaṉi malaikaḷ

peyark kāraṇam :

paḻaṉimalai eṉṟu kuṟippiṭumpōtu, murukaṉ kōvil koṇṭu eḻuntaruḷiyirukkum karumalaiyē ellōruṭaiya uḷḷattilum tōṉṟum. āṉāl iṅkuk kuṟippiṭappaṭum paḻaṉi malaikaḷ, paḻaṉi nakarukkut teṟkiluḷḷa malaik kūṭṭamē ākum. vaṭa moḻiyil ivaṟṟai 'varāka kiri' eṉpar. tamiḻil itaip paṉṟi malai eṉṟu kuṟippiṭuvar. im malaikku ip peyar ēṟpaṭṭataṟkuk kāraṇamāka oru karṇa paramparaik katai vaḻaṅkukiṟatu. im malaimītu oru muṉivar tavam ceytukoṇṭiruntārām, av vaḻi vanta paṉṉiraṇṭu kuṟumpukkāra iḷaiñarkaḷ am muṉivarai ikaḻntu pēciṉārkaḷām. itaṉāl ciṉaṅkoṇṭa am muṉivar ap paṉṉiruvaraiyum paṉṟikaḷākap pōkumpaṭi capittārām. avarkaḷum paṉṟikaḷāka māṟi am malaimītu cuṟṟit tirintārkaḷ . avarkaḷ pāl irakkam koṇṭa civaperumāṉ avarkaḷuṭaiya paṉṟiyuruvai māṟṟi, pāṇṭiya maṉṉaṉ avaikkaḷattil amaiccarkaḷāka amarttiṉārām. ik kāraṇam paṟṟiyē im malai paṇṭai nāṭkaḷil paṉṟi malai eṉṟu peruvaḻakkāka aḻaikkappaṭṭu vantatu eṉpar. paṉṟi malaiyiṉ tiripē paḻaṉi malai eṉṟum cilar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. pukaḻ mikka paḻaṉi nakarukku arukil iruppataṉāl, im malai paḻaṉi malai eṉṟu peyar peṟṟatākavum vēṟu cilar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.

amaippu:

itu mēṟkut toṭarcci malaiyiṉ oru pirivākum. im malaikkut teṉ mēṟkē ēlakkāy malai (Cardamon Hills) amaintuḷḷatu. am malaiyiṉ kiḻakkē kampam paḷḷattākku amaintuḷḷatu. paḻaṉi malaiyiṉ nīḷam 40 kal, akalam 25 kal, itu kiḻakkup pakuti eṉṟum, mēṟkup pakuti eṉṟum iru pirivukaḷākap pirikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ippirivukaḷai mēṟpaḻaṉi eṉṟum kīḻppaḻaṉi eṉṟum kuṟippiṭuvar. kīḻppaḻaṉi malaiyil 3000 aṭiyiliruntu 5000 aṭivarai uyaramuḷḷa pala cikaraṅkaḷ citaṟik kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa. ic cikaraṅkaḷiṭaiyē kuṟukiya pala paḷḷattākkukaḷ amaintuḷḷaṉa. aḻakiṉ iruppiṭamāka viḷaṅkum ippaḷḷattākkukaḷil aṅkoṉṟum iṅkoṉṟumākap pala kukkirāmaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟil kuṟippiṭattakkatum, periyatum 'paṇṇaikkāṭu' eṉṟa ciṟṟūrām. itil 4000 makkaḷ vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. ic ciṟṟūrkaḷaic cuṟṟi mā, palā, puḷiyaṉ, ārañcu, elumiccai, cītaḷai (itu oruvakaip pērelumiccai, kaṭāraṅkāy eṉṟum kūṟuvar. Citron) mutaliya maraṅkaḷaṭarnta cōlaikaḷ kaṇkavarum vaṉappōṭu viḷaṅkum. iṅku vāḻum makkaḷaip 'paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷ' (Hill tribes) eṉṟu kūṟuvataṟkillai. ivarkaḷellām kōvai, maturai māvaṭṭaṅkaḷiluḷḷa camaveḷikaḷiliruntu paṇṭai nāṭkaḷil iṅkuk kuṭiyēṟiyavarkaḷē, ivarkaḷ tōṟṟattilum, naṭai uṭai pāvaṉaikaḷilum, aṅka amaippilum camaveḷiyil vāḻum makkaḷiliruntu vēṟupaṭṭavarkaḷākat teriyavillai. iṅku vāḻum 'kuṉṉuvar' (Kunnuvans) eṉṟa kulattārē perumpālum nilakkiḻārkaḷāka uḷḷaṉar. iṅku vāḻum ‘pulaiyar' eṉṉum iṉattār ivarkaḷukkup paṇipurintu vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. iṅku vāḻum teluṅkuc ceṭṭimārkaḷum, iculāmiyarkaḷum kuṉṉuvarkaḷiṉ nilattaik koñcam koñcamākat taṅkaḷukku urimaiyākkik koṇṭaṉar. ivviru iṉattārum ōraḷavu paṇam paṭaittavarkaḷ, kuṉṉuvarukkum, pulaiyarukkum vēṇṭiyapōtu paṇaṅkoṭuttu avarkaḷaik kaṭaṉkārarkaḷākki, piṟaku avarkaḷ nilaṅkaḷaiyum paṟittuk koṇṭaṉar. ikkīḻp pakutiyāṉatu malēriyā nōykku iruppiṭam eṉṟu collalām. mārc mutal jūlai varai iṅku vāḻum makkaḷ kāyccalāl tākkuṇṭu peritum varuntukiṉṟaṉar, āṉāl maṟṟa tiṅkaḷkaḷil kāyccal varātu eṉṟu colla muṭiyātu.

mēl paḻaṉimalai 6000 aṭi mutal 8000 aṭi varaiyil uyarvuḷḷatu. paḻaṉi malaiyil mikavum uyarnta cikaram vempāṭi ṣōlā malai eṉpatākum. itu maturai māvaṭṭattiluḷḷa ellā malaiccikaraṅkaḷaiyum viṭa uyarntatu eṉṟē collalām. itaṉ uyaram 8218 aṭi. kuṟiñci nakaramāṉa kōṭaikkāṉal im mēṟpaḻaṉiyiṉ teṉpākattil amaintuḷḷatu. kīḻppaḻaṉiyil paraviyuḷḷa aṭarnta kāṭukaḷai iṅkuk kāṇamuṭiyātu. niṟainta paḷḷattākkukaḷum iṅkuk kiṭaiyā. veppa nilaiyum kuḷirntu tōṉṟum. payirt toḻilukkup payaṉpaṭāta nilam iṅku atikam. iṅkuḷḷa pīṭapūmikaḷil muraṭṭup pul muḷaitta paranta veḷikaḷ (Downs) mikuntu tōṉṟum, oru cila paḷḷat tākkukaḷil makkaḷiṉ kuṭiyiruppukaḷaic cuṟṟi utakamaṇṭalattil iruppavaṟṟaip pōṉṟu iḷamarakkāṭukaḷ (Sholas) niṟaintirukkum. ip paḷḷattākkukaḷait tavira maṟṟa iṭaṅkaḷiluḷḷa nilaṅkaḷellām, pul cettai aṭarnta melliya maṇpaṭalattāl mūṭappaṭṭirukkum. appaṭalattiṉaṭiyil, mañcaḷ niṟamāṉa kaḷimaṇ nilam amaintirukkum. ak kaḷimaṇṇiṟ kaṭiyil paraviyuḷḷa kaṟpāṟaikaḷ, eṅkup pārttālum talai nīṭṭikkoṇṭirukkum.

paḻaṉimalai mītuḷḷa carivu vaṭakku nōkkip paṭippaṭiyāka iṟaṅkukiṟatu. āṉāl teṉpakutiyō mikavum ceṅkuttāṉa pāṟaikaḷaiyum carivukaḷaiyum koṇṭatu. kampam paḷḷattākkiliruntu kāṇpōrukku accariviluḷḷa pāṟaikaḷ periya cuvarkaḷ pōl kāṭciyaḷikkum. appāṟaikaḷiṉiṭaiyē perumperum paḷḷaṅkaḷ amaintuḷḷaṉa. aṭivārattiliruntu kāṇpōrukku akkāṭci viyappūṭṭum eṉpatil aiyamillai. vaṭa pakutiyil amaintuḷḷa iruperum paḷḷattākkukaḷ appīṭapūmiyai iṭaiyaṟuttuk koṇṭu vilpaṭṭi, pūmpāṟai ākiya ciṟṟūrkaḷ varai teṟku nōkkic cellukiṉṟaṉa. camaveḷiyiliruntu ivviru paḷḷattākkukaḷiṉ vaḻiyāka malaiyucciyai vantaṭaiyum iru kuṟukiya pātaikaḷum kuṟippiṭattakkavai. im malaiyōṭu neruṅkiya vāṇipat toṭarpu koṇṭa nakaram 'paḻaṉi' ākum. paḻaṉiyiliruntu puṟappaṭuvōr kāl naṭaikaḷiṉ mītu potikaḷai ēṟṟikkoṇṭu ippātaikaḷ mūlamākavē ucciyai aṭaivar. paḻaṉiyiliruntu vilpaṭṭi cellum malaivaḻip pātai aṭikkaṭi kuṟukiya paḷḷaṅkaḷāl iṭaiyaṟukkappaṭuvatāl, kutiraikaḷ ivvaḻiyākac cella muṭivatillai. āṉāl pūmpāṟaikkuc cellum malaivaḻippātai celluvataṟku eḷitāṉatu. periyakuḷattiliruntu kōṭaikkāṉal cellum kutiraip pātai (bridle path)' amaikkappaṭṭatum, mēṟkūṟiya iru pātaikaḷum kavaṉippāraṟṟuc cīr keṭṭup pōyiṉa. kutiraip pātaiyum lās pātai (Law's Ghat)yum, putiya āttūrppātaiyum, kōṭaikkāṉal kuṭiyiruppukaḷai iṇaikkum pātaikaḷumē paḻaṉi malaimītu puḻakkattiliruntavai. ippōtu ōraḷavu cīrāṉa pātaikaḷ amaikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ōr ūriliruntu maṟṟōr ūrukkuc ceyti aṟivikkum vēlaiyāṉatu, karaṭu muraṭāṉa malaivaḻip pātaikaḷiṉ mūlamum, kaṭappataṟkariya kāṭṭuvaḻikaḷiṉ mūlamumē. naṭaipeṟṟaṉa.



immalai mītu amaintuḷḷa pūmpāṟaip paḷḷattākku mikavum kuṟippiṭattakkatu. ip paḷḷattākkiṉ irupuṟac carivukaḷum cama aḷavuḷḷavai. ic carivukaḷil payirttoḻil ciṟanta muṟaiyil naṭaipeṟukiṟatu. eṅkup pārttālum aḻakiya iḷa marak kāṭukaḷ uḷḷaṉa, aṅkoṉṟum iṅkoṉṟumākap piḷavupaṭṭa nilappakutikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. cuṟṟilumuḷḷa nilaṅkaḷiṉ tāḻcciyiṉāl ēṟpaṭṭa ciṟu ciṟu ceṅkuttāṉa mēṭukaḷ teṉpaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ippaḷḷat tākkiṉ ucciyil amaintuḷḷa oru mēṭṭu nilattilēyē 'pūmpāṟai' amaintuḷḷatu. itil ēṟakkuṟaiya 2000 makkaḷē vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. mēl paḻaṉi malaimītu amaintuḷḷa ciṟṟūrkaḷil itu mukkiyamāṉatu. immalaimītu nilavari vacūlippataṟkāṉa aluvalakam, muṉpu iṅku tāṉ amaintiruntatu. iṅku amaintuḷḷa cuppiramaṇiyar kōvil mikavum pukaḻ vāyntatākum. viḻā nāṭkaḷil paḻaṉimalaiyiṉ pala pakutiyiliruntum makkaḷ iṅku vantu kūṭu kiṉṟaṉar.

mēlpaḻaṉiyil amaintuḷḷa vīṭukaḷiṉ cuvarkaḷ kuṟukkum neṭukkumākap poruttappaṭṭa mūṅkil kaḻikaḷiṉ mēl maṇ pūcappaṭṭuk kaṭṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. kūrai puṟkaḷāl vēyappaṭukiṉṟatu. ovvoru vīṭṭilum kaṉappu amaikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. iṅkuk kuṉṉuvar, kārakkāṭṭu veḷḷāḷar (kārkātta vēḷāḷar) eṉṟa iru iṉattārum vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. āṉāl avarkaḷ tokai mikak kuṟaivu. mēl paḻaṉi, kīḻppaḻaṉi ākiya ellā iṭaṅkaḷilum vāḻum makkaḷiṉ mottat tokai 20,000.

maturai māvaṭṭattiluḷḷa malaikaḷiṉiṭaiyē īṭum eṭuppumaṟṟup paḻaṉimalai perumitattōṭu nimirntu niṟkiṟatu. kālaiñāyiṟṟup poṉṉoḷiyil pakkaṅkaḷiluḷḷa toṭarkaḷōṭu talai nimirntu niṟkum cikaraṅkaḷ, kāḷaiyarkaḷ palar tam valimikka tōḷkaḷai uyarttikkoṇṭu niṟpatu pōl tōṉṟum. naṭuppakal nērattil maṟṟa malaikaḷait taṉ cemmānta uyarattāl iruḷil āḻttiviṭṭu oḷiyuṭaṉ tikaḻum, mālai nērattil katiravaṉiṉ cenniṟak kiraṇaṅkaḷaip pōrttukkoṇṭu īṭillāp peruvaṉappōṭu tikaḻum. perumaḻai peytu ōynta camayattil itaṉ uyariya cikaraṅkaḷ mēkaṅkaḷākiya muṭiyait talaiyil cumantukoṇṭu, peruñ ciṟappōṭu niṟkum pēraracaippōl kāṭciyaḷikkum. itaṉ carivukaḷil iḻintu calacalattu ōṭi varum nīr aruvikaḷiṉ mītum, ekkāḷamiṭṭut tāvi varum nīr vīḻccikaḷiṉ mītum taṅkak katirōṉ taṉ taṇaṟ kuḻampaip pūci makiḻumpōtu, pacumaiyum nīlamum māṟi māṟic cuṭarviṭum.

āṟukaḷ :

maturai māvaṭṭattiṉ vaṭa pakutiyil uḷḷa paḻaṉi, tiṇṭukkal vaṭṭaṅkaḷiluḷḷa cemmaṇ nilattil cama tūraṅkaḷil pāyum nāṉku āṟukaḷum paḻaṉi malaiyilēyē tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa. koṭavaṉāṟu, naṅkāñci āṟu, nal taṅkiyāṟu, caṇmuka nati eṉpavaiyē avai. innāṉkum kāviriyiṉ tuṇai natiyāṉa amarāvatiyil kalakkiṉṟaṉa. paḻaṉiyil perumaḻai peyta kālaṅkaḷil ivvāṟukaḷilum tiṭīreṉṟu veḷḷap perukku ēṟpaṭum. cila nāṭkaḷil ap perukkut taṇintu, ciṟṟōṭaiyākak kāṭci tarum. innāṉku āṟukaḷil mikavum payaṉuṭaiyatu caṇmuka natiyākum. vilpaṭṭi, pūm pāṟaip paḷḷattākkukaḷil vaḻiyum nīraip peṟṟu itu ōṭi varukiṟatu. ippaḷḷattākkukaḷil amaintuḷḷa āṟu pirivāṉa paṭukaikaḷil iḻintu varum nīr oṉṟākakkūṭi ivvāṟṟai urukkoḷḷumāṟu ceyvatāl ivvāṟu caṇmuka (āṟumuka) nati eṉṟu peyar peṟukiṟatu. itu paḻaṉi nakariṉiṭaiyē ōṭuvatāl, appaḻaṉiyil kōyil koṇṭirukkum caṇmukaṉiṉ peyarē ivvāṟṟukkum vaḻaṅkuvatāka ellōrum eṇṇikkoṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉar.

mēṟkūṟiya āṟukaḷ nāṉkum maturai māvaṭṭattiṉ vaṭa ellaiyil ōṭukiṉṟaṉa. maturai māvaṭṭattiṉ naṭuvil vaikaiyum, ataṉ tuṇaiyāṟukaḷum ōṭukiṉṟaṉa. vaikaiyiṉ tuṇaiyāṟukaḷil pala paḻaṉimaṭaiyil tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa. varāka nati, mēl paḻaṉimalaiyil tōṉṟi, periya kuḷattiṟkarukil pāmpāṟṟōṭu kalakkiṟatu, pāmpāṟum paḻaṉimalaiyilēyē tōṉṟukiṟatu. ivvāṟu kōṭaikkāṉalukkarukil ōr aḻakiya nīrvīḻcciyai uṇṭākkukiṟatu. kutiraip pātaikkarukiliruntu kāṇpōrukku innīrvīḻcci peruvaṉappōṭu kāṭci tarum. ivviru natikaḷum vaikaiyōṭu kalakkiṉṟaṉa. mañcaḷāṟu eṉṟa vēṟoru natiyum paḻaṉimalaic carivil tōṉṟi ōṭi varukiṉṟatu. itai vattalakuṇṭu āṟu eṉṟum kūṟuvar. itu tēvatāṉappaṭṭikku mēl 200 aṭi uyarattiliruntu vīḻntu, ōr aḻakiya nīrvīḻcciyai uṇṭākkukiṟatu. pātaiyiliruntu kāṇpōrukku avvīḻcci kaṇkavarum vaṉappōṭu kāṭciyaḷikkum, piṟaku ivvāṟu vattalakuṇṭuvai nōkki ōṭi varukiṟatu. kīḻppaḻaṉiyiliruntu tōṉṟivarum piṟitōr āṟāṉa ayyampāḷaiyam āṟṟōṭu cērntu, mañcaḷāṟu vaikaiyil kalakkiṟatu.

kāṭukaḷ :

paḻaṉimalaic carivukaḷil aṭarnta kāṭukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. āṉāl avai ippōtu vāṭiyum, perum pakuti aḻikkappaṭṭum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. aḷavaṟṟa maraṅkaḷai veṭṭiyum, tīyiṭṭup pocukkiyum pāḻpaṭutti viṭṭaṉar. ivvāṟu aḻikkappaṭṭaṉa pōka mikavum kuṟaivāṉa maraṅkaḷē iṅkuk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. cariviluḷḷa kāṭukaḷil kuṟippiṭattakkavai, paḻaṉimalaiyiṉ vaṭakiḻakkup pakutiyiluḷḷa āyakkuṭi, kaṉṉivāṭi esṭēṭṭukaḷukku iṭaiyiluḷḷa kāṭukaḷē. maṟṟa iṭaṅkaḷaip pōlallāmal ivviṭattil nilam vaḷamuḷḷatāka uḷḷatu. vaṭa mēṟku mūlaiyil, kūkal ṣōlāviliruntu amarāvati āṟuvaraiyilum uḷḷa carivu eḷitil aṭaitaṟkariyatu. ākaiyiṉāl accarivukaḷiluḷḷa kāṭukaḷ aḻivuṟāmal irukkiṉṟaṉa. periya kuḷattiṟku mēṟkil amaintuḷḷa akkamalaittoṭaril uḷḷa kāṭṭu maraṅkaḷ veṭṭappaṭṭum, tīyiṭappaṭṭum, payirt toḻilukkāka aḻikkappaṭṭum kēṭuṟṟaṉa. āṉāl ippoḻutu araciyalār talaiyīṭṭāl akkāṭukaḷ puttuyir peṟṟuḷḷaṉa, aḻikkappaṭṭa kāṭukaḷil uyarnta raka maraṅkaḷ, piṟaku nalla muṟaiyil vaḷarva tillai. muṭpu tarkaḷum, payaṉaṟṟa ciṟu maraṅkaḷumē vaḷarkiṉṟaṉa. paḻaṉimalaic carivukaḷil potuvāka vēṅkai, vekkali, veṉ tēvatāru, centēvatāru, tēkku, karuṅkāli, kālnaṭ mutaliya maraṅkaḷ mikuntu kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.

kīḻp paḻaṉi, mēṟ paḻaṉip pīṭapūmikaḷiluḷḷa kāṭukaḷil vaḷarum maraṅkaḷ mikavum vilai uyarntavai. kīḻppaḻaṉip pīṭapūmikaḷiluḷḷa kāṭukaḷ vāḻaiyum, kāḵpiyum payiriṭuvataṟkākap perum aḷavu aḻikkappaṭṭaṉa. ēlamum aṅku mikutiyākap payiriṭappaṭukiṉṟatu. ivviṭattil amaintuḷḷa paḷḷat tākkukaḷil paṭintirukkum karumaṇ vaḷamāṉatu; maṇalum kaḷiyum kalantatu. iṅku vaḷarntirukkum iḷamarakkāṭukaḷ aḻivuṟāmal ceḻippuṟṟu viḷaṅku kiṉṟaṉa. centēvatāru maraṅkaḷum, kāl naṭ maraṅkaḷum, vaiṭeks, ālṭīcimā mutaliya maraṅkaḷum iṅku ceḻittu vaḷarkiṉṟaṉa. centēvatāru maraṅkaḷum, vaiṭeks, ālṭīcimā maraṅkaḷum peṭṭikaḷ ceyvataṟkum caṭṭaṅkaḷ aṟuppataṟkum peritum payaṉpaṭukiṉṟaṉa,

mēṟkiluḷḷa mēṟpaḻaṉip pīṭapūmi ciṟitu ciṟitāka uyarntu cellat toṭaṅkiyatum aṅkuḷḷa maṇ, vaḷam aṟṟatāka māṟukiṟatu. tāṇṭik kuṭikkum paṇṇaikkāṭṭiṟkum iṭaiyiluḷḷa tāḻnta malaippakutiyait tāṇṭiyatum, maram ceṭi koṭikaḷellām vaḷarcciyil muṟṟilum māṟupaṭṭut tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa. aṭarnta kāṭukaḷ aṅkuk kiṭaiyā. eṅkup pārttālum pulveḷikaḷum, muṭputarkaḷum, kuṭṭaiyāṉa maraṅkaḷumē teṉpaṭukiṉṟaṉa. aṅkuḷḷa īramāṉa paḷḷattākkukaḷil maṭṭum ōriru iḷamarakkāṭukaḷ teṉpaṭukiṉṟaṉa. pattup paṉṉiraṇṭu kāṭukaḷē ōraḷavu periyavai. pulik kāṭu (Tiger Shola), perumāḷ kāṭu (Perumal Shola) vañcakkāṉal, kōṭaikkāṉal, kuṇṭaṉ kāṭu (Kundan Shola} kūkal kāṭu {Kukal Shola) eṉpavai kuṟippiṭattakkavai. kōṭaikkāṉaliliruntu nāṉku kal mēṟkiluḷḷa ṭākṭars ṭilaiṭ (Doctor's delight) eṉṉum iḷamarakkāṭu, vaṉapōcaṉam uṇpōrukku mikavum ēṟṟa iṭamāka viḷaṅkukiṟatu. iṅku vaḷarumpaṭiyāṉa ciṟu maraṅkaḷ viṭṭaṅkaḷ aṟuppataṟkō, palakaikaḷ aṟuppataṟkō payaṉpaṭātavai, iṅku pala nīr aruvikaḷ tōṉṟuvataṟkuriya īrattaip pātukāppataṟkē immaraṅkaḷ peritum payaṉpaṭukiṉṟaṉa. iṅkuḷḷa kāṭukaḷ muṉpu peritum aḻikkappaṭṭaṉa. mēṟpaḻaṉi malaiyiṉ ucciyil uḷḷa ippīṭapūmi kōṭaikkāṉal ellaiyiliruntu toṭaṅki mēṟkil tiruvāṅkūr ellai varaiyilum, teṟkil pōṭināyakkaṉūr varaiyilum paraviyuḷḷatu. kāṭṭuc caṭṭattiṉ (Forest Act)paṭi iṅkuḷḷa kāṭukaḷ araciyalārāl pātukākkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa, ippīṭapūmikku, āmptil veḷi, ( Ampthill downs) eṉṟu peyariṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉar. itaṉ parappu 5 caturamail, ippīṭapūmiyiṉ kālpakuti kāṭukaḷ. mukkāl pakuti paranta pacumpul veḷikaḷ. iṅku 7000 aṭi mutal 8000 aṭivarai uyaramuḷḷa ciṟitum peritumāṉa pala cikaraṅkaḷ teṉpaṭukiṉṟaṉa. im mā nilattiluḷḷa mikavum aḻakiya malaiveḷikaḷil āmptil veḷiyum oṉṟu.

paḻaṉimalaiyiṉmītu pala iṭaṅkaḷil ceyaṟkai muṟaiyil kāṭukaḷ vaḷarkkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ki. pi. 1870-ām āṇṭu kēmpel vākkar (Colonel Compbell Walker) eṉpavar, paḻaṉimalaiyiṉ vaṭapuṟa aṭivārattil uḷḷa vēlaṉ kōmpai eṉṟa iṭattilum, periyāṟṟu ērikkarukiluḷḷa aṭivāramākiya vaṇṇāttippāṟai eṉṟa iṭattilum tēkku maraṅkaḷaip payiriṭṭār. avviṭattil ēṟakkuṟaiya 4500 maraṅkaḷ vaḷarntaṉa. āṉāl, tēkku payiriṭuvataṟkut tērnteṭukkappaṭṭa avviṭaṅkaḷ, teṉ mēṟkup paruvakkāṟṟiṉāl peyyum maḻaiyiṉ muḻup payaṉaiyum peṟuvatillai. vēlaṉ kōmpaiyil vaḷarkkappaṭṭa tēkkuk kāṭukaḷ, appakkamāka ōṭivarum kālvāyiṉāl nalla nīr vaḷattaip peṟṟiruntaṉa. āṉāl aṭikkaṭi ēṟpaṭum peruveḷḷattāl niṟaiya maraṅkaḷ aḻivuṟṟaṉa, atē āṇṭil kōṭaikkāṉalil nīlap piciṉ maraṅka (Blue gum trees}ḷum, āstirēliya nāṭṭaic cērnta karumaraṅkaḷum payiriṭappaṭṭaṉa. kōṭaikkāṉalil vāḻum makkaḷukku eriporuḷ vaḻaṅkuvataṟkum, aṅkuḷḷa aḻakiya iḷamarak kāṭṭai aḻivuṟāmal kāppataṟkumē ivaikaḷ payiriṭappaṭṭaṉa. ivviṭamum immaraṅkaḷiṉ vaḷarccikku ēṟṟatāka illai. kōṭaikkāṉaliṉ eriporuḷ tēvaiyai annakarukku mēṟkil 2 kal tolaiviluḷḷa kuṇṭaṉ kāṭṭiluḷḷa maraṅkaḷē pūrtti ceykiṉṟaṉa. ikkāṭṭil 1887-88ām āṇṭukaḷil niṟaiya maraṅkaḷ payiriṭappaṭṭaṉa. 1895ām āṇṭilum, 1905 ām āṇṭilum, kuṇṭaṉ kāṭṭiṉ perumpakuti tīyiṉāl aḻivuṟṟatu.

vilaṅkukaḷ :

paḻaṉi malaiyiṉ ellāp pakutikaḷilum yāṉaikaḷ muṉpu niṟaiyat tirintaṉa, kiḻakkiluḷḷa kaṉṉivāṭi varaiyilum kūṭa avaikaḷ vantu payirkaḷukku mikavum cētam viḷaivittaṉa. ivaṟṟāl ēṟpaṭum aḻivait taṭukka araciyalār perum muyaṟcikaḷ eṭuttuk koṇṭaṉar. jervis eṉṟa oru veḷḷaiyar, tām eḻutiyuḷḷa, kāviriyiṉ nīr vīḻccikaḷai nōkkic ceṉṟa payaṇattiṉ varuṇaṉai' (Narrative of a journey to the falls of Cauvery) eṉṟa nūlil paḻaṉimalaiyil tiriyum yāṉaikaḷaippaṟṟi virivāka eḻutiyuḷḷār. kampam paḷḷattākkiṟku arukiluḷḷa malaikaḷiṭaiyē iyaṟkaiyāka amaintuḷḷa kaṇavāykaḷiṉ vaḻiyāka avaikaḷ varukiṉṟaṉavām, poyk kuḻikaḷ amaittu, ikkaṇavāykaḷil eḷitāka ivaṟṟaip piṭikkiṉṟaṉarām. ikkaṇavāy ōriṭattil mikavum kuṟukic cellukiṟatām. akkuṟukalāṉa iṭattil oru yāṉai tāṉ nuḻaiya muṭiyum. avviṭattiṟku appāluḷḷa akaṉṟa kaṇavāyil varicai varicaiyāṉa pala kuḻikaḷai veṭṭi vaippārkaḷ. yāṉaik kūṭṭattai akkuṟukiya vaḻiyiṉ mūlamāka viraṭṭiṉāl, mika viraivil av yāṉaikaḷ kuḻikaḷil viḻuntuviṭum. oru taṭavai nāṉkumaṇi nērattil, 63 yāṉaikaḷ immuṟaiyil kaippaṟṟappaṭṭaṉavām. mēṟpaḻaṉiyilum, carivukaḷilum puli, civiṅkip puli, karaṭi, kāṭṭerumai, māṉ, varaiyāṭu, kāṭṭuppaṉṟi, cennāy mutaliyaṉa vāḻkiṉṟaṉa. kōṭaikkāṉalil kākkaikaḷaik kāṇa muṭiyātu.

payir vakaikaḷ :

kīḻppaḻaṉip pīṭapūmikaḷil vāḻum kuṉṉuvarkaḷum pulaiyarkaḷum puṉceyp payirkaḷaip payiriṭuvatōṭu, vāḻai maraṅkaḷaiyum niṟaiyap payiriṭukiṉṟaṉar. iṅkup payirākum vāḻaippaḻam mikka cuvaiyum maṇamumuḷḷatu. kōṭaikkāṉal vaṭṭattil vēṟeṅkum ivvaḷavu uyarnta raka vāḻai payirāvatillai. iṅkup payiriṭappaṭum vāḻai maraṅkaḷ toṭarntu 20 āṇṭukaḷukkup palaṉ koṭukkum taṉmaiyuṭaiyavai. iṅku, nel, kāḵpi, ēlam, iñci, mañcaḷ mutaliyaṉavum nalla muṟaiyil payiriṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. iṅku arēpikā eṉṉum uyarnta rakak kāḵpi viḷaikiṟatu. kāḵpip payirai mutaṉ mutalil payiriṭṭavar em. emiliṭi ḵpāṇṭkiḷēr eṉṉum veḷḷaiyar. ciṟumalaiyiṉmītu ivaruṭaiya tantaiyār payiriṭṭa kāḵpi vitaikaḷai iṅkuk koṇarntu ivarum payiriṭṭār. ippōtu nam nāṭṭiṟku nalla varuvāy nalkum payirāka itu iṅku viḷaṅkukiṟatu. ēlamum, iñciyum viḷaiya nalla niḻal vēṇṭiyiruppatāl aṭarnta kāṭukaḷilēyē ivaikaḷ viḷaikiṉṟaṉa. ēlam aintu āṇṭukaḷilum, mañcaḷ 18 mātaṅkaḷilum palaṉ koṭukkum. mañcaḷ tiṟanta veḷikaḷilēyē payiriṭappaṭukiṟatu. mā, palā, ārañcu, elumiccai, mātuḷai, koṭittirāṭcai, pḷam tirāṭcai, āppiḷ, perri mutaliya paḻaṅkaḷum iṅku viḷaikiṉṟaṉa.

mēṟpaḻaṉimalaiyil nel, kāḵpi, maṭṭarakak kōtumai, maṭṭarakap pārli, veḷḷaippūṇṭu, paḻa vakaikaḷ ākiyavai niṟaiya viḷaikiṉṟaṉa. iṅku viḷaiyum veḷḷaippūṇṭu perum aḷavil nāṭeṅkum ēṟṟumati ceyyappaṭukiṟatu. malaiccarivukaḷil aṭukkaṭukkāka nel vayalkaḷ amaikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. malaiyiliruntu iḻintu varum nīraruvikaḷait taṭuttut tēkki, ciṟu vāykkālkaḷiṉ mūlamāka nel vayalukkup pāyccukiṟārkaḷ. ivarkaḷ nel vayalkaḷil eruvaik koṭṭi mitippatillai. tēkkiya nīrai vāyk kālkaḷiṉ mūlamāka erukkuviyalkaḷiṭaiyē pāycci, avaṟṟil ūṟi varum nīrai nel vayalkaḷil pāyccukiṉṟaṉar. iṅku viḷaiyum nel avvaḷavu uyarnta rakattaic cārntatalla. neṟpayir muṟṟa eṭṭu allatu pattu mātaṅkaḷ cellum.

makkaḷ

kuṉṉuvar :

nīlakiri malaiyiṉmītu paṭakar evvāṟu ciṟanta uḻavarkaḷāka vāḻkiṉṟaṉarō, atupōl paḻaṉimalaiyil kuṉṉuvar ciṟanta uḻavarkaḷāka vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷuṭaiya tāymoḻi tamiḻ moḻiyē ākum. ivarkaḷ taṅkaḷaik kuṉṉuva vēḷāḷar eṉṟu kūṟik koḷkiṉṟaṉar. kōvai māvaṭṭattiluḷḷa tārāpuram, kāṅkayam pakutikaḷē taṅkaḷ muṉṉōr vāḻnta iṭameṉṟum kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. pōrum pañcamum mikuntirunta kāraṇattāl, aintāṟu nūṟṟāṇṭukaṭku muṉ ivarkaḷiṉ muṉṉōrkaḷ camaveḷiyiliruntu iṅkuk kuṭipukuntārkaḷām. vijayanakara maṉṉar, marāṭṭiya maṉṉar, tippucultāṉ ākiyōriṉ āṭciyil, variccumai tāḷāmalum vēṟu pala tuṉpaṅ kaḷukku āṭpaṭṭum varuntiyavarkaḷ palar iṅkuk kuṭi pukuntārkaḷ. oru camayam kakkaṟkaḻicca (Cholera) liṉāl avatippaṭṭa camaveḷi makkaḷum iṅkuk kuṭi. pukuntatākak kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. kōvai māvaṭṭattiluḷḷa kuṉṉūr eṉṟa ciṟṟūriṉ peyarē taṅkaḷ kuṭippeyarāka amaintateṉṟu kāraṇam kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. kuṉṉuvar paḻaṉimalaiyil kuṭipukuntatu paṟṟi vēṟoru ceytiyum vaḻaṅkukiṟatu. virūpāṭci, āyakkuṭi ākiya iṭaṅkaḷil vāḻnta pōlikar eṉṉum vakuppār malaimītuḷḷa taṅkaḷuṭaiya nilaṅkaḷil paṇipurivataṟkāka ivarkaḷai iṅkuk kuṭiyēṟṟiṉārkaḷ. atuvaraiyil annilaṅkaḷil paṇipurintu vantavarkaḷ pulaiyar eṉṉum kulattār. avarkaḷ cōmpēṟikaḷ. kuṉṉuvar avarkaḷait turattiviṭṭu, avarkaḷ iṭattaik kaippaṟṟik koṇṭaṉar. atōṭu avarkaḷai māṟāta aṭimaikaḷākavum ākkikkoṇṭaṉar.

kuṉṉuvar vāḻum ovvōr ūrilum avarkaḷuṭaiya camūkaviyalai mēṟpārkka oru talaivaṉuṇṭu. avaṉukku ‘maṇṇāṭi' eṉṟu peyar. ūril avaṉ mikavum celvākku mikkavaṉākak karutappaṭukiṟāṉ. ivviṉattār, kuṉṉuvar, periya kuṉṉuvar, ciṉṉak kuṉṉuvar eṉa muppirivākap pirintu vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. ippirivukaḷukku 'vakuppu'kkaḷ eṉṟu peyar. oru vakuppiṉar maṟṟa vakuppiṉarōṭu maṇa uṟavu koḷḷum vaḻakkam illai. āṉāl oṉṟāka amarntu uṇṇuvar. kuṉṉuvap peṇ aṇiyum uṭai oru mātiriyāka irukkum. avarkaḷ taṅkaḷuṭaiya mēlāṭaiyāl mārpaip pōrttu muṉṉāl muṭiyiṭṭuk koṇṭu, avvāṭaiyaiyē iṭuppil cuṟṟikkoḷkiṉṟaṉar. ulōkattāl ceyta kaḻuttaṇikaḷaiyum, pittaḷaiyāl ceyta vaḷaiyal, kolucukaḷaiyum, veḷḷiyiṉāṟ ceyta kaṭakam mūkku vaḷaikaḷaiyum aṇikiṉṟaṉar. veḷḷai āṭaiyai muṉṉāṭkaḷil peṇkaḷ aṇiyakkūṭātu eṉṟa kaṭṭuppāṭu iruntatu. āṉāl ippoḻutu perumpālōr veḷḷai āṭaiyē uṭukkiṉṟaṉar.

ivarkaḷiṭaiyilum māṉarīkamuṟai mikavum kaṭṭuppāṭṭōṭu kaṭaippiṭikkappaṭukiṟatu. tantaiyōṭu piṟanta attaiyiṉ makaḷē perumpālum maṇamakaḷākat tērnteṭukkappaṭukiṟāḷ. vayatup poruttam kūṭa ivarkaḷāl kavaṉikkappaṭuvatillai. cila camayaṅkaḷil maṇamakaṉaiviṭa maṇamakaḷ mikavum vayatil mūttavaḷāka iruppatuṇṭu. mikavum iḷaiñaṉāṉa oruvaṉukku oṉṟuviṭṭa attaimārkaḷiṉ vīṭṭiliruntu, iraṇṭu mūṉṟu peṇkaḷait tirumaṇam ceytu vaippatuṇṭu. paruvamaṭaiyāta acciṟuvaṉaiviṭa, vayatil mūttirukkum maṉaiviyar, avarkaḷ kulattilē viruppamuḷḷa vēṟu āṭavarkaḷōṭu toṭarpu koḷḷuvārkaḷ. avvāṟu toṭarpu koḷḷuvataṉ mūlam piṟakkum kuḻantaikaḷ caṭṭappaṭi kaṇavaṉukkē piṟantavaikaḷākak karutappaṭum. ataṉāl oṉpatu allatu pattu vayatuḷḷa cila ciṟuvarkaḷ (Putative fathers) iraṇṭu mūṉṟu kuḻantaiyarukkut tantaiyarāka iruppatuṇṭu. ivarkaḷuṭaiya tirumaṇam caṭaṅkukaḷaṟṟatu. maṇamakaṉiṉ peṟṟōr maṇamakaḷukkup paricam (Bride Price) vaḻaṅkuvar. maṇamakaṉiṉ tamakkai peṇṇiṉ kaḻuttil tāliyaik kaṭṭuvāḷ. piṟaku ellōrum viruntuṇpar. ittuṭaṉ tirumaṇam muṭivaṭaikiṟatu.

‘vīṭṭu vaippu' eṉṟa putumaiyāṉa muṟaiyoṉṟu ivarkaḷāl kaṭaippiṭittu varappaṭukiṟatu. ētēṉum oru kuṭumpattil āṇ cantatiyillāmal oru peṇ maṭṭum iruntāl appeṇṇai yārukkum tirumaṇam ceytu koṭuppatillai. avvāṟu tirumaṇam ceytu koṭuttāl attōṭu avarkaḷ kuṭi arukiviṭṭatāka eṇṇukiṟārkaḷ. attakaiya peṇṇai maṇam pēca muṟai māppiḷḷai (māmaṉ makaṉ) yārum varakkūṭātu. appeṇṇai avaḷuṭaiya vīṭṭiṉ vāyililuḷḷa oru kampattiṟkut tirumaṇam ceytu vaippārkaḷ. avaḷ kaḻuttil tāli kaṭṭuvataṟkup patilāka, avaḷuṭaiya valatu kaiyil veḷḷiyiṉāṟ ceyta vaḷaiyal oṉṟai aṇivippārkaḷ. ataṉ piṟaku appeṇ avaḷ kulattaic cārnta enta āṭavaṉuṭaṉum toṭarpu koḷḷalām. avaḷuṭaiya varumāṉam peṟṟōraiyē cērum. avaḷukku āṇ kuḻantai piṟantāl, akkuṭumpattiṟkuriya cottu muḻuvatum ataiyē cārum, mēṟku pellāriyilum, tarvār, maicūr ākiya nāṭukaḷukku aṇmaiyilum vāḻum paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷ kaṭaippiṭikkum 'pacavimuṟai'yōṭu iv vīṭṭu vaippu muṟai ottuḷḷatu.

maṇavilakku muṟaiyum ivarkaḷiṭaiyē uṇṭu. paricat tokaiyait tiruppik koṭuttuviṭṭāl uṭaṉē maṇavilakkup peṟalām. āṉāl kuḻantaikaḷ ellām kaṇavaṉaiyē cārum. kaimpeṇkaḷum, maṇavilakkup peṟṟavarkaḷum mika viraivil maṟumaṇam ceytu koḷḷukiṉṟaṉar, ākaiyiṉāl maṟṟa iṉamakkaḷ ippaḻakkattai iḻittup pēcukiṉṟaṉar.

pulaiyar :

paḻaṉimalaiyil mutaṉ mutal kuṭiyēṟiyavarkaḷ pulaiyarkaḷē. kuṉṉuvar immalaiyil kuṭiyēṟuvataṟku muṉ ivarkaḷ iṅku urimai vāḻvu vāḻntu vantaṉar. ivarkaḷ iṉattāl tamiḻarkaḷē. ivarkaḷ kaṭaippiṭittu oḻukum paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷ, camaveḷi yil vāḻum makkaḷiṉ paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷōṭu peritum ottirukkiṉṟaṉa. ivarkaḷ pēcum moḻiyum tamiḻē. ikkulattāriṉ talaivaṉ nāṭṭāṇmaikkāraṉ eṉṟu aḻaikkappaṭukiṟāṉ. avaṉukkut tuṇaiyākac cērvaikkāraṉ, tōṭṭi eṉṟa iruvar uḷḷaṉar. tōṭṭi nāṭṭāṇmaikkāraṉiṉ paṇiyāḷ. tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉ maṟṟa iṭaṅkaḷil vāḻum nāṭṭāṇmaikkāraraip pōlavē, ikkulattāriṉ nāṭṭāṇmaikkāraṉum celvākku mikkavaṉāka irukkiṟāṉ. pulaiyarkaḷ mūṉṟu piriviṉarākap pirintu vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. ovvoru piriviṟkum ‘kūṭṭam' eṉṟu peyar. kōlaṉkuppaṉ kūṭṭam, pīccik kūṭṭam, maṇṭiyāṉ kūṭṭam eṉpavaiyē avai. mēṟkūṟiya mūṉṟu pērkaḷum avarkaḷ kulattiṉ muṉṉōrkaḷ.

oru peṇ paruvamaṭainta piṟakē, tirumaṇam ceykiṉṟaṉar. tirumaṇam peṟṟōrkaḷālēyē ēṟpāṭu ceyyappaṭukiṟatu. tirumaṇac caṭaṅku mikavum eḷiya muṟaiyilēyē naṭaipeṟukiṟatu. maṇappeṇṇukku rū. 25 paricappaṇamāka aḷikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. veḷḷai maṇikaḷaik kōttup peṇṇiṉ kaḻuttil, tāliyāka aṇivikkiṉṟaṉar. paricappaṇattiṟku īṭāṉa tokaiyai oṟuppuk kaṭṭaṇa (fine) mākac celuttiviṭṭu āṇ, peṇ ākiya iruvaril yār vēṇṭumāṉalum maṇavilakkup peṟalām. maṇavilakkup peṟṟavarkaḷum, kaimpeṇkaḷum tām virumpum yāraiyum tirumaṇam ceytu koḷḷalām. pulaiyarkaḷ māyāṇṭi, karu malaiyāṉ, pūvāṭai (peṇ teyvam) eṉṟa teyvaṅkaḷai vaṇaṅkukiṉṟaṉar. ovvoru ciṟṟūrukkum arukil amaintirukkum tiṭaliṉmēl māyāṇṭiyiṉ kōvil irukkum. ikkaṭavuḷarkaḷukkup pulaiyarkaḷ cittirait tiṅkaḷil viḻā eṭukkiṉṟaṉar. ivviḻāvil paṉṉiraṇṭu āṭavarkaḷ cērntu āṭum āṭṭam mikavum kuṟippiṭattakkatu. ivvāṭṭattil paṅku koḷḷum paṉṉiraṇṭu āṭavarum taṅkaḷait tūymaiyōṭu vaittuk koḷvataṟkāka, māṭṭiṟaicciyai uṇṇāmal vilakkukiṉṟaṉar. viḻāveṭutta mutal nāḷil māyāṇṭikku ōr āṭu paliyiṭukiṉṟaṉar. iraṇṭām nāḷ oru pāṉaiyil rākik kaḷi camaittu, karumalaiyāṉ kōvilil vaittu ataic cuṟṟi āṭuvārkaḷ. āṭal muṭintatum ellōrukkum kaḷi vaḻaṅkappaṭum. mūṉṟām nāḷ pūvāṭai kōvilil paṭaiyal tuvaṅki eṭṭu nāḷ toṭarntu naṭaipeṟum. viḻā muṭivuṟumpōtu, āṭal ciṟappāka naṭaipeṟum. pulaiyarkaḷ āṭalil peruviruppam koṇṭavarkaḷ. paṅkuṉit tiṅkaḷil āṭavarum peṇṭirum tiraḷākak kūṭi, koṭṭum paṟaiyōcaikkēṟpac cuṟṟiyāṭum āṭṭam kāṇṭaṟkuriyatu. pulaiyarkaḷ māṭṭiṟaicciyaiyum, paṉṟiyiṟaicciyaiyum virumpiyuṇkiṉṟaṉar. eliyaik kūṭa uṇṇum paḻakkam avarkaḷiṭam uṇṭu. 'yārēṉum oruvaṉ periyammaiyāl pīṭikkappaṭṭāl, uṟṟārum uṟaviṉarum avaṉai appaṭiyē viṭṭuviṭṭu ōṭiviṭuvārkaḷ. āṭavarum peṇṭirum uṭal toṭarpu koḷḷamāṭṭārkaḷ' eṉṟu ṭarṉpul eṉṟa veḷḷaiyar tam nūlil kuṟippiṭukiṟār. kalrāyaṉ malaikaḷil vāḻum malaiyāḷikaḷiṭamum ippaḻakkam uṇṭu

kiruttava camayattaip parappum nōkkattōṭu ki, pi, 1850-ām āṇṭil oru pātiri aṉuppappaṭṭār. āṉāl appaṇi carivara naṭaipeṟavillai. iṭaiyilē taṭaippaṭṭatu. mīṇṭum amerikkat tiruccapaiyaic cārnta kiruttavap pātirimār iṅku camayattaip parappap perum muyaṟci eṭuttu varukiṉṟaṉar. ki. pi. 1850-ām āṇṭu camayap piracārattiṟkākap pulaiyarkaḷiṭaiyē ceṉṟa pātiriyār pulaiyarkaḷaip paṟṟiya cila kuṟippukaḷait tam kaṭitaṅkaḷil eḻutiyuḷḷār, Madras Quarterly Missionary Journal for 1850-55-eṉṟa nūlil akkaṭitaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. akkaṭi taṅkaḷil kāṇappaṭum ceytikaḷ piṉ varumāṟu :

'vēṭṭaiyāṭuvataṟkākap pulaiyarkaḷ cila camayaṅkaḷil tiraḷākak kūṭuvatuṇṭu. vēṭṭaiyil kiṭaitta mutal vilaṅkiṉ tōlaiyō allatu vēṟu cila uṟuppukkaḷaiyō arukiluḷḷa kōvilukkuk kāṇikkaiyāka aṉuppuvar. avvāṟu ceyvataṉ mūlam, avarkaḷuṭaiya kaṭavuḷ maṉa niṟaivu koṇṭu avarkaḷukku vēṭṭaiyil atikamāṉa vilaṅkukaḷ kiṭaippataṟku aruḷpurivārām. vēṭṭaiyiṉ pōtu yārēṉum iṟantupaṭṭāl, mikavum mariyātaiyōṭu avaṉ uṭalaik kāṭṭil aṭakkam ceyvārkaḷ. avvāṟu iyantapaṭṭavaṉ. pulaiyarkaḷiṉ mariyātaikkuriyavaṉākavum, vaḻipāṭṭiṟkuriyavaṉākavum karutappaṭukiṉṟāṉ. pulaiyarkaḷ, avarkaḷuṭaiya āṇṭai (Masters) kaḷāṉa kuṉṉuvarkaḷāl mikavum aṭimaittaṉattil āḻttappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉar. kuṉṉuvar pulaiyarkaḷukkuk kaṭum vaṭṭikkup paṇam koṭuttu, avarkaḷāl koṭukka iyalātapōtu, taṅkaḷ aṭimaikaḷāka ākkik koḷkiṉṟaṉar. kuṉṉuvarkaḷ avarkaḷai iravil viḷakku vaittuk koḷvataṟkum, kaṭṭilil paṭuttuṟaṅkuvataṟkumkūṭa aṉumatippatillai. nōy noṭikaḷait tīrppataṟkuk kuṉṉuvarkaḷ pulaiyarkaḷaiyē peritum nāṭavēṇṭiyirukkiṟatu. ēṉeṉṟāl kāṭṭiluḷḷa mūlikaikaḷiṉ taṉmaikaḷai naṉkuṇarntavarkaḷ pulaiyarkaḷē, pēy piṭittavarkaḷaik kuṇappaṭuttum tiṟamaiyum avarkaḷiṭamē uḷḷatu. ēṉeṉṟāl ammalai mītu vāḻum pēy picācukaḷai vacappaṭuttum āṟṟalai avarkaḷ peṟṟiruppatākak kuṉṉuvarkaḷ karutukiṉṟaṉar. pulikaḷai nañcūṭṭik kollum kalaiyaip pulaiyarkaḷ naṉku aṟivārkaḷ. yārēṉum oru pulaiyaṉ oru puliyai nañcūṭṭik koṉṟa ceytiyai, maṟṟa pulaiyarkaḷ aṟintavuṭaṉ ellōriṭamum paṇam vacūlittu avaṉukkup puttāṭai vāṅki vaḻaṅkuvar. avaṉai oru nāṟkāliyil amartti ūrvalamākak koṇṭu varuvar. ūrvalattiṉpōtu avaṉaic cuṟṟip palar āṭikkoṇṭu varuvar.

paḷiyar :

paḷiyarkaḷ, paḻaṉimalaiyil vāḻvōril mikavum piṟpaṭṭa iṉattār. mēl paḻaṉimalaiyilum, varucanāṭṭup paḷḷattākkilum uḷḷa kāṭukaḷil, ciṟu ciṟu kūṭṭaṅkaḷākac citaṟi vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. mañcampaṭṭikku arukiluḷḷa kāṭukaḷilum vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. āṉāl ivarkaḷuṭaiya uccarippu mikavum vēṟupaṭṭum, purintukoḷḷa muṭiyāmalum amaintuḷḷatu. pulaiyarkaḷaiviṭa nākarikattil ivarkaḷ mikavum kēvalamāṉa nilaiyil uḷḷaṉar. āṉāl ivarkaḷ māṭṭiṟaicciyai uṇpatillai. perumpālum ivarkaḷ marattiṉ mēl amaitta paraṇkaḷilum, kukaikaḷilum pāṟai iṭukkukaḷilum vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. cila camayaṅkaḷil puṟkaḷāl amaikkappaṭṭa kuṭicaikaḷil vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. uṭalai maṟaikkap pōtiya aḷavaṟṟa āṭaiyaiyē aṇikiṉṟaṉar. avvāṭaiyōṭu taḻaikaḷaiyum, puṟkaḷaiyum cērttu iṭuppil cuṟṟik koḷkiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷ āṭai mikavum aḻukkēṟit tūymaiyaṟṟirukkum. ilaikaḷaiyum, kiḻaṅkukaḷaiyum (perumpālum maravaḷḷik kiḻaṅku) tēṉaiyumē mukkiya uṇavākak koḷkiṉṟaṉar. kiḻaṅkukaḷai oru kuḻiyiṉuḷ pōṭṭu, ataṉmēl cuḷḷikaḷaip parappit tīyiṭṭuc cuṭṭut tiṉkiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷ iruppiṭattiṟku muṉṉāl eppoḻutum tī erintukoṇṭē irukkum. kāṭṭu vilaṅkukaḷiṭamiruntu taṅkaḷait taṟkāttuk koḷḷavē avvāṟu ceykiṉṟaṉar.



kāṭṭil erintukoṇṭirukkum tīyiṉ mūlamākavē, paḷiyarkaḷ avviṭattil irukkiṟārkaḷ eṉṟu aṟintukoḷḷa muṭiyum. ēṉeṉṟāl paḷiyarkaḷ mikavum añcum iyalpiṉar. piṟa iṉa makkaḷaik kāṇa avarkaḷ virumpuvatillai. avvāṟu kaṇṭālum ōṭi oḷintu koḷkiṉṟaṉar. cakkikmukkik kalliṉ utaviyiṉālēyē ivarkaḷ neruppu uṇṭākkukiṉṟaṉar. ataṟkut tuṇaip poruḷākak kāṭṭumaraṅkaḷil kiṭaikkum pañcu pōṉṟa poruḷaip payaṉpaṭuttukiṉṟaṉar. tirumaṇaṅkaḷ evvitac caṭaṅkumiṉṟi naṭaipeṟukiṉṟaṉa. 'tirumaṇam ōr icainta ēṟpāṭu' (Marriage is an adjustment) eṉṟu oru mēlai nāṭṭu aṟiñaṉ kūṟiya kūṟṟu ivarkaḷ vāḻkkaikku mikavum poruttamē, ivarkaḷ vāḻkkaiyil evvita iṭarppāṭum kiṭaiyātu. kaṇavaṉ uṇavup poruḷait tēṭik koṇara vēṇṭum. maṉaivi ataic camaittuk koṭukka vēṇṭum. ivarkaḷuṭaiya vāḻkkaip poṟuppukkaḷ ivaṟṟōṭu muṭivuṟukiṉṟaṉa. avarkaḷil yārēṉum oruvar iṟantuviṭṭāl, appiṇattai irunta iṭattilēyē viṭṭuviṭṭu ellōrum vēṟu iṭattiṟkuc ceṉṟu viṭuvārkaḷ. avviṭattiṟkuc cila tiṅkaḷ varaiyil yārum cellamāṭṭārkaḷ. tiruvāḷar tarsṭaṉ eṉṟa oru veḷḷaiyar, tirunelvēlik kāṭukaḷil vāḻum paḷiyarkaḷiṉ vāḻkkaiyaip paṟṟi virivāṉa kuṟippukaḷaip paṭaṅkaḷōṭu oru nūlāka veḷiyiṭṭirukkiṟār. tirunelvēlip paḷiyarkaḷ iṟantavaṉaip putaittu, apputaikuḻiyiṉmēl oru kallai nāṭṭiviṭṭuc ceṉṟu viṭuvar. piṟaku avviṭattai akkuṭumpattār eppōtum kāṇa virumpuvatillai, mañcampaṭṭikku arukiluḷḷa malaip pakutikaḷil ivarkaḷ vāḻkiṉṟaṉar,



kōṭaikkāṉal :

kōṭaikkāṉal vaṭṭam (taluk) maturai māvaṭṭattaic cārntatākum. āṅkilēyarkaḷ kīḻp paḻaṉi malai, mēṟpaḻaṉi malai ākiya iru pakutikaḷaiyum cērttu ki. pi. 1889 ām āṇṭu ivvaṭṭattai amaittaṉar. mēṟpaḻaṉimalaiyiṉ mēl amaintirukkum kōṭaikkāṉal nakarattiṉ peyarāl ivvaṭṭamum aḻaikkappaṭukiṟatu.

peyark kāraṇam :

kōṭaikkāṉal eṉṟa peyar araciyalāriṉ kuṟippukkaḷil ki. pi. 1860-ām āṇṭiṟku mēl kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. ippeyar innakarattiṟku evvāṟu ēṟpaṭṭatu eṉṟu tiṭṭavaṭṭamākak kūṟa muṭiyātu. 'kāṉal' eṉṟa collukkuk kāṭu allatu cōlai eṉṟu poruḷ. itil evvita aiyappāṭum illai. āṉāl ippeyaril amaintirukkum nilai moḻi (muṉ col) yāṉa 'kōṭai' etaik kuṟikciṟatu eṉpatē ārāyccikkuriyatāka irukkiṟatu. innakaram amaintuḷḷa kāṭu, paḻaṉimalaikaḷiṉ kaṭaiciyil uḷḷa toṭariṉmītu iruppatāl itu "kōṭikkāṉal' eṉṟu mutalil aḻaikkappaṭṭup piṟaku kōṭaikkāṉal āyiṟṟu eṉpar. vēṟu cilar 'koṭikkāṉal' eṉṟa peyarē kōṭaikkāṉal āyiṟṟu eṉpar. innakaraic cūḻntirukkum kāṭṭil, piṉṉip paṭarntirukkum eṇṇaṟṟa koṭikaḷum, liyāṉā (Liana) mutaliya kāṭṭumaraṅkaḷiṉ toṅkum vērkaḷum, ippeyarai uṟutippaṭuttukiṉṟaṉa. kōṭaik kālattilum - pacumaiyāṉa iḷamarakkāṭukaḷaip peṟṟiruppatāl, itu kōṭaikkāṉal eṉap peyar peṟṟatu eṉṟu kūṟuvārumuṇṭu. caṅka ilakkiyattil kūṭap pacumarak kāṭṭaik kuṟippiṭak 'kōṭaikkāṉal' eṉṟa toṭar āḷappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. 'koṭaikkāṉalē' kōṭaikkāṉal ākiyirukkum eṉṟu cilar eṇṇukiṉṟaṉar. āṉāl itu yārukku yārāl koṭaip poruḷāka aḷikkappaṭṭatu eṉpatu viḷaṅkavillai. kōṭaikkāṉal perumaḻaip piratēcattil amaintuḷḷatu. āṇṭukku 91 aṅkula maḻaiyaip peṟukiṟatu. iṅku vāḻum makkaḷ eppōtum kuṭaiyum kaiyumākat tirivatāl 'kuṭaikkāṉal' eṉṟa peyarē kōṭaik kāṉalāka māṟiyirukkum eṉṟum kūṟalām.

vaḷarcciyum varalāṟum :

kōṭaikkāṉal nakar periyakuḷattiṟku nēr ucciyil kaṭal maṭṭattiliruntu 7000 aṭi uyarattilamaintuḷḷatu. ceṅkuttāka uyarntu cellum teṉpakka ucciyiṉ ōrattil innakaram uḷḷatu. malai ucciyil tāḻvāṉa iṭattaic cuṟṟiyuḷḷa carivukaḷil innakaram kaṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. itaṉ nīḷam 2 kal; akalam 1 kal. ivvucciyiliruntu kāṇpōrukkuc camaveḷiyiṉ tōṟṟam teḷivākat tōṉṟum. innakariṉ vaṭakkil malaiyāṉatu uyarntum, ceṅkuttākavum cellukiṟatu. mēṟkil uyarnta malaittoṭar taṭuttu niṟkiṉṟatu. kiḻakkup pakkattil malaiyāṉatu ciṟitu ciṟitākat tāḻntu kīḻppaḻaṉi malaikaḷai nōkkic cellukiṟatu. appakkattil tāṉ maṟṟa ellāc cikaraṅkaḷaiyumviṭa uyarntu aḻakiya tōṟṟattōṭu perumāḷ malai niṟkiṟatu. itaṉ uyaram 7326 aṭi. kōṭaikkāṉal amaintuḷḷa carivukaḷil teṉpakkattuc carivu, pacu maraṅkaḷait taṉṉakattil niṟaiyak koṇṭirukkiṟatu. tolaivil iruntu kāṇpōrukkut toṅkum tōṭṭamāka {hanging wood) atu teṉpaṭum. ikkāṭē kōṭaikkāṉal eṉṟu aḻaikkappaṭṭu, nakarukkum āki vantatu. nakarukku naṭuvil uḷḷa tāḻviṭam mutalil catuppu nilamāka iruntatu. oru ciṟṟōṭai ataṉ naṭuvil ūrntu ceṉṟu koṇṭiruntatu. maturai māvaṭṭat taṇṭalarāka irunta tiruvāḷar liveñ (Mr. Levenge) eṉpār, ki. pi. 1863 ām āṇṭu tiṭṭamiṭṭu, araciyalāriṉ naṉkoṭaiyaiyum, tam kaippaṇattaiyum celaviṭṭu acciṟṟōṭaiyaic cuṟṟik karai amaittu, ēriyāka māṟṟiṉār. aḻakiya avvēriyaic cuṟṟip pacumaraṅkaḷ aṭarnta vaḻikaḷ pala amaintuḷḷaṉa. avvaḻikaḷilēyē eḻilpeṟu maṉaikaḷ pala eḻuppappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivvēri uruvattāl oru naṭcattira mīṉai (Star fish) ottu irukkiṟatu. itaṉ kuṟukkaḷavu ½ kal irukkum. āṉāl itaṉ cuṟṟaḷavu 3 kal nīḷam irukkum. ērikkaraiyaic cuṟṟi oṭṭiṉāṟpōl oru pātai cellukiṟatu. appātaikkuc caṟṟu mēlē oru pātaiyum, ataṟku mēl maṟṟoru pātaiyum carivukaḷil amaintuḷḷaṉa. avaikaḷ muṟaiyē 'naṭu ērip pātai' (Middle Lake Road) eṉṟum, 'mēl ērip pātai' (Upper Lake Road) eṉṟum aḻaikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. immūṉṟu peruñcālaikaḷum, iṭaiyiṭaiyē pala ciṟu pātaikaḷāl iṇaikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.

nakarukku veḷiyil aintu mukkiyap pātaikaḷ cellukiṉṟaṉa. teṉ mēṟkākac cellum pātai tūṇ pāṟai (Pillar Rocks) kaḷai nōkkic cellukiṟatu. mēṟkup pakkattil cellum pātai, 12 kal tolaivil amaintirukkum 'pūmpāṟai' eṉṉum ciṟṟūrai aṭaikiṟatu. avviṭattil ciṟanta vāṉāyvuk kūṭam oṉṟu uḷḷatu. vaṭakkup pakkattil cellum naṭaipātai tirunelvēlik kuṭiyēṟṟa (Tinnevelli settlement)ttiṉ vaḻiyāka vilpaṭṭiyai aṭaikiṟatu. nīrvīḻccikku aṇmaiyil ceṅkuttāṉa carivukaḷukkiṭaiyil ivvaḻakiya ciṟṟūr amaintuḷḷatu. kiḻakkup pakkattil cellum 'lāskāṭ' eṉṉum vaḻiyāṉatu mēṟpaḻaṉi malaiyaiyum kīḻppaḻaṉi malaiyaiyum pirikkum iyaṟkai ellaiyāṉa niyūṭral cēṭal (Neutral Saddle) eṉṟa iṭattaiyaṭaikiṟatu. ivviṭam perumāḷ cikarattiṉ aṭivārattiluḷḷatu. teṟkil cellum pātai kutiraip pātaiyākum. itu ceṅkuttāka amaintuḷḷatu. 12 kal nīḷamuḷḷa ippātai, ceṇpakaṉūr aṭivārattiluḷḷa kiṣṭṉamā nāyakkar tōppu (Kistnama Nayak's Tope) vaḻiyākac camaveḷiyai aṭaikiṟatu. kiṣṭṉamā nāyakkar tōppu, (potuvākat tōppu eṉṟē aḻaikkap peṟum) maturaiyil nāyakka maṉṉariṭam amaiccarākap paṇiyāṟṟiya oruvaruṭaiya uṟaviṉar peyarāl amaikkappaṭṭatu. maturaiyil nāyakkar āṭci aḻivuṟṟatum, apparamparaiyiṉar periya kuḷattiṟku ōṭivantu taṅkiṉar. avarkaḷ paramparaiyiṉarē toṭarntu ki. pi. 1870-ām āṇṭuvarai, vaṭakarai eṉṉum ūrukkuk kirāmat talaivarkaḷākap paṇiyāṟṟi vantaṉar.

it tōppiliruntu periyakuḷam 5 kal tolaivil amaintuḷḷatu. periya kuḷattiliruntu ammaiya nāyakkaṉūr pukai vaṇṭi nilaiyam 28 kal tolaiviluḷḷatu. paṇṭaikkālattil kōṭaik kāṉalai aṭaiya virumpuvōr ammaiya nāyakkaṉūriliruntu tōppiṟkuc cellum 33 kal tolaivai māṭṭu vaṇṭiyilēyē kaṭantu cella vēṇṭi yiruntatu. tōppiliruntu puṟappaṭṭuk kutiraiyil ēṟiyō, naṭantō, ceṇpakaṉūr vaḻiyāka malaiyucciyai yaṭaiya vēṇṭum. naṭantu cella muṭiyātavarkaḷai, paḻaṉimalaip pulaiyarkaḷ tiṟanta pallakkup (Canvas Chair) pōṉṟa irukkaiyil amarttit tūkkic celvar. cellumpōtu avarkaḷ taṅkaḷ moḻiyil urakkap pāṭikkoṇṭē. vaḻi naṭaip payaṇattai maṟantu celvar.



utakamaṇṭalattiliruppataip pōṉṟē kōṭaik kāṉalilum taṭpa veppa nilai uḷḷattaik kavarum taṉmaiyatāy amaintuḷḷatu. āṉāl utakaiyil peyvataiviṭa iṅku maḻai atikam. āṇṭukku ēṟakkuṟaiya 100 aṅkulam maḻai poḻikiṟatu. utakai teṉmēṟkup paruvak kāṟṟiṉāl jūṉ, jūlai, ākasṭut tiṅkaḷkaḷil maḻaiyaip peṟukiṟatu. āṉāl kōṭaikkāṉal vaṭa kiḻakkup paruvak kāṟṟiṉāl maḻaiyaip peṟukiṟatu. mē, jūṉ tiṅkaḷkaḷil pārvaiyāḷarkaḷ (Visitors) iṅku niṟaiyak kūṭukiṉṟaṉar. appōtu maḻai peytu avarkaḷukkut tollai koṭuppatillai. āṉāl atē camayattil utakai ceṉṟāl nāḷ tōṟum maḻaiyiṉāl avatippaṭa nēriṭum. kacivum, veppa nilai vīḻcciyum utakaiyiliruppataip pōl avvaḷavu atikamāka iṅku iruppatillai. kuḷir kālattilkūṭa iṅkuk kuḷiriṉ kaṭumai mikavum atikamillai eṉṟē collalām. iṅkuḷḷa nilam maṇalum kallum kalantatāka irukkiṟatu. ataṉāl iṅkuḷḷa pātaikaḷum, maṭṭaip pantāṭṭa maitāṉaṅkaḷum, maḻai peyta ciṟitu nērattil ularntu viṭukiṉṟaṉa. kōṭaikkāṉalukku mēṟkil aṭuttaṭuttu amaintuḷḷa ceṅkuttāṉa malait toṭarkaḷ, avaikaṭkappāluḷḷa aṭarnta kāṭukaḷai maṟaittu niṟkiṉṟaṉa. ik kāṭukaḷukkuḷ eḷitāka yārum cella muṭiyātu. ik kāṭukaḷilum, kōṭaikkāṉaluk karukiluḷḷa kāṭukaḷilum varalāṟṟuk kālattukku muṟpaṭṭa kaṟ karuvikaḷum, camātikaḷum kaṇṭaṟiyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. perumāḷ cikarattiṟkut teṉmēṟkup pakutiyiluḷḷa kāṭukaḷilum (vilpaṭṭi cellum pātaiyil) pālamalaiyilum ivaikaḷ mikutiyākak kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. kīḻp paḻaṉi malaiyil maccūr, paṇṇaikkāṭu, tāṇṭikkuṭi, kāmaṉūr, pāccalūr mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷilum ivaikaḷ niṟaiyat teṉpaṭuvatāka ārāycciyāḷarkaḷ veḷiyiṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉar. ic ciṉṉaṅkaḷaip pōl intiya nāṭṭiṉ vēṟu ep pakutiyilum kiṭaikkavillai. ic camātikaḷ perumpālum 8 aṭi nīḷamum 3 aṭi akalamum uṭaiyaṉavāy coracorappāṉa kaṟkaḷāl peṭṭi pōl ceyyappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. oru pakkam tiṟantirukkiṟatu. turup piṭitta arivāḷ oṉṟum, cenniṟa, karuniṟa maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷum kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. kōṭaikkāṉalaic cuṟṟik kāṇat takunta pala iṭaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. nakaraic cuṟṟi amaintuḷḷa mūṉṟu nīr vīḻccikaḷ kuṟippiṭattakkavai ; kaṇkavarum vaṉappuṭaiyavai. ivaikaḷai yaṭaitaṟku nalla vaḻikaḷum amaintuḷḷaṉa. lā malait toṭaril (Law's ghat) veḷḷi vīḻcci (Silver 'cascade) amaintuḷḷatu. itu parappāṟu eṉṉum aruviyāl uṇṭākiṟatu. kiḷeṉ vīḻcci (Glan falls) yāṉatu vilpaṭṭikku vaṭakkil cellum ciṟu pātaiyarukil amaintuḷḷatu. ituvum parappāṟṟiṉ oru piriviṉālēyē uṇṭākkappaṭukiṟatu. kōṭaikkāṉaliṉ teṉmēṟkil pāmpāṟṟiṉāl mōkiṉi vīḻcci (Fairy Falls) uṇṭākkappaṭukiṟatu. kōkkars naṭaiveḷi (Coaker's walk) yāṉatu, kōṭaik kāṉaliṉ ellaiyil, paḻaṉi malaiyiṉ teṉpakkac cariviṉ ucciyil amaintuḷḷatu. (leḵpṭiṉeṇṭ kōkkar eṉpavar ōr ciṟanta poṟiyiyal vallunar. ivar ki. pi. 1870 mutal 1872 varai maturai māvaṭṭattil paṇiyāṟṟi vantār. ki. pi. 1870-ām āṇṭu kōṭaikkāṉaliṉ paṭattai (Map) varaintu veḷi yiṭṭavar ivarē). in naṭai veḷiyiliruntu kāṇ pōrukkuc camaveḷiyil amaintuḷḷa ūrkaḷ aḻakōṭu kāṭciyaḷikkiṉṟaṉa. vāṉam nirmalamāka irukkum nāṭkaḷil 47 kal tolaivil amaintuḷḷa maturai nakaram kūṭat teṉpaṭuvatākak kūṟukiṟārkaḷ, at teṉpakkap pīṭa pūmiyilēyē, kōkkar naṭai veḷiyiliruntu 3 kal ceṉṟāl tūṇ pāṟaikaḷaik kāṇalām. mūṉṟu uyaramāṉa parutta pāṟaikaḷ perum perum tūṇkaḷaippōl uyarntu niṟkiṉṟaṉa. ivaikaḷiṉ uyaram ēṟakkuṟaiya 400 aṭi irukkum. ivaṟṟiṉ naṭuvilum aṭiyilum pala kukaikaḷum, piḷavukaḷum amaintuḷḷaṉa. ip pāṟaikaḷiṉ ucciyiliruntu kāṇpōrukku atikamalaiyiṉ aḻakiya kāṭcikaḷum, kampam paḷḷattākkiliruntu uyarntu cellum ceṅkuttuc carivukaḷum, cama veḷiyum naṉku teriyum.

tūṇ pāṟaikaḷiliruntu iraṇṭu kal tolaivil ṭākṭars ṭilaiṭ {Doctor's Delight) eṉṟa uyarnta mēṭu oṉṟu uḷḷatu. itu tūṇ pāṟaikaḷaiviṭa mikunta pēraḻakōṭu viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu. kōṭaik kāṉaliliruntu 9½ kal tolaivil 'hēmilṭaṉ kōṭṭai ' (Fort Hamilton} uḷḷatu. mējar ṭakḷas hēmilṭaṉ eṉpavariṉ peyarāl itu aḻaikkappaṭukiṟatu. ceṉṉai mānilat talaivarāka viḷaṅkiya car cārlas ṭirevelyāṉ eṉpavariṉ āṇaiyiṉpaṭi ivar ki. pi. 1859, 1861; 1862 ākiya āṇṭukaḷil paḻaṉi malaiyil taṅki, ataṉ nilappaṭattai varaintār. appaṭam viḷakkamākavum, peritākavum uḷḷatu. potu nūlakaṅkaḷilum, aluvalakaṅkaḷilum ip paṭam toṅkik koṇṭirukkiṉṟatu. paḻaṉi malaiyaip paṟṟi avar eḻutiya iraṇṭu kuṟippukaḷ kuṟippiṭattakkavai. avai muṟaiyē ki. pi. 1862-ām āṇṭilum ki. pi. 1864-ām āṇṭilum ceṉṉaiyil acciyaṟṟi veḷiyiṭappaṭṭaṉa. hēmilṭaṉ kōṭṭai eṉṟu kuṟippiṭappaṭum iṭattil kōṭṭai etuvumillai. orē oru kuṭicaitāṉ uḷḷatu. avviṭam mutalil oru periya ēriyāka iruntatu eṉṟa cuvaiyāṉa uṇmaiyait tiruvāḷar hēmilṭaṉ mutaṉ mutalākak kaṇṭu veḷiyiṭṭār. ivvēriyaip paṟṟiya muṉ kuṟippukkaḷō, cevi vaḻic ceytikaḷō kūṭak kiṭaiyātu. nīr maṭṭam irunta aṭaiyāḷaṅkaḷ iṉṟum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avvaṭaiyāḷaṅkaḷai vaittukkoṇṭē avviṭattil ōr ēri iruntirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟu avar muṭivu kaṭṭiṉār. avvēri ēṟakkuṟaiya 5 kal nīḷamum, 1 kal mutal 2 kal varai akalamum, 30 aṭi mutal 70 aṭi varai āḻamum uṭaiyatāka iruntirukka vēṇṭum eṉṟu kaṇakkiṭṭirukkiṟār. oru paḷḷattākkiṉ kuṟukkē nīṇṭu paṭintirunta malait toṭar, iyaṟkaiyāka ivvēri amaivataṟkuk kāraṇamāka iruntatu. it toṭar vaṭakku nōkki amarāvati āṟuvarai cellukiṟatu. iv vēriyil viḻunta ōr aruviyait taṭuttu nīrai it toṭar tēkki vantatu. āṉāl, tiṭīreṉṟu aṇaipōl irunta at toṭar piḷavupaṭṭa kāraṇattāl ēriyil nīr tēṅkuvatillai. aṇaipōl taṭuttirunta at toṭar 200 keja nīḷam uḷḷatu. atil ēṟpaṭṭirukkum piḷavu 100 keja akalamum 90 aṭi āḻamum uḷḷatu. mējar hēmilṭaṉ, 'ivviṭam ōr aḻakiya kuṟiñci nakaramō , paṭaiyiṉariṉ pāṭiyō (Contonement) amaippataṟku mikavum ēṟṟatu' eṉak kuṟippiṭṭār. āṉāl ivviṭam ceṉṟaṭaitaṟkariyatu eṉṟu palar etirpput terivittaṉar. kōṭaikkāṉalāṉatu, taṟpōtu amaintirukkum iṭattil mutaṉ mutal kaṭṭappaṭṭa pōtu, 'iyaṟkai aḻakumikka evvaḷavō iṭaṅkaḷ paḻaṉi malai mītu irukka ivviṭattait tērnteṭuttatu aṟiyāmai' eṉṟu palar kuṟṟam cāṭṭiṉar. avarkaḷukkum itē kāraṇattaittāṉ kūṟa vēṇṭum. periya kuḷattiliruntu paḻaṉi malaiyiṉ ucciyai aṭaiyap puḻakkattilirunta kutiraip pātaikku aṇmaiyilēyē, in nakarai amaittaṉar. kāraṇam pōkku varavu vacatiyē.

paḻaṉi malaiyai mutaṉ mutalil pārvaiyiṭṭa airōppiyar leḵpṭiṉaṇṭ pi. es. vārṭu eṉpavar. ivar ki. pi. 1821-ām āṇṭu im malaiyai aḷappataṟkāka iṅku vantār. periya kuḷattiliruntu cellum pātaiyil puṟappaṭṭu, veḷḷakkavi eṉṉum ciṟṟūr vaḻiyāka malaiyucciyai aṭaintu avvāṇṭu mē tiṅkaḷ 25-ām nāḷ pāmpāṟu vīḻccikkuc caṟṟu mēlē taṅkiyiruntār eṉa avar kuṟippāl aṟiyappaṭukiṟatu. ak kuṟippil paḻaṉi malaiyai ‘varākakiri' eṉṟum tiruvāṅkūr malaikaḷaik 'kaṇṇaṉ tēvaṉ malaikaḷ' eṉṟum avar kuṟippiṭukiṟār.

- ki. pi. 1834-ām āṇṭu, maturai māvaṭṭat tuṇait taṇṭalarāka irunta je. ci. rāṭṭaṉ eṉpārum, teṉmaṇṭala naṭuvarāka (Judge of the Provincial court, Southern Division) irunta ci, ār. kāṭṭaṉ eṉpārum periya kuḷattiliruntu puṟappaṭṭuc ceṇpakaṉūrai vantaṭaintaṉar. avarkaḷ varavāl cīr kēṭuṟṟirunta akkutiraip pātai cīrtiruttam peṟṟatu. ki. pi. 1836-ām āṇṭu tiruvāḷar vaiṭ eṉpavar tēvatāṉap paṭṭiyiliruntu aṭukkam cikarattiṟku aṇmaiyiluḷḷa kaṇavāyai nōkkic cellum ceṅkuttāṉa kaṇavāy mūlam malaiyucciyai aṭaintār. avar ippōtu kōṭaikkāṉal amaintuḷḷa tāḻnilattiṟkuc cellavillai. avar paḻaṉimalaiyil uḷḷa tāvaraṅkaḷaip paṟṟi veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷa kuṟippukkaḷ kuṟippiṭat takkavai.

kōṭaikkāṉalil mutaṉ mutalākat taṅkuvataṟku māḷikaiyaik kaṭṭiyavarkaḷ, maturaiyil vāḻnta amerikkap pātirimārkaḷē (American missionaries). nam nāṭṭiṉ taṭpa veppanilai avarkaḷukku ottuvarāmaiyāl palar nōyvāyppaṭṭaṉar. ākaiyiṉāl avarkaḷuṭaiya kēntiramāṉa 'jaḵpṉā'viṟku nōyuṟṟavarkaḷaik koṇṭu cellavum, nōyiliruntu mīṇṭu kuṇamaṭaintavarkaḷai aḻaittuvaravum ki. pi. 1838ām āṇṭu oru ciṟu kappalē vilaikku vāṅka muṭivu ceytaṉar. maturaikku aṇmaiyiluḷḷa ciṟu malaiyiṉ mītu oru māḷikaiyai amaittu nōyuṟṟavarkaḷai aṅkuk koṇṭu cella muṭivu ceytatāl, at tiṭṭam kaiviṭappaṭṭatu. ciṟu malaiyiṉmītu kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ eḻuppappaṭṭaṉa. āṉāl aṅkuc ceṉṟavarkaḷ malaik kāyccaliṉāl aṭikkaṭi avatippaṭṭaṉar. ākaiyiṉāl avviṭattaik kaiviṭṭu viṭṭup paḻaṉi malaiyiṉ mītu ki. pi. 1845-ām āṇṭu ōriṭattait tērnteṭuttaṉar. kōṭaikkāṉal eṉṉum iḷa marakkāṭu - amaintirunta cariviṉ aṭivārattil iraṇṭu kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ eḻuppappaṭṭaṉa. kaṭṭiṭa vēlai jūṉ tiṅkaḷil toṭaṅkap peṟṟu akṭōparil muṭivuṟṟatu.

ki. pi. 1834 mutal 1847 varai maturai māvaṭṭat taṇṭalarākap paṇiyāṟṟiya tiruvāḷar jāṉ piḷēkparṉ eṉpavar paḻaṉi malaiyiṉ mītu nilavarit tiṭṭattai amulākkap peru muyaṟci eṭuttuk koṇṭār. aṭukkam kaṇavāyiṉ ucciyiliruntu 5 kal tolaivil, tām taṅkuvataṟkeṉṟu oru māḷikaiyai amaittār. āṉāl, mutaṉ mutalāka am māḷikaiyil neruppu mūṭṭiyapōtu, atu paṟṟi erintu pāḻākiyatu. ataṉuṭaiya aṭippaṭaic (asti vāram) cuvarai iṉṟum kāṇalām. ki.pi. 1848-49-il maturai māvaṭṭat tuṇait taṇṭalarāka irunta tiruvāḷar tāmas kiḷār (ivar 1853-ām āṇṭu mēt tiṅkaḷil, paḻaṉi malaiyaip paṟṟi veḷiyiṭṭa nūl kuṟippiṭat takkatu) eṉpārum, māvaṭṭa naṭuvarāka irunta tiruvāḷar ci. ār. pēṉs eṉpārum, taṇṭalarāka irunta tiruvāḷar ār. ṭi. pārkkar eṉpārum, kōṭaikkāṉaliṉ ucciyil, camaveḷikaḷaip pārttāṟ pōṉṟu kōṭaik kālak kuḷirmaṉaikaḷai eḻuppiṉar. ippōtu uḷḷa ‘pāmpāṟu maṉai' (Pambar House) kkum, 'rōcaṉīt' (Roseneath) tiṟkum iṭaippaṭṭa iṭattilēyē avaikaḷ kaṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. aluvalakak kuṟippukaḷil kāṇappaṭum maṉai amaippu (House plans) kaḷai vaittukkoṇṭu pārttāl, tiruvāḷar pārkkar amaitta maṉai, ippoḻutu pāmpāṟu maṉai amaintirukkum iṭattilēyē iruntirukka vēṇṭum eṉṟu karutappaṭukiṟatu. tiruvāḷar pēṉs kaṭṭiya maṉai pāmpāṟu maṉaikku aṭuttāṟ pōl, kiḻakkil amaintuḷḷatu. ippōtu atu rōmaṉ kattōlikka camayattārukku urimaiyuṭaiyatāka uḷḷatu. tiruvāḷar kiḷārk kaṭṭiya maṉai ippōtuḷḷa 'rōcaṉīt 'tiṉ oru pakutiyākum.

•rōcaṉīt' eṉṟa ik kaṭṭiṭam, maṟṟavaikaḷai viṭa varalāṟṟuc ciṟappup peṟṟatu eṉṟu kūṟalām. tirāviṭa moḻikaḷai ārāyntu, oppuyarvaṟṟa oppilakkaṇattai (Comparative grammar) eḻutiya kālṭuvel pātiriyār im maṉaiyil tāṉ vāḻntār. avar eḻutiya oppilakkaṇam tirāviṭa moḻiyārāyccit tuṟaikku vaḻi vakuttatōṭallāmal, tamiḻ moḻi āriyattiṉiṉṟum muṟṟilum vēṟupaṭṭa uyartaṉic cemmoḻi eṉṟa uṇmaiyaiyum ulakiṟku uṇarttiyatu. kālṭuvel pātiriyār tam iṟuti nāṭkaḷai im maṉaiyilēyē kaḻittu iyaṟkai eytiṉār. kēpṭaṉ ṭapiḷyū. ec. ārssi eṉṟa oru poṟiyiyal vallunar, pēṉs, kiḷārk ākiya iruvariṉ maṉaikaḷukkiṭaiyē oru kaṭṭiṭattai eḻuppiṉār. aṭuttāṟpōl amerikkaṉ miṣaṉaic cērnta pātirimār taṅkaḷukkeṉa oru maṉaiyai amaittaṉar. atu ippoḻutu 'kiḷavarak' eṉṟu aḻaikkappaṭukiṟatu. pampāyp paṭṭāḷattaic cērnta mējar jē. em. pārṭriṭj eṉpavar ki. pi. 1852ām āṇṭu iṅku vantu kūṭāramaṭittu ērikkarukil taṅkiṉār. kuḷiriṉ koṭumaiyaiyum, puyaliṉ vēkattaiyum tāḷa muṭiyāta avar oru ciṟu maṉaiyai amaittut taṅkiṉār. avviṭam ippōtu 'pampāy ṣōlā' (Bombay Shola) eṉṟu aḻaikkappaṭukiṟatu. avarukku urimaiyāṉa tōṭṭam oṉṟum aṅku iruntatu. mutaṉ mutalākap paḻaṉi malaiyil nīlap piciṉ maram eṉṟa āstirēliya mara vakaiyai naṭṭup payiriṭṭavar ivarē. ivar vīṭṭiṉ muṉṉāl irunta iraṇṭu nīlap piciṉ maraṅkaḷil oṉṟu iṉṟum uḷḷatu. ap pakutiyil mikavum periya maram atutāṉ. kōṭaikkāṉal vācikaḷukkup payaṉpaṭum vakaiyil mutaṉ mutalāka icaik kalaiñarkaḷaik kuṭiyēṟṟa muyaṉṟavarum ivarē. ivar vīṭṭiṟkarukil oru kaṭaiyum iruntatākak kuṟippukaḷiliruntu aṟiya muṭikiṟatu.

mēṟkūṟiya eḻuvarumē kōṭaikkāṉaliṉ muṉṉōṭikaḷ (Pioneers) eṉṟu kūṟalām. ki. pi. 1853-il ivarkaḷukkurimaiyāṉa ēḻumaṉaikaḷē kōṭaikkāṉaliliruntaṉa. ki. pi. 1861-ām āṇṭu, mēlum mūṉṟu vīṭukaḷ kaṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. ki. pi. 1854. ām āṇṭu uccikkuc cellum kutiraip pātaiyaic ceppaṉiṭa rū 4,500 celaviṭappaṭṭatu. kōṭaik kāṉalil vāḻnta kiruttavap pātirikaḷ oru kal nīḷamuḷḷa pātaiyoṉṟai nakaril amaittaṉar. mēlum 6 kal nīḷamuḷḷa pātai aṅku vāḻvōrāl amaikkappaṭṭatu. 1860 ām āṇṭu ceṉṉai mānila āḷunarāka irunta car cārlas ṭirivelyāṉ eṉpavar tōppiliruntu puṟappaṭṭuk kutiraip pātaiyiṉ vaḻiyākap paḻaṉimalaiyucciyai aṭaintu, kōṭaikkāṉalaip pārvai yiṭṭār. (ivar varukaiyiliruntē innakar, araciyaṟ kuṟippēṭukaḷil kōṭaikkāṉal eṉṟu kuṟippiṭappaṭukiṟatu) paḻaṉi malaiyaippaṟṟi avar koṇṭa karuttukkaḷai aḻakiya oru kuṟippu nūlāka eḻutiyuḷḷār. tiruvāḷar kiḷārkkiṟku urimaiyāka irunta rōcaṉīt, maṉaiyilēyē ivar taṅki yiruntār. ki. pi. 1871-ām āṇṭu tiruvāḷar nēppiyar pirapu iṅku vantiruntār. ippoḻutu kōṭaik kāṉalil 'nēppiyar villā' eṉṟa oru maṉai uḷḷatu. nēppiyar pirapu avviṭattil ciṟitu nēram niṉṟu ceṉṟatāl, ammaṉai appeyar peṟṟatākak kūṟukiṟārkaḷ.

ki. pi. 1860-ām āṇṭu tiruvāḷar verheṉṟiliveñ maturai māvaṭṭat taṇṭalarāka amarttappaṭṭār. 1867 varai appaṇiyil iruntu viṭṭu, ōyvu peṟṟavarākak kōṭaikkāṉalil kuṭippukuntār. ki. pi. 1885-ām āṇṭu ceṉṉaiyil uyir nīttār. iṟappataṟkuc cilavāraṅkaḷ muṉ varaiyil kōṭaikkāṉaliluḷḷa 'pāmpāṟu maṉai' yilēyē vāḻntu vantār. kōṭaikkāṉalil avar vāḻat toṭaṅkiyatum, āṅkila araciyalārāl pirapu nilai (Baronetcy) kku uyarttappaṭṭār. avar māvaṭṭat taṇṭalarāka irunta pōtum, ōyvupeṟṟu iṅkut taṅkiya pōtum, kōṭaikkāṉaliṉ muṉṉēṟṟattiṟkup peritum uḻaittār. kōkkar naṭai veḷikkuc caṟṟumēl avaruṭaiya camāti amaintuḷḷatu. atil nāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷa ciluvaiyil 'kōṭaikkāṉaliṉ muṉṉēṟṟattiṉ perum pakuti ivaraiyē cārum' eṉṟu eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷatu. mutalilēyē kuṟippiṭṭatu pōl, kōṭaikkāṉal ēri ivaratu muḻu muyaṟciyālum, kaipporuḷālum amaikkappaṭṭatu. periya kuḷattiliruntu varum kutiraippātaiyai, pīṭapūmiyiṉ teṟku ellaiyiluḷḷa pāmpaṭi ṣōlā (Pambadi Shola) varai amaittār. nakariṉuḷḷum pala pātaikaḷai amaittār. airōppiyap paḻavakaikaḷaiyum pūvakaikaḷaiyum paḻaṉimalaiyiṉ mītu payiriṭuvataṟkup perumuyaṟci eṭuttukkoṇṭavarum ivarē.

ki. pi. 1853-ām āṇṭu amerikkaṉ miṣaṉaic cērntavarkaḷ 'caṉṉicaiṭ' (Sunny Side) eṉṟa iṭattiṟkarukil taṅkaḷukkurimaiyāṉa nilattil oru kōvil kaṭṭat toṭaṅkiṉar. atu 1856-ām āṇṭu muṭivuṟṟatu. āṅkilikaṉ miṣaṉaic cārntōrum akkōvililēyē vaḻipāṭu nikaḻttalām eṉṟu muṭivu ceyyappaṭṭatu. akkōvilaic cuṟṟi ōr iṭukāṭu amaikkappaṭṭatu. (ippōtu atu mūṭappaṭṭu viṭṭatu) mutaṉ mutalāka avviṭukāṭṭil amaikkappaṭṭa camāti iru kuḻantaikaḷiṉuṭaiyatu. akkuḻantaikaḷ ki. pi. 1819-ām āṇṭu iṟantaṉa; nakarukkuc ciṟitu tolaiviluḷḷa nēpōmalai {Mount Nebo) yil putaikkappaṭṭaṉa. avaikaḷ mīṇṭum tōṇṭi eṭukkappaṭṭu, iṅkuk koṇṭu vantu putaikkappaṭṭaṉa. ki. pi. 1896-ām āṇṭu ik kōvil kōṭaikkāṉal kaḻaka (club) ttiṟku aṇmaiyil putitākak kaṭṭappaṭṭa iṭattiṟku māṟṟappaṭṭatu. piṟaku paḻaiya kōvil aḻivuṟṟatu. ki. pi. 1863-ām āṇṭu ceyiṇṭ cair, kōṭaikkāṉalukku vantār. kattōlikkaril mutaṉ mutalāka vanta pātiriyār ivarē. ivar tiruvāḷar pēṉsiṉ maṉaiyai vilaikku vāṅkiṉār ; ippōtu kōṭaikkāṉalilirukkum rōmaṉ kattōlikkak kōvilukkuk kālkōḷiṭṭār. āṅkilikaṉ tiruccapaik kōvilai amaippataṟkāka nēpō malaiyil kālṭuvel pātiriyāriṉ peyarukkuk ki. pi. 1883ām āṇṭu araciyalārāl nilam vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatu. atē āṇṭil kōṭaikkāṉal vaṭṭat talaiva (Tahsildar) riṉ aluvalakam amaippataṟku araciyalāriṭamiruntu aṉumatiyum kiṭaittatu. ceṇpakaṉūriliruntu cellum malaivaḻip pātaik karukil airōppiyarukkuriya iṭukāṭu oṉṟu amaintuḷḷatu. kōṭaikkāṉalukkuḷ nuḻaintatum itu nam kaṇkaḷil paṭum. itu ki. pi. 1900-ām āṇṭu kaṭṭi muṭikkappaṭṭatu. ovvoru camayattārkkum taṉit taṉip pirivu itil uṇṭu.

kōṭaikkāṉal, muṉṉāḷil vilpaṭṭiyiṉ oru pakutiyāka amaintiruntatu. ki. pi. 1899-ām āṇṭu akṭōpar tiṅkaḷil iṅku nakarāṭci maṉṟam (Municipality) niṟuvappaṭṭatu. ceṉṉai mānilattilēyē mikavum ciṟiya nakarāṭci maṉṟam itutāṉ. mikak kuṟainta eṇṇikkaiyuḷḷa makkaḷē iṅku vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. mutaṉ mutalāka innakarāṭci maṉṟattil 12 uṟuppiṉarkaḷ iruntaṉar. ivarkaḷ yārum tērnteṭukkap paṭṭavarkaḷallar. muṉpellām, innakaril vāḻvōrukkut tēvaiyāṉa kuṭinīr, kiṇaṟukaḷiliruntum, aruvikaḷiliruntum peṟappaṭṭatu. ki. pi. 1902-ām āṇṭu kuṭinīr vacatikkāṉa tiṭṭam oṉṟu vakukkappaṭṭatu. attiṭṭattiṉpaṭi pāmpāṟṟil, mōkiṉi vīḻccikkumēl 370 keja tūrattil ōr aṇaikaṭṭi nīrait tēkki, kuḻāykaḷiṉ mūlam an nīrai nakariṉ ellāp pakutikaḷukkum vaḻaṅkuvateṉṟu muṭivu ceyyappaṭṭatu. ittiṭṭattiṟkāka rū 43,000 celaviṭuvateṉṟum muṭivu ceyyappaṭṭatu. piṟaku, in nīrttēkkam cuṇṇāmpuk kalavaiyāl uṟutiyākak kaṭṭappaṭa vēṇṭu meṉṟu muṭivu ceyyappaṭṭatāl ittiṭṭattiṉ celavu rū 62,250 kku uyarttap paṭṭatu. nakarāṭci maṉṟam pūrāc celavaiyum ēṟṟuk koḷḷa muṭiyāta nilaiyil iruntatāl, araciyalāriṉ utaviyai nāṭavēṇṭiya tāyiṟṟu.

ḵpiṣar cīmāṭṭiyiṉ irukkai :

tiruvāḷar ḵpiṣar eṉṟa oru veḷḷaiyar maturaiyil aluvalil iruntār. ceṇpakaṉūril avarukkuc contamāṉa māḷikai oṉṟiruntatu. atil tam maṉaiviyai viṭṭuviṭṭu maturaikkuc ceṉṟu viṭuvār. ōyvu nāṭkaḷil ceṇpakaṉūr tirumpuvār. paḻaṉi malaiyiṉmītu varum kutiraip pātaiyiṉ ucciyil amaintuḷḷa vaḷaivil, aḻakiya ciṟiya oru kōṭaimaṉai amaittār. taṉimai varuttum pōtu, ḵpiṣar cīmāṭṭi avvaḷaivil amarntukoṇṭu maturai nakar amaintuḷḷa tikkaip pārtta vaṇṇam iruppārām, kuṟippiṭṭa nāṭkaḷil tam kaṇavar kutirai ūrntu malaivaḻip pātaiyil ēṟivarum kāṭciyai aṅkiruntu kaṇṭu kaḷippatuṇṭām. ataṉāl avviṭam iṉṟum ḵpiṣar cīmāṭṭiyiṉ irukkai (Lady Fischer's seat) eṉṟu ellōrālum aḻaikkap paṭukiṟatu. 'vaṉa pōcaṉam' uṇpataṟku itu mikavum ēṟṟa iṭamāka ellōrālum ippoḻutu karutappaṭukiṟatu.

vāṉāyvukkūṭam :

kōṭaikkāṉalil amaintuḷḷa vāṉāyvuk kūṭam ceṉṉai mānilattilēyē mikavum kuṟippiṭat takka oṉṟākum. itu kōṭaikkāṉalukku iraṇṭu kal tolaivil, pūmpāṟaikkuc cellum pātaikku aṇmaiyil amaintuḷḷa oru cikarattiṉmēl niṟuvappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ki. pi. 1899-ām āṇṭu intiya nāṭṭiṉ vāṉa ārāyccik kūṭaṅkaḷ tiruttiyamaikkappaṭṭapoḻutu, ceṉṉaiyilirunta vāṉāyvuk kūṭam iṅku māṟṟappaṭṭatu. utakamaṇṭalattilum, kōtakiriyilum vāṉa ārāyccik kūṭaṅkaḷ niṟuva muyaṟcikaḷ eṭuttukkoḷḷappaṭṭaṉa. āṉāl avviṭaṅkaḷaiviṭak kōṭaikkāṉalē vāṉa ārāyccikku ēṟṟa iṭam eṉṟu muṭivu ceyyappaṭṭatu. ivviṭattiṉ poruttamāṉa taṭpa veppa nilaiyum, mappu mantāramaṟṟa teḷinta vāṉamum ivvārāyccikkup peritum tuṇaipurikiṉṟaṉa.

19-ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutiyil kōṭaikkāṉalil oru vīḻkoḷḷi (Meteorite) tiruvāḷar lōkaṉ eṉpāriṉ tōṭṭakkāraṉāl tōṇṭi eṭukkappaṭṭatu. nila ārāyccit tuṟaiyiṉar, intiyāvil kāṇappaṭum iraṇṭāvatu irumpu vīḻ koḷḷi ituvē eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. itaṉ niṟai 35 rāttal. itu ippōtu kalkattā poruṭkāṭci cālaiyil vaikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. iv vīḻkoḷḷi vāṉa ārāycciyāḷariṉ kavaṉattaip peritum kavarntatāl, 105 āṇṭukaḷākac ceṉṉaiyil paṇipurintu koṇṭirunta ceṉṉai vāṉāyvuk kūṭam kōṭaikkāṉalukku māṟṟappaṭṭatu. mutaṉ mutalil kiḷeṉ vīḻccikkarukil taṟkālikamāka ikkūṭam niṟuvappaṭṭatu. mikavum uyarntuḷḷa, kōṭaikkāṉalukku arukil amaintirukkum taṟpōtaiya iṭattiṟkup piṟaku itu māṟṟappaṭṭatu.

taṟpōtuḷḷa vāṉāyvukkūṭam tiruvāḷar ār. eḵp, sṭōṉi eṉṟa oru ciṟanta poṟiyiyal vallunarāl kaṭṭi muṭikkappaṭṭatu. ceṉṉaimānila āḷunarāka irunta tiruvāḷar veṉlāk eṉpavar tām ki. pi. 1895-ām āṇṭu ikkaṭṭiṭattiṟkuk kal nāṭṭiṉār. kutiraip pātaiyiṉ vaḻiyākavē kutiraiyūrntu avarum kōṭaikkāṉalukku vantār. itai nalla vāyppākak koṇṭa kōṭaikkāṉal makkaḷ ikkuṟiñci nakarai iruppuppātai mūlamākavō, vaṇṭippātai mūlamākavō iṇaikka vēṇṭumeṉṟu vaṟpuṟuttik kēṭṭuk koṇṭār. veṉlāk avarkaḷ iṅku vantapōtu, maḻaikkāla iravil iraṭṭai vaṇṭiyil payaṇam ceytum, kutiraippātaiyil naṭantum, puraviyūrntum mikavum tollaikaḷukkuḷḷākik kōṭaik kāṉalaiyaṭaintār. kōṭaikkāṉalukku oru nalla pātai avaciyam eṉpatai avarum uṇarntirukka vēṇṭum.

car ārtar hāvlāk eṉpavar 1899-ām āṇṭu vāṉāyvukkūṭattaip pārvaiyiṭṭār. ceṉṉaik kiruttavak kallūriyil pērāciriyarāka iruntavarum, ceṉṉai araciyalāriṉ vāṉa ārāyccittuṟait talaivarāka iruntavarumāṉa tiruvāḷar micci smit eṉpavar kōṭaikkāṉal vāṉāyvuk kūṭattiṉ poṟuppai ēṟṟukkoṇṭu ciṟitukālam paṇi ceytār. avar aluvaliliruntu ōyvu peṟṟatum, tiruvāḷar jē. evarceṭ eṉpavar poṟuppēṟṟār. evarceṭ avarkaḷiṉ maṉaiviyārum vāṉa ārāyccik kalaiyil nalla pulamai peṟṟavar, avvammaiyār 'teṉmaṇṭala viṇmīṉkaḷiṉ vaḻi kāṭṭi' (Guide to the Southern Stars) eṉṟa oru nūlai eḻutip patippittār.

ki. pi. 1910-ām āṇṭu tiruvāḷar kēṉāṉ mīcci eṉpārum avar maṉaiviyārum, tirunelvēlik kuṭiyēṟṟattiliruntu taṅkaḷ illattiṟkut tirumpik koṇṭiruntapōtu, vāṉattil oru periya vālmīṉaik (Comet) kaṇṭārkaḷ. āṅkilak kaḻakat (English Club) tiṉarukē niṉṟu ataip paṟṟi, vāṉa ārāycci vallunarāṉa micci smittaik kēṭṭārkaḷ. ataik kaṇṭa micci smit, atu oru hāli vālmīṉ (Halley's Comet) eṉṟu kūṟi viṭṭu, 'jerucalam' eṉṟa tam kutiraimēl tāvi ēṟi, vāṉa āyvukkūṭattiṟku ōṭi, ataippaṟṟi ārāyntār. atupōṉṟa vālmīṉ pala nūṟṟāṇṭu kaṭku orumuṟaitāṉ teṉpaṭumām. ki. pi. 1910-ām āṇṭu, kōṭaikkāṉalil vāḻnta oruvar tam naṇparukku eḻutiya kaṭitattil, 'nāṉ kōkkar naṭai veḷiyil atikālai 4-40 maṇikkuc ceṉṟu koṇṭiruntapoḻutu oru vālmīṉai vāṉattil kaṇṭēṉ. ataṉ pēraḻaku eṉṉai viyappil āḻttiyatu. atu pōṉṟa vālmīṉai nāṉ eṉṟum kaṇṭatillai. atu aṭivāṉattiliruntu naṭuvāṉam varai nīṇṭu pēroḷiyōṭu viḷaṅkiyatu' eṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭār.

tiruvāḷar evar ceṭ oyva peṟṟakam. ṭākṭar ṭi. rāyṭs eṉpavar poṟuppēṟṟār. 1936-ām āṇṭu jūlai 19-ām nāḷ ēṟpaṭṭa 'pūraṇa cūriya kirakaṇattai ' (total eclipse of the sun)p paṟṟi ārāyvataṟkāka avarum jappāṉukku aṉuppappaṭṭār.

kōṭaikkāṉaliluḷḷa ivvāṉāyvuk kūṭam, 'intiya araciyalāriṉ vāṉakkalai āyvukkūṭam' (The Government of India Solar Physics Observatory) eṉṟa peyar koṇṭu viḷaṅkukiṟatu. ik kūṭam cūriyaṉaippaṟṟi ārāyntum, cuṇṇa oḷi (Calcium light) yaiyum, nīrvāyu oḷi (Hydrogen light) yaiyum paṭam piṭittum, pala uṇmaikaḷai veḷiyiṭṭu vāṉakkalai vaḷarccikkup peritum pāṭupaṭṭu varukiṟatu. mēlum puvikkavarcci (Terrestrial magnetism) yaip paṟṟiyum, aṉilōṟpaṉṉak kalai (Meteorology) yaip paṟṟiyum, pūkampaviyal (Seismology) paṟṟiyum itu ārāykiṟatu. amerikka aikkiya nāṭṭil vilcaṉmalai (Mt. Wilson) mītu amaintuḷḷa vāṉāyvuk kūṭamum ḵprāṉs nāṭṭil miyūṭaṉ (Meudon) eṉṟa iṭattil amaintuḷḷa vāṉāyvukkūṭamum āṟṟum attaṉai ārāyccikaḷaiyum itu iṅku āṟṟukiṟatu. mikavum putumaiyāṉa vāṉa ārāyccik karuvikaḷum, aṉilōṟpaṉṉak kalaiyaip paṟṟi ārāyum karuvikaḷum iṅkup poruttappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ki. pi. 1937-ām āṇṭu ṭākṭar rāyṭ aluvaliliruntu ōyvu peṟṟatum ṭākṭar ē. el. nārāyaṇaṉ ivvāṉāyvuk kūṭattiṉ poṟuppai ēṟṟār.

mutalilēyē vāṉa āyvukkūṭap poṟuppāḷariṭam aṉumati peṟṟu, aṅku amaikkappaṭṭuḷḷa periya tolainōkkāṭi (Telescope)yiṉ mūlam vāṉa maṇṭalattil tiriyum kōḷkaḷaiyum viṇmīṉkaḷaiyum kāṇalām. vāṉa maṇṭalattiṉ taṉmaiyai aṟintu iṉpaṭaivataṟkāka ovvoru veḷḷikkiḻamai kālaiyilum potumakkaḷ ivvāṉāyvuk kūṭattiṟkuḷ aṉumatikkappaṭukiṟārkaḷ. intiya nāṭṭuk kāla aḷavaip (Indian Standard Time) potumakkaḷukku uṇarttuvataṟkāka nāḷtōṟum kālai pattu maṇikku oru koṭi ivvāṉāyvuk kūṭattil uyarttappaṭukiṟatu. vāṉa ārāycciyiṉ mūlamākavum, ulakattiṉ maṟṟa pakutikaḷil amaintuḷḷa vāṉāyvuk kūṭaṅkaḷiliruntu veḷiyākum rēṭiyō kālak kuṟippukaḷiṉ mūlamākavum, iṅku amaintuḷḷa maṇippoṟi cariyāṉa kāla aḷavaik kāṭṭi niṟkiṟatu. paiṉ maraṅkaḷum, nīlappiciṉ maraṅkaḷum kōṭaikkāṉalaic cuṟṟiyuḷḷa kāṭukaḷil uyarntu vaḷarntiruppatāl iṅku ēṟṟappaṭum koṭi, ellā iṭaṅkaḷukkum teṉpaṭuvatillai.



kalvik kūṭaṅkaḷ

puṉita itayak kallūri :

puṉita itayak kallūri (Sacred Heart Collage) ki. pi. 1895-ām āṇṭu tuvakkappaṭṭatu. itil ēṟakkuṟaiya 2000 māṇavarkaḷ payiṉṟu veḷiyēṟiyirukkiṉṟaṉar. intiya māṇavarkaḷallāmal, iṅkilāntu, āstirēliyā, kolampiyā, parmā mutaliya nāṭukaḷiliruntum māṇavarkaḷ iṅku vantu kalvi payilkiṉṟaṉar. itu kōṭaikkāṉalukkuc cellum pātaiyil, ceṇpakaṉūril amaintuḷḷatu. itu rōmaṉ kattōlikkarukkariya camayak kallūriyākum. ippaḷḷi tuvakkappaṭṭa kālattil pātikkumēl ḵpreñcu nāṭṭu iḷaiñarkaḷ kalvi payiṉṟaṉar. ulakap pōrkaḷiṉāl veḷināṭṭiliruntu varum māṇavarkaḷiṉ tokai kuṟaintuviṭṭatu. ik kallūri kaṭṭappaṭṭirukkum iṭam ki. pi. 1878-ām āṇṭiliruntu palataṭavai ciṟuciṟu pakutikaḷāka vāṅkappaṭṭatu. oru vivacāyap paḷḷiyaiyum, toḻiṟpaḷḷiyaiyum niṟuvum nōkkattōṭu ivviṭam vāṅkappaṭṭatu. ciṉkōṉā maraṅkaḷum vēṟu cila payirkaḷum iṅku mutalil payiriṭappaṭṭaṉa. āṉāl avai nalla muṟaiyil viḷaiyāta kāraṇattāl vivacāya, toḻil kallūrikaḷ naṭattum eṇṇam kaiviṭappaṭṭatu. piṟaku ippoḻutu uḷḷa camayak kallūri tuvakkappaṭṭatu.

kōṭaikkāṉalil vāḻum makkaḷukku ik kallūri mikavum aṟimukamāṉa oṉṟu. ceṇpakaṉūriluḷḷa yūkilipṭas kāṭṭiṉ vaḻiyāka varumpōtu, kaṇkavarum aḻakiya tōṭṭattiṉ naṭuvē kalaiyaḻakumikka ikkallūrik kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ nimirntu niṟpataik kaṇṭu makiḻalām. kōṭaikkāṉalukku varum untu vaṇṭi, ceṇpakaṉūr añcal nilaiyattiṟku arukil niṉṟāl, arukiluḷḷa ikkallūriyiṉ vāyil teṉpaṭum. kōkkar naṭaiveḷiyil ceṉṟōmāṉāl ikkallūriyiṉ maṇiyoli nam kātil mōtum. kaḻuttiliruntu kālvarai nīṇṭa āṭai uṭuttuk koṇṭu, iṭuppil cenniṟappaṭṭai aṇintu koṇṭu, kaiyil puttakamō, mātirippeṭṭiyō (Specimen-case) tāṅkiya vaṇṇam pātiri naṭai veḷi (Priest's walk) yilum, kōṭaikkāṉaliṉ iyaṟkaiyaḻaku coriyum ellā iṭaṅkaḷilum im māṇavarkaḷiṉ kūṭṭattaik kāṇalām. ak kallūrikkuḷ pārvaiyāḷarkaḷ aṉumatikkap paṭuvatillai. avarkaḷ kalvimuṟai, vēlait tiṭṭam ākiyavai paṟṟi yārukkum teriyātu.

iṭaikkalaiyō (Intermediate), ataiviṭa uyarnta kalviyō kaṟṟa māṇavarkaḷē iṅkuc cērttuk koḷḷappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. iṅku eṭṭāṇṭukkalvi ellārukkum payiṟṟappaṭukiṉṟatu. mutal iraṇṭāṇṭu, camayavāḻvil āḻnta paṟṟuk koḷḷum vakaiyil māṇavarkaḷ payiṟṟappaṭukiṟārkaḷ. piṟaku mūṉṟāṇṭukaḷ ilattīṉ, kirēkkam ākiya moḻikaḷil aṟivu peṟukiṟārkaḷ. pala tāymoḻikaḷaip pēcum māṇavarkaḷ iṅkuk kūṭuvatāl potuvāka āṅkilamum, ilattīṉum pēccu moḻiyākavum kaṟpikkum moḻiyākavum payaṉpaṭukiṉṟaṉa. kaṭaici mūṉṟāṇṭukaḷum tattuva ārāycciyilē kaḻikiṉṟaṉa, kirēkka rōma nāṭṭup periyārkaḷ aruḷic ceyta paḻamaiyāṉa tattuvaṅkaḷum, intiya nāṭṭut tattuvaṅkaḷum, mēlai nāṭṭut tattuvaṅkaḷum teḷivākak kaṟpikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. kallūriyil varalāṟu paṭittavarkaṭku iṅku aṟiviyalum (Science), aṟiviyal paṭittavarkaṭku varalāṟum kaṟpikkap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivvāṟu eṭṭāṇṭuk kalvi muṟṟup peṟṟatum, ammāṇavarkaḷ pūṉāviṟkō, kurciyāṅkiṟkō cella vēṇṭum. aṅku, nāṉkāṇṭuc camayak kalviyum, pātirit toḻilukkēṟṟa ōrāṇṭup payiṟciyum avarkaḷukku aḷikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa, añcal nilaiyattiṟkarukil ikkallūriyiṉāl naṭattappaṭum poruṭkāṭcicālai oṉṟuḷḷatu. tōṇṭi yeṭukkappaṭṭa putaiporuḷkaḷum paḻaṉi malaiyiluḷḷa tāvara vakaikaḷum, vilaṅkukaḷum, palavakaiyāṉa pāmpukaḷum aṅku vaikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. maṇṇil mūḻkiya putaiporuḷkaḷait tēṭiyeṭuttu ārāyvatil, ikkallūrip pērāciriyarkaḷ palar pērūkkum kāṭṭiṉar; kāṭṭivarukiṉṟaṉar.

haikiḷerk paḷḷi :

pala āṇṭukaḷ tiṭṭamiṭappaṭṭuk kōṭaikkāṉalil 'haikiḷerk paḷḷi' ki. pi. 1901-ām āṇṭu tuvakkappaṭṭatu. ippaḷḷi tuvakkappaṭuvataṟku muṉ 'haikiḷerk paḷḷi' (Highclerc boarding School) eṉṟa oru niṟuvaṉam kōṭaikkāṉalil iruntatu. kōṭaimātaṅkaḷil iṅkut taṅka varum amerikka, āṅkilēya makkaḷiṉ kuḻantaikaḷ ippaḷḷiviṭutiyil taṅkip paṭittaṉar. appoḻutu inniṟuvaṉattaip 'pātirimāriṉ kuḻantaikaḷ payilum kōṭaik kāṉal paḷḷi' (The Kodaikanal School for Missio naries children) eṉṟum, 'kōṭaippaḷḷi' eṉṟum, 'haikiḷerk, eṉṟum aḻaittaṉar. mutaṉ mutalākap patiṉmūṉṟu māṇavarkaḷē ippaḷḷiyil cērntaṉar. tirumati em. el. eṭṭi eṉṟa amerikka mātu, taṉ makaṉāṉa ṣervuṭ eṭṭiyaik kāṇa intiyā vantār. kōṭaikkāṉal makkaḷ avarai ippaḷḷiyiṉ mutalvarākap paṇi ēṟkumāṟu vēṇṭiṉārkaḷ. anta ammaiyārum, poṟuppēṟṟār. tāymaiyuḷḷam koṇṭa avvammaiyār māṇavarkaḷiṉ tēvaiyai yaṟintu avarkaḷ taṅkip paṭipputaṟkāka, irāppaḷḷikaḷaip pōṉṟu iraṇṭu ciṟiya kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷaik kaṭṭiṉār. avai periya, 'paḷḷi yaṟai', 'ciṟiya paḷḷiyaṟai' eṉṟu aḻaikkappaṭṭaṉa. appaḷḷiyaic cuṟṟi rōjāppūt tōṭṭamum, paiṉ marakkāṭum pērikkāyt tōṭṭamum amaikkappaṭṭaṉa.

ki. pi. 1902-ām āṇṭu ippaḷḷi 'ceṇṭral havucukkum (Central House) rāk kāṭṭēju (Rock cottage)kkum māṟṟappaṭṭatu. appōtu tirumati eṭṭi amerikkā ceṉṟiruntārkaḷ. mīṇṭum intiyā tirumpiyapōtu, rū 10,000 tiraṭṭikkoṇṭu vantār. appaṇattaik koṇṭu cantaiveḷiyil amaintirunta haikiḷerk paḷḷiyai vilaikku vāṅkiṉār. ki.pi.1902 mutal 1904 varai ivviṭam kumāri ōrlpār eṉpavarāl kuṭikkūlikku eṭuttuk koḷḷappaṭṭatu. avar koṭaittaṉmai mikkavar; intiyāvil pala iṭaṅkaḷil taṅkum uṇavuviṭutikaḷaiyum (Boarding houses), kiruttavap pātirikaḷukkāṉa taṅkal maṉaikaḷaiyum niṟuviyavar. mīṇṭum ip paḷḷi haikiḷerkkiṟku māṟṟappaṭṭatu. ulakappōrkaḷ naṭanta camayattil pala nāṭukaḷaic cērnta āciriyarkaḷ iṅkup paṇiyāṟṟiṉar. potuvāka amerikka āṅkila nāṭṭuk kalvimuṟaikaḷ iṅkuk kaṭaippiṭikkappaṭṭu vantaṉa. āṉāl, cila camayaṅkaḷil amerikka māṇavarkaḷ mikuntirunta kāraṇattāl amerikka nāṭṭuk kalvittiṭṭamē ippaḷḷiyilum mēṟkoḷḷappaṭṭatu. āṇ peṇ ākiya iru pālarum iṅkuk kalvi payilkiṉṟaṉar. kōṭaiyil taṅkuvataṟkākak kōṭaikkāṉal varum peṟṟōrkaḷiṉ, iḷañciṟuvar ciṟumiyarkaḷukkāṉa 'kiṇṭar kārṭaṉ paḷḷi' oṉṟum āṇṭutōṟum mūṉṟu tiṅkaḷkaḷukku iṅku naṭattappaṭukiṟatu. amerikkaṉ miṣaṉaic cārnta kiruttavariṉ kuḻantaikaḷ intiyāviṉ pala pakutikaḷiliruntum, ilaṅkai, arēpiyā, pāracīkam ciyām mutaliya aṇṭai nāṭukaḷiliruntum iṅku vantu kalvi payilkiṉṟaṉar. ōr iraṭṭaimāṭik kaṭṭiṭamum, vēṟupala kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷum, ippaḷḷikkākak kaṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. pārṭaṉ (Barton), ērli (Airlie) mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷum ippaḷḷikkāka vāṅkappaṭṭaṉa. peṇṭarlāc( Benderloch) eṉṟa iṭam paḷḷittalaivar taṅkuvataṟkāka vāṅkappaṭṭatu. villisṭaṉ (Williston), liṭṭil villi (Little Willie) eṉṟa iṭaṅkaḷ āciriyarkaḷiṉ kuṭiyiruppukaḷukkāka vāṅkappaṭṭaṉa. 'viṉsṭaṉ' (Winston) eṉṟa iṭam paḷḷikkuriya ilavaca maruttuvamaṉaiyākap payaṉpaṭukiṟatu. ki. pi. 1932-ām āṇṭu itu ōr uyar nilaippaḷḷiyāka māṟṟappaṭṭatu. 'iṅku naṭattappaṭum iṟutit tērvu meṭrikulēṣaṉukku oppāṉatu. itil kalvi kaṟṟu veḷiyēṟum māṇavarkaḷ amerikkā, kāṉaṭā nāṭṭuk kallūri vakuppukkaḷil cērttukkoḷḷap paṭukiṉṟaṉar. iṅkup payilum māṇavarkaḷ cīṉiyar kēmpiriṭj' tērvilum kalantukoṇṭu nalla veṟṟiyaṭaikiṉṟaṉar. ērikkarukil, ippaḷḷikkāka oru paranta viḷaiyāṭumiṭam amaikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ṭākṭar aleks, tirumati aleks, es. vilcaṉ, kumāri privāsṭ, tiruvāḷar kārl, tirumati kārl, ṭapiḷyū ḵpels ākiyōr, nīṇṭa nāḷ paḷḷippoṟuppāḷarkaḷākap paṇiyāṟṟiṉar. potumakkaḷiṭattil paṇam tiraṭṭi, tirumati mārkaraṭ eṭṭiyiṉ niṉaivuc ciṉṉamāka ippaḷḷiyil oru kōvilaik kaṭṭiyuḷḷaṉar.

piraceṇṭēṣaṉ kāṉveṉṭ :

kōṭaikkāṉalilē kuṟippiṭattakka maṟṟoru paḷḷi, 'piraceṇṭēcaṉ kāṉveṇṭ' eṉṟa peṇkaḷ uyar nilaippaḷḷiyākum. ceṉṉaip piraceṇṭēcaṉ tiruccapaiyaic cārnta cēviyar tāyār (Rev. Mother Xavier) avarkaḷum vēṟu cila kaṉṉiyarum 1914-ām āṇṭu kōṭaikkāṉalukku vantiruntaṉar. avvāṇṭu 'hilcaiṭ' (Hill Side) eṉṟa iṭattil taṅkiṉar. ki. pi. 1915-ām āṇṭu 'keṉmūr' (Kenmure) eṉṟa iṭattil taṅkiṉar. appoḻutu avarkaḷuṭaṉ cila kuḻantaikaḷum taṅkiyiruntaṉar. avvāṇṭu ellōrum ceṉṟa piṟaku, cēviyar tāyārum, ikṉēciyas tāyārum, ḵpōr vīri eṉṟa iṭattil taṅki, oru paḷḷi niṟuvuvataṟku ēṟṟa iṭattait tēṭiṉar. kiḷeṉ vīḻccikku arukil amaintirunta malaiyucciyil (muṉpu vāṉāyvukkūṭam irunta iṭam) paḷḷiyai niṟuvuvatu eṉṟu muṭivu ceytaṉar. appaḷḷiyai amaippataṟku vēṇṭiya poruḷātārattaip paṟṟit tiruvāḷar ī. ār. lōkaṉ eṉpāruṭaṉ ālōcaṉai naṭattiṉar.

ki. pi. 1916-ām āṇṭu ēpral tiṅkaḷil piraceṇṭēcaṉ tiruccapaiyaic cārnta kaṉṉiyarkaḷ, 'hil caiṭ' eṉṟa iṭattilēyē 17 māṇaviyarkaḷaik koṇṭu ippaḷḷiyait tuvakkiṉar. 1916-ām āṇṭu jūṉ tiṅkaḷ 16-ām nāḷ ippōtuḷḷa paḷḷik kaṭṭiṭattiṟkuk kal nāṭṭu viḻā naṭattappaṭṭatu. maraṅkaḷum naṭappaṭṭaṉa. kaṭṭiṭa vēlai muṭintatum ki. pi. 1917-ām āṇṭu ḵpepruvarit tiṅkaḷil, paḷḷi putiya kaṭṭiṭattiṟku māṟṟappaṭṭatu. paḷḷiyil oru kōvilum amaikkappaṭṭatu. akkōvil kālaiyil toḻukaikkuriya iṭamākavum, mālaiyil paḷḷiyākavum payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. mutalil mikavum eḷiya muṟaiyilēyē ip paḷḷi toṭaṅkiyatu. iṅkup paṇipurinta kaṉṉimārkaḷukkup pōtiya vacatiyum illai. cāmāṉkaḷ koṇarnta peṭṭikaḷē nāṟkālikaḷākap payaṉ paṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. māṭṭu vaṇṭiyil poruttappaṭṭa pīppāykaḷiṉ mūlam neṭuntolaiviliruntu nīr koṇṭuvarappaṭṭatu. appōtu pōkkuvaravu vacatikaḷum mikak kuṟaivu. ki. pi. 1916-ām āṇṭu naṭunilaippaḷḷiyākat tuvaṅkiya itu ki. pi. 1919-ām āṇṭu uyar nilaippaḷḷiyāka māṟiyatu. ki. pi. 1920- ām āṇṭu kōṭaikkāṉalukku vanta veliṅkṭaṉ cīmāṭṭi, ippaḷḷikkāṉa iraṭṭai māṭik kaṭṭiṭam oṉṟukkuk kālkōḷ iṭṭār. 1 laṭcam rūpāy poruṭ celavil ikkaṭṭiṭam kaṭṭappaṭṭatu. 'kiṇṭar kārṭaṉ' vakuppiliruntu, cīṉiyar kēmpiriṭj vakuppu varai, ikkaṭṭiṭattil naṭaipeṟṟaṉa.

ippaḷḷi nāḷaṭaivil nalla viḷamparamum muṉṉēṟṟamum peṟṟu oru ciṟanta airōppiyap paḷḷiyāka māṟiyuḷḷatu. pattu vayatu muṭiyumvarai, ciṟuvarkaḷum iṅku māṇavarkaḷākac cērttuk koḷḷappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. peṇkaḷ paḷḷiyiṟuti vakuppuvarai paṭippatōṭu, ṭiriṉiṭi kallūri icait tērvukkum payiṟci peṟukiṉṟaṉar. cēviyar tāyār ōyvu peṟṟatum, akasṭiṉ tāyār paḷḷit talaivarākap poṟuppēṟṟār. ki. pi. 1926-mutal 1944 muṭiyap paṇiyāṟṟiṉār. avarukkup piṟaku jōcaḵpaiṉ tāyār, paḷḷit talaivarākap paṇiyāṟṟi varukiṟār. kōṭai nāṭkaḷil ippaḷḷiyil naṭaipeṟum āṇṭu viḻā kuṟippiṭattakkatu. ceyiṇṭ jāṉ cōtariyālum, kumāri elsperk eṉpavarālum iṅku māṇavikaḷukkuc ciṟanta kalaip (Fine Arts) payiṟcikaḷ aḷikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. āṇṭu viḻāk kālaṅkaḷil māṇavikaḷ nikaḻttum āṭal pāṭal nikaḻccikaḷ pārāṭṭutalukkuriyavai,



kōheṉ niṉaivup paḷḷi :

ki. pi. 1912-ām āṇṭu, kōṭaikkāṉalil lāc eṇṭu' (Loch End) eṉṟa ōr iṭattai, amerikka lūtaraṉ camayattaic cārntavarkaḷ, tiruvāḷar ṭēṉiyal maknāyar eṉṟa poṟiyiyal vallunariṭamiruntu vilaikku vāṅkiṉārkaḷ. ki. pi. 1922-ām āṇṭu, taṅkaḷ kuḻantaikaḷ payiluvataṟ keṉṟu oru paḷḷiyai niṟuviṉārkaḷ. taṅkaḷ tantaiyar nāṭāṉa amerikka nāṭṭukkuk kalvi muṟaiyai appaḷḷiyil pukuttiṉar. kōṭaikkālattil iṅku vantu taṅkiyavarkaḷiṉ kuḻantaikaḷuk cāṉa paruvappaḷḷi (Season school) yākavē itu mutalil tuvakkappaṭṭatu. tiruvāḷar pācmēṉ eṉṟa āciriyar paḷḷikkākac ciṟiya oru kaṭṭiṭattaik kaṭṭiṉār. piṟaku māṇavarkaḷukkut taṅkumiṭamum, kōvilum amaittār. ivviraṇṭiṉ kaṭṭiṭak kalaiyaḻaku kōṭaikkāṉalil kuṟippiṭattakkatu, tiruvāḷar puccāṉaṉ putaiporuḷ ārāycciyil ārvamuḷḷavar. paḻaṉimalaikaḷil ki. pi. 1942-ām āṇṭu avar tōṇṭiyeṭutta putaiporuḷkaḷ ceṇpakaṉūriluḷḷa poruṭkāṭci cālaiyil vaikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ippaḷḷiyil 8 vakuppukkaḷ ippoḻutu uḷḷaṉa. tiruvāḷar miyūller eṉpārum tirumati hekkal ammaiyārum āciriyarkaḷāka iṅkup paṇiyāṟṟukiṉṟaṉar.

svīṭiṣ paḷḷi :

intiyāvil vāḻum svīṭiṣ kuḻantaikaḷ payilvataṟkeṉṟu kōṭaikkāṉalil oru paḷḷi uḷḷatu. svīṭiṣ kuḻantaikaḷ mutalil haikiḷerk paḷḷikkē aṉuppappaṭṭaṉar. āṉāl irupatāṇṭukku muṉ svīṭiṣ kuḻantaikaḷ taṉiyākap payilvataṟ keṉṟu ippaḷḷi tuvakkappaṭṭatu. ippaḷḷiyiṉ peyar 'cālvik' (Solvik) eṉpatākum. haikiḷerk paḷḷi irukkum atē malaiyiṉmītu tāṉ ituvum amaintuḷḷatu. iṅkup payilum māṇavarkaḷ taṅkaḷ tāy moḻiyilēyē kalvi peṟukiṉṟaṉar. haikiḷerk paḷḷiyil payilumpōtu pala nāṭṭu māṇavarkaḷōṭu cērntu paḻakum vāyppu iruntatu. ippoḻutu atu illai. intiya nāṭṭil uḷḷa svīṭiṣ paḷḷi itu oṉṟutāṉ. eṉavē, intiya nāṭṭiṉ ellāp pakutikaḷilum vāḻum svīṭiṣkārarkaḷ, taṅkaḷuṭaiya kuḻantaikaḷai 'cālvik'kiṟkē aṉuppukiṉṟaṉar. kaṭanta cila āṇṭukaṭku muṉpāka, svīṭaṉ nāṭṭup pārāḷu maṉṟamum itai aṅkīkarittatu. ippaḷḷi uruvattil ciṟiyatāka iruntālum īṭaṟṟa aḻakōṭu ēriyaip pārtta vaṇṇam amaintuḷḷatu.

tamiḻp paḷḷikaḷ :

kōṭaikkāṉalil vāḻum tamiḻ māṇavarkaḷ payiluvataṟkeṉṟu pala tamiḻp paḷḷikaḷ iṅku amaikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. yūṉiyaṉ carc kōṭaikkāṉalil niṟuvappaṭṭa pōtu pātiriyāriṉ illattiṟkarukil, amerikkaṉ miṣaṉaic cārntōr oru tuvakkap paḷḷiyai niṟuviṉar. kōṭaikkāṉalil makkaṭ perukkam ēṟpaṭṭatum, nakarāṭciyiṉar vāṉāyvuk kūṭap pātaiyilum, untu vaṇṭi nilaiyattiṟkarukilum tamiḻ māṇavarkaḷukkeṉṟu pala tuvakkap paḷḷikaḷai niṟuviṉar. ki. pi. 1942-ām āṇṭu 'tōpikāṉā'viṟku etiril oru naṭunilaip paḷḷi tuvakkappaṭṭatu. itu ippoḻutu uyar nilaip paḷḷiyāka vaḷarntuḷḷatu. rōmaṉ kattōlikkac camayattārāl mūṉṟu tamiḻp paḷḷikaḷ naṭattappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avai mēl ērip pātaiyilum, kāṉveṇṭ pātaiyilum amaintuḷḷaṉa.



ēḻai māṇavar paḷḷi :

ki. pi. 1919-ām āṇṭu intiya ēḻai māṇavarkaḷukkāka ōr ilavacap paḷḷi tuvakkappaṭṭatu. ippōtu atu 'ceyiṇṭ cēviyar intiya ēḻai māṇavar paḷḷi' eṉṟa peyar koṇṭu viḷaṅkukiṟatu. 80 māṇavarkaḷukku ilavaca uṇavum uṭaiyum iṅku aḷikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. rōmaṉ kattōlikka māṇavarkaḷaṭaṅkiya oru kuḻu ip paḷḷiyil, amaikkap paṭṭuḷḷatu. ik kuḻu , kattōlikka camayattaic cārnta ēḻaikaḷukkut toṇṭu puriyap payaṉpaṭuttappaṭukiṟatu.

aṉātaip paḷḷi :
